# <span class="deva-large">उ</span> <span class="iast-large">u</span>

<div class="dict-page-header">
  <span class="badge-count">873 entrées</span>
  <a href="/" class="back-link">← Index du dictionnaire</a>
</div>

---

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry--u">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">॰उ</span> <i>-u</i> forme des agt., not. de dés.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry--uka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">॰उक</span> <i>-uka</i> forme des participes progressifs; cf. <a href="#entry-bhaavuka" class="skt-link">bhāvuka</a>, <a href="#entry-var_suka" class="skt-link">varṣuka</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-u">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उ</span> <i>u</i> interj. cl. véd. et, aussi, de plus <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> véd. maintenant, de suite <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> véd. (suivi de <a href="#entry-su_1" class="skt-link">su_1</a>) tout de suite.<br> <i>u</i> <i>uttiṣṭha</i> lève toi donc!
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-u_d_diyaana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उड्डियान</span> <i>uḍḍiyāna</i> <a href="#entry-u_d_diyaana" class="skt-link">n.</a> cf. <a href="#entry-udyaana" class="skt-link">udyāna</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-u_du">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उडु</span> <i>uḍu</i> <a href="#entry-u_du" class="skt-link">f.</a> étoile — <a href="#entry-u_du" class="skt-link">n.</a> astérisme lunaire.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-u_duloma">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>uḍuloma</i> [<a href="#entry-loman" class="skt-link">loman</a>] <a href="#entry-u_duloma" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. du sage Uḍuloma «au Poil en étoile».<br> <a href="#entry-u_du" class="skt-link">uḍu</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-u_dumbara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उडुम्बर</span> <i>uḍumbara</i> <a href="#entry-u_dumbara" class="skt-link">m.</a> cf. <a href="#entry-udumbara" class="skt-link">udumbara</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-u_dupa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>uḍupa</i> [<a href="#entry-pa_1" class="skt-link">pa_1</a>] <a href="#entry-u_dupa" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-u_dupa" class="skt-link">n.</a> bateau, barque; radeau <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> la Lune.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-u_dupii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उडुपी</span> <i>uḍupī</i> <a href="#entry-u_dupii" class="skt-link">f.</a> géo. np. d'Uḍupī, ville de pélerinage de la côte du <a href="#entry-Ukar_naa_taka" class="skt-link">Karṇāṭaka</a> [<a href="#entry-Ukaraavalii" class="skt-link">Karāvalī</a>]; son temple <a href="#entry-Ucandramauliizvara_1" class="skt-link">Candramaulīśvara_1</a> est consacré à <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> «portant la Lune dans son diadème»; son temple <a href="#entry-Uanantezvara_1" class="skt-link">Ananteśvara_1</a> est syncrétiste-on dit que <a href="#entry-Uvi_s_nu" class="skt-link">Viṣṇu</a> sous sa forme primordiale réside dans le <a href="#entry-lifga" class="skt-link">liṅga</a>; <a href="#entry-Umadhva" class="skt-link">Madhva</a> y fonda le <a href="#entry-Uzriik_r_s_nama_tha" class="skt-link">Śrīkṛṣṇamaṭha</a>, qui devint un centre de débats philosophiques au 9⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-u_n">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उण्</span> <i>uṇ</i> nota. gram. affixe primaire [<a href="#entry-k_rt_2" class="skt-link">kṛt_2</a>].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-u_naadi">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>uṇādi</i> [<a href="#entry-aadi" class="skt-link">ādi</a>] <a href="#entry-u_naadi" class="skt-link">m.</a> pl. <i>uṇādayas</i> gram. classe d'affixes primaires [<a href="#entry-k_rt_2" class="skt-link">kṛt_2</a>] (commençant par <a href="#entry-u_n" class="skt-link">uṇ</a>); ces affixes spécifiques sont non compositionnels: ils forment des thèmes tout-faits non analysables sémantiquement; ils sont listés dans les traités <i>pañcapādī</i> et <i>daśapādī</i>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-u_naadisuutra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>uṇādisūtra</i> [<a href="#entry-suutra" class="skt-link">sūtra</a>] <a href="#entry-u_naadisuutra" class="skt-link">n.</a> pl. <i>uṇādisūtrāṇi</i> gram. formulaire des affixes primaires non compositionnels.<br> <a href="#entry-u_naadi" class="skt-link">uṇādi</a> <br> <a href="#entry-u_n" class="skt-link">uṇ</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-u_s">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">उष्</span> <i>uṣ</i> [relié à <a href="#entry-vas_4" class="skt-link">vas_4</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(oṣati)</i> aor. [<span class="skt-grammar">5</span>] <i>(auṣīt)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>uṣṭa</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>upa</i><i>)</i> brûler; briller <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> tourmenter, punir <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lat. <i>ustus</i>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-u_s_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उष्ण</span> <i>uṣṇa</i> [obj. <a href="#entry-u_s" class="skt-link">uṣ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-u_s_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-u_s_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-u_s_naa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>uṣṇā</i> chaud, brûlant <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> (aliment) échauffant — <a href="#entry-u_s_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-u_s_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> chaleur, ardeur <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> la canicule (juin-juillet).<br> <i>oṣṇa</i> légèrement chaud, tiède.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-u_s_naga">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>uṣṇaga</i> [<a href="#entry-ga_1" class="skt-link">ga_1</a>] <a href="#entry-u_s_naga" class="skt-link">m.</a> pl. <i>uṣṇagās</i> saison chaude.<br> <a href="#entry-u_s_na" class="skt-link">uṣṇa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-u_s_naka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उष्णक</span> <i>uṣṇaka</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-u_s" class="skt-link">uṣ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-u_s_naka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-u_s_naka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-u_s_nikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>uṣṇikā</i> laborieux, zélé; opp. <a href="#entry-ziitaka" class="skt-link">śītaka</a> — <a href="#entry-u_s_nikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>uṣṇikā</i> brouet.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-u_s_nih">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उष्णिह्</span> <i>uṣṇih</i> [<a href="#entry-ut" class="skt-link">ut</a>-<a href="#entry-snih_2" class="skt-link">snih_2</a>] <a href="#entry-u_s_nih" class="skt-link">f.</a> phon. mètre védique (de 28 syllabes) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. du cheval Uṣṇik, attelé au char de <a href="#entry-Usuurya" class="skt-link">Sūrya</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-u_s_nihaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उष्णिहा</span> <i>uṣṇihā</i> [<a href="#entry-u_s_nih" class="skt-link">uṣṇih</a>] <a href="#entry-u_s_nihaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> phon. mètre védique <a href="#entry-u_s_nih" class="skt-link">uṣṇih</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> nuque.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-u_s_nii_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उष्णीष</span> <i>uṣṇīṣa</i> [<a href="#entry-u_s_na" class="skt-link">uṣṇa</a>-<a href="#entry-ii_s" class="skt-link">īṣ</a>] <a href="#entry-u_s_nii_sa" class="skt-link">n.</a> turban [«qui protège de la chaleur»] <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> bd. protubérance crânienne canonique du <a href="#entry-Ubuddha" class="skt-link">Buddha</a>, surmontée de la flamme de la connaissance <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> arch. couronnement.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-u_s_ta_1">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उष्ट</span> <i>uṣṭa_1</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-u_s" class="skt-link">uṣ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-u_s_ta_1" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-u_s_ta_1" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-u_s_taa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>uṣṭā</i> brûlé.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-u_s_ta_2">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उष्ट</span> <i>uṣṭa_2</i> var. <i>uṣita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-vas_1" class="skt-link">vas_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-u_s_ta_2" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-u_s_ta_2" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-u_s_taa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>uṣṭā</i> passé (temps) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> qui a séjourné.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-u_s_tra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उष्ट्र</span> <i>uṣṭra</i> [instr. <a href="#entry-u_s" class="skt-link">uṣ</a>] <a href="#entry-u_s_tra" class="skt-link">m.</a> natu. zoo. chameau — <a href="#entry-u_s_trii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>uṣṭrī</i> natu. chamelle.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-u_s_tramukha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>uṣṭramukha</i> [<a href="#entry-mukha" class="skt-link">mukha</a>] a. <a href="#entry-u_s_tramukha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-u_s_tramukha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-u_s_tramukhii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>uṣṭramukhī</i> [«qui a une tête de chameau»] laid.<br> <a href="#entry-u_s_tra" class="skt-link">uṣṭra</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-u_sa_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उषण</span> <i>uṣaṇa</i> [act. <a href="#entry-u_s" class="skt-link">uṣ</a>] <a href="#entry-u_sa_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> poivre noir — <a href="#entry-u_sa_naa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>uṣaṇā</i> natu. bot. <i>Piper longum</i> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gingembre sec.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-u_sas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उषस्</span> <i>uṣas</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-u_s" class="skt-link">uṣ</a>] <a href="#entry-u_sas" class="skt-link">f.</a> l'aurore <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> véd. np. d'Uṣā «la Brillante», personnifiant l'Aurore; elle commença la Création au matin du monde par l'inceste primordial avec son père <a href="#entry-Uprajaapati" class="skt-link">Prajāpati</a>; on la dit commune épouse des <a href="#entry-Uazvinau" class="skt-link">Aśvinau</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-Um_rgaziras" class="skt-link">Mṛgaśiras</a>, <a href="#entry-Urohi_nii" class="skt-link">Rohiṇī</a>, <a href="#entry-Umariici_1" class="skt-link">Marīci_1</a> — <a href="#entry-u_saa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>uṣā</i> l'aurore <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] np. d'Uṣā_1 «Aurore», fille de l'<a href="#entry-asura" class="skt-link">asura</a> <a href="#entry-Ubaa_na" class="skt-link">Bāṇa</a>; ayant vu <a href="#entry-Uaniruddha" class="skt-link">Aniruddha</a> dans un rêve érotique, elle le fit venir par la magie de l'<a href="#entry-apsaras" class="skt-link">apsaras</a> <a href="#entry-Ucitralekhaa" class="skt-link">Citralekhā</a> dans son château, où <a href="#entry-Ubaa_na" class="skt-link">Bāṇa</a> le retint prisonnier; <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_na" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇa</a> et <a href="#entry-Upradyumna" class="skt-link">Pradyumna</a> vinrent tuer <a href="#entry-Ubaa_na" class="skt-link">Bāṇa</a> pour le libérer, et <a href="#entry-Uaniruddha" class="skt-link">Aniruddha</a> épousa <a href="#entry-Uu_saa_1" class="skt-link">Uṣā_1</a> — v. [<span class="skt-grammar">11</span>] pr. <i>(uṣasyati)</i> apparaître (aurore) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gr. εος; lat. <i>aurorā</i>; all. Ostera; ang. East; fr. aurore.<br> <i>uṣasi</i> adv. à l'aurore; le matin.<br> <i>uṣā</i> adv. à l'aube.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-u_sita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उषित</span> <i>uṣita</i> var. <i>uṣṭa_2</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-vas_1" class="skt-link">vas_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-u_sita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-u_sita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-u_sitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>uṣitā</i> passé (temps) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> qui a séjourné.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-u_sitvaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उषित्वा</span> <i>uṣitvā</i> [abs. <a href="#entry-vas_1" class="skt-link">vas_1</a>] ind. ayant séjourné.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-u_sman">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उष्मन्</span> <i>uṣman</i> [<a href="#entry-u_s" class="skt-link">uṣ</a><a href="#entry--man" class="skt-link">-man</a>] <a href="#entry-u_sman" class="skt-link">m.</a> chaleur, ardeur.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ubh">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उभ्</span> <i>ubh</i> cf. <a href="#entry-umbh" class="skt-link">umbh</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ubha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उभ</span> <i>ubha</i> [<a href="#entry-umbh" class="skt-link">umbh</a>] pn. <a href="#entry-ubha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ubha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ubhaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ubhā</i> du. les deux, tous deux <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gr. αμφω; lat. <i>ambo</i>; fr. ambigu.<br> <i>āvāmubhau</i> nous deux.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ubhaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उभय</span> <i>ubhaya</i> [<a href="#entry-ubha" class="skt-link">ubha</a><a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ubhaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ubhaya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ubhayii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ubhayī</i> l'un et l'autre, les deux; des deux sortes <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. les deux mondes; le ciel et la terre.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ubhayaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उभया</span> <i>ubhayā</i> [<a href="#entry-ubhaya" class="skt-link">ubhaya</a>] adv. de deux manières.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ubhayacakravartin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ubhayacakravartin</i> [<a href="#entry-cakravartin" class="skt-link">cakravartin</a>] <a href="#entry-ubhayacakravartin" class="skt-link">m.</a> souverain des deux mondes.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ubhayakaama">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ubhayakāma</i> [<a href="#entry-kaama" class="skt-link">kāma</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ubhayakaama" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ubhayakaama" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ubhayakaamaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ubhayakāmā</i> qui désire les deux.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ubhayapaarzvatas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ubhayapārśvatas</i> [<a href="#entry-paarzvatas" class="skt-link">pārśvatas</a>] adv. des deux côtés.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ubhayapada">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ubhayapada</i> [<a href="#entry-pada" class="skt-link">pada</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ubhayapada" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ubhayapada" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ubhayapadaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ubhayapadā</i> gram. (se dit d'un vb.) se conjuguant à la fois à la voix active [<a href="#entry-parasmaipada" class="skt-link">parasmaipada</a>] et à la voix réfléchie [<a href="#entry-aatmanepada" class="skt-link">ātmanepada</a>].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ubhayapadaartha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ubhayapadārtha</i> [<a href="#entry-padaartha" class="skt-link">padārtha</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ubhayapadaartha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ubhayapadaartha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ubhayapadaarthaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ubhayapadārthā</i> gram. se dit d'un composé dont le sens compose celui de ses deux membres (not. <a href="#entry-dvandva" class="skt-link">dvandva</a>).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ubhayapadin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ubhayapadin</i> [<a href="#entry--in" class="skt-link">-in</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ubhayapadin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ubhayapadin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ubhayapadinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ubhayapadinī</i> gram. vb. se conjuguant à la fois en <a href="#entry-parasmaipada" class="skt-link">parasmaipada</a> et en <a href="#entry-aatmanepada" class="skt-link">ātmanepada</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-ubhayapadaartha" class="skt-link">ubhayapadārtha</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ubhayata_hsasya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ubhayataḥsasya</i> [<a href="#entry-sasya" class="skt-link">sasya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ubhayata_hsasya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ubhayata_hsasya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ubhayata_hsasyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ubhayataḥsasyā</i> qui fait deux récoltes par an.<br> <a href="#entry-ubhayatas" class="skt-link">ubhayatas</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ubhayatas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ubhayatas</i> [<a href="#entry--tas" class="skt-link">-tas</a>] adv. des deux côtés, dans les deux cas, les deux à la fois; de part et d'autre de ⟨acc. g.⟩.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ubhayathaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ubhayathā</i> [<a href="#entry-yathaa" class="skt-link">yathā</a>] adv. des deux manières.<br> <a href="#entry-ubha" class="skt-link">ubha</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ubhayatra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ubhayatra</i> [<a href="#entry-yatra" class="skt-link">yatra</a>] adv. des deux côtés, aux deux endroits <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> dans les deux cas, les deux à la fois.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ubhayatva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ubhayatva</i> [<a href="#entry--tva" class="skt-link">-tva</a>] <a href="#entry-ubhayatva" class="skt-link">n.</a> état d'être double; se dit not. de la voyelle ‘u’ dans le <a href="#entry-pra_nava" class="skt-link">praṇava</a> <a href="#entry-o_m" class="skt-link">oṃ</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ubhayavya_njana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ubhayavyañjana</i> [<a href="#entry-vya_njana" class="skt-link">vyañjana</a>] <a href="#entry-ubhayavya_njana" class="skt-link">n.</a> hermaphrodite.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ubhayazle_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ubhayaśleṣa</i> [<a href="#entry-zle_sa" class="skt-link">śleṣa</a>] <a href="#entry-ubhayazle_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. ambiguité d'un texte combinant <a href="#entry-sabhafgazle_sa" class="skt-link">sabhaṅgaśleṣa</a> et <a href="#entry-abhafgazle_sa" class="skt-link">abhaṅgaśleṣa</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-ubhaya" class="skt-link">ubhaya</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uc">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">उच्</span> <i>uc</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">4</span>] pr. <i>(ucyati)</i> fut. <i>(</i><i>uciṣyati</i><i>)</i> pft. <i>(uvoca)</i> aor. [<span class="skt-grammar">5</span>] <i>(aucīt)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>ucita</i><i>)</i> ppft. <i>(ūcivas)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>sam</i><i>)</i> véd. prendre plaisir à <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> avoir l'habitude de; être apte à; être digne de.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ucca">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उच्च</span> <i>ucca</i> [<a href="#entry-uccaa" class="skt-link">uccā</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ucca" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ucca" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-uccaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>uccā</i> élevé, haut; aigu (son); intense — <a href="#entry-ucca" class="skt-link">m.</a> hauteur — i. pl. cf. <a href="#entry-uccais" class="skt-link">uccais</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ucca_n_da">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उच्चण्ड</span> <i>uccaṇḍa</i> [<a href="#entry-ut" class="skt-link">ut</a>-<a href="#entry-ca_n_da" class="skt-link">caṇḍa</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ucca_n_da" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ucca_n_da" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ucca_n_daa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>uccaṇḍā</i> <a href="#entry-ucca_n_dii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>uccaṇḍī</i> rapide, expéditif; passionné <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> violent, furieux, forcené; redoutable.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ucca_t">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उच्चट्</span> <i>uccaṭ</i> [<a href="#entry-ut" class="skt-link">ut</a>-<a href="#entry-ca_t" class="skt-link">caṭ</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(uccaṭati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>uccaṭita</i><i>)</i> disparaître — ca. <i>(uccāṭayati)</i> chasser, renvoyer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uccaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उच्चा</span> <i>uccā</i> [i. <a href="#entry-udac" class="skt-link">udac</a>] adv. au dessus, vers le haut; du haut; au Ciel.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uccaa_t">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उच्चाट्</span> <i>uccāṭ</i> [ca. <a href="#entry-ucca_t" class="skt-link">uccaṭ</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">10</span>] pr. <i>(uccāṭayati)</i> chasser, renvoyer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uccaa_tana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उच्चाटन</span> <i>uccāṭana</i> [act. <a href="#entry-uccaa_t" class="skt-link">uccāṭ</a>] <a href="#entry-uccaa_tana" class="skt-link">n.</a> renvoi, bannissement; destruction, éradication <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. [<a href="#entry-tantra" class="skt-link">tantra</a>] perte d'emploi par envoûtement — <a href="#entry-uccaa_tana" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. d'Uccāṭana, une des flèches de <a href="#entry-Ukaama" class="skt-link">Kāma</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uccaar">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उच्चार्</span> <i>uccār</i> [ca. <a href="#entry-uccar" class="skt-link">uccar</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">10</span>] pr. <i>(uccārayati)</i> prononcer, dire; émettre <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> évacuer; déféquer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uccaara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उच्चार</span> <i>uccāra</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-uccaar" class="skt-link">uccār</a>] a. <a href="#entry-uccaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-uccaara" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-uccaaraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>uccārā</i> qui s'élève — <a href="#entry-uccaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> décharge; évacuation, émission; excrément <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> énonciation.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uccaara_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उच्चारण</span> <i>uccāraṇa</i> [act. <a href="#entry-uccaar" class="skt-link">uccār</a>] <a href="#entry-uccaara_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> acte de prononcer, énonciation; récitation; acte de rendre audible.<br> <i>uccāraṇārtham</i> gram. pour les besoins de la prononciation.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uccaara_naj_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>uccāraṇajña</i> [<a href="#entry-j_na" class="skt-link">jña</a>] <a href="#entry-uccaara_naj_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> linguiste.<br> <a href="#entry-uccaara_na" class="skt-link">uccāraṇa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uccaavaca">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>uccāvaca</i> [<a href="#entry-ava" class="skt-link">ava</a>-<a href="#entry-ca" class="skt-link">ca</a>] a. <a href="#entry-uccaavaca" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-uccaavaca" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-uccaavacaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>uccāvacā</i> haut et bas, petit et grand; multiforme; hétérogène, irrégulier <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> varié, divers, de toutes sortes.<br> <i>uccāvacā</i> <i>janapadadharmā</i> <i>grāmadharmāśca</i> [<a href="#entry-Uaazvalaayanag_rhyasuutra" class="skt-link">Āśvalāyanagṛhyasūtra</a>] Les peuples ont des us et coutumes variés.<br> <a href="#entry-ucca" class="skt-link">ucca</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uccaaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उच्चाय</span> <i>uccāya</i> [act. <a href="#entry-ucci" class="skt-link">ucci</a>] <a href="#entry-uccaaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> cueillette (à la main, sans vol).<br> <i>phaloccāyaḥ</i> cueillette des fruits.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uccai_hzravas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>uccaiḥśravas</i> [<a href="#entry-zravas" class="skt-link">śravas</a>] <a href="#entry-uccai_hzravas" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. du cheval blanc merveilleux Uccaiḥśravā «Très Glorieux», issu du barratage de l'océan de lait [<a href="#entry-k_siirodamathana" class="skt-link">kṣīrodamathana</a>]; il est l'ancêtre de la race chevaline; <a href="#entry-Uindra" class="skt-link">Indra</a> se l'appropria pour conduire son char; [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] le voyant de loin, <a href="#entry-Ukadruu" class="skt-link">Kadrū</a> et <a href="#entry-Uvinataa" class="skt-link">Vinatā</a> parièrent sur la couleur de sa queue, avec comme enjeu l'esclavage de la perdante; <a href="#entry-Uvinataa" class="skt-link">Vinatā</a>, ayant parié noir, tenta de tricher en demandant à ses fils-serpents de recouvrir sa queue; sur leur refus, elle les condamna à périr dans le sacrifice de <a href="#entry-Ujanamejaya" class="skt-link">Janamejaya</a>; se ravisant, ils permirent à <a href="#entry-Uvinataa" class="skt-link">Vinatā</a> de gagner son pari, mais <a href="#entry-Ukadruu" class="skt-link">Kadrū</a> fut sauvée de l'esclavage par son fils <a href="#entry-Ugaru_da" class="skt-link">Garuḍa</a> qui déroba l'ambroisie [<a href="#entry-am_rta" class="skt-link">amṛta</a>] à la demande des serpents.<br> <a href="#entry-uccais" class="skt-link">uccais</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uccais">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उच्चैस्</span> <i>uccais</i> [i. pl. <a href="#entry-ucca" class="skt-link">ucca</a>] adv. en haut; à haute voix; très, fortement, intensément — iic. hautement ⟨ifc.⟩.<br> <i>uccaistarām</i> adv. plus haut.<br> <i>atyuccais</i> adv. très fort (voix, bruit).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uccar">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उच्चर्</span> <i>uccar</i> [<a href="#entry-ut" class="skt-link">ut</a>-<a href="#entry-car" class="skt-link">car</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(uccarati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>uccarita</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>pra</i><i>)</i> monter, s'élever; surgir — ca. <i>(uccārayati)</i> prononcer, dire, énoncer; émettre <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> évacuer; déféquer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uccara_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उच्चरण</span> <i>uccaraṇa</i> [act. <a href="#entry-uccar" class="skt-link">uccar</a>] <a href="#entry-uccara_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> soulèvement, débordement <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gram. énonciation.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uccarita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उच्चरित</span> <i>uccarita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-uccar" class="skt-link">uccar</a>] a. <a href="#entry-uccarita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-uccarita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-uccaritaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>uccaritā</i> émis; prononcé; évacué — <a href="#entry-uccarita" class="skt-link">n.</a> excrément.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uccataa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>uccatā</i> [<a href="#entry--taa" class="skt-link">-tā</a>] <a href="#entry-uccataa" class="skt-link">f.</a> supériorité.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uccatva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>uccatva</i> [<a href="#entry--tva" class="skt-link">-tva</a>] <a href="#entry-uccatva" class="skt-link">n.</a> supériorité.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uccaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उच्चय</span> <i>uccaya</i> [obj. <a href="#entry-ucci" class="skt-link">ucci</a>] <a href="#entry-uccaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> tas, pile, collection.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ucchal">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उच्छल्</span> <i>ucchal</i> [<a href="#entry-ut" class="skt-link">ut</a>-<a href="#entry-zal" class="skt-link">śal</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(ucchalati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>ucchalita</i><i>)</i> sauter, jaillir; déborder; s'envoler.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ucchalita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उच्छलित</span> <i>ucchalita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-ucchal" class="skt-link">ucchal</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ucchalita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ucchalita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ucchalitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ucchalitā</i> jailli.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ucche_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उच्छेष</span> <i>uccheṣa</i> [obj. <a href="#entry-ucchi_s" class="skt-link">ucchiṣ</a>] <a href="#entry-ucche_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> reste; déchet.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uccheda">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उच्छेद</span> <i>uccheda</i> [act. <a href="#entry-ucchid" class="skt-link">ucchid</a>] <a href="#entry-uccheda" class="skt-link">m.</a> action de couper, d'extirper; destruction <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> fait de mettre le holà à; abréger <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. excision.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ucchedana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उच्छेदन</span> <i>ucchedana</i> [act. <a href="#entry-ucchid" class="skt-link">ucchid</a>] <a href="#entry-ucchedana" class="skt-link">n.</a> découpage; destruction.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ucchedavaada">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ucchedavāda</i> [<a href="#entry-vaada" class="skt-link">vāda</a>] <a href="#entry-ucchedavaada" class="skt-link">m.</a> phil. doctrine disant que la mort produit l'anéantissement.<br> <a href="#entry-uccheda" class="skt-link">uccheda</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ucchedin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उच्छेदिन्</span> <i>ucchedin</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-ucchid" class="skt-link">ucchid</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ucchedin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ucchedin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ucchedinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ucchedinī</i> qui détruit.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ucchi_s">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उच्छिष्</span> <i>ucchiṣ</i> [<a href="#entry-ut" class="skt-link">ut</a>-<a href="#entry-zi_s" class="skt-link">śiṣ</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">7</span>] pr. <i>(ucchinaṣṭi)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>ucchiṣṭa</i><i>)</i> laisser en reste; rejeter; demeurer comme reste.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ucchi_s_ta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उच्छिष्ट</span> <i>ucchiṣṭa</i> var. <i>uñśiṣṭa</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-ucchi_s" class="skt-link">ucchiṣ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ucchi_s_ta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ucchi_s_ta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ucchi_s_taa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ucchiṣṭā</i> laissé (comme reste), rejeté, craché, vomi; gâté, abîmé — <a href="#entry-ucchi_s_ta" class="skt-link">m.</a> (homme) impur rituellement (not. ne s'étant pas rincé la bouche et les mains après un repas) — <a href="#entry-ucchi_s_ta" class="skt-link">n.</a> restes (de repas, de sacrifice).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ucchi_sya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उच्छिष्य</span> <i>ucchiṣya</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] <a href="#entry-ucchi_s" class="skt-link">ucchiṣ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ucchi_sya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ucchi_sya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ucchi_syaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ucchiṣyā</i> à laisser de reste
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ucchid">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उच्छिद्</span> <i>ucchid</i> [<a href="#entry-ut" class="skt-link">ut</a>-<a href="#entry-chid_1" class="skt-link">chid_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">7</span>] pr. <i>(ucchinatti)</i> pr. md. <i>(ucchintte)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>ucchinna</i><i>)</i> enlever, détruire; exterminer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uccho_sa_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उच्छोषण</span> <i>ucchoṣaṇa</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-ucchu_s" class="skt-link">ucchuṣ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-uccho_sa_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-uccho_sa_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-uccho_sa_naa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ucchoṣaṇā</i> qui dessèche <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. épith. de la brûlure d'amour [<a href="#entry-dahana" class="skt-link">dahana</a>], flèche de <a href="#entry-Ukaama" class="skt-link">Kāma</a> — <a href="#entry-uccho_sa_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> dessication.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ucchri">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उच्छ्रि</span> <i>ucchri</i> [<a href="#entry-ut" class="skt-link">ut</a>-<a href="#entry-zri" class="skt-link">śri</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(ucchrayati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>ucchrita</i><i>)</i> lever, ériger — pr. md. <i>(ucchrayate)</i> se lever.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ucchrita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उच्छ्रित</span> <i>ucchrita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-ucchri" class="skt-link">ucchri</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ucchrita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ucchrita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ucchritaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ucchritā</i> levé, dressé, haut; exubérant.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ucchu_s">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उच्छुष्</span> <i>ucchuṣ</i> [<a href="#entry-ut" class="skt-link">ut</a>-<a href="#entry-zu_s" class="skt-link">śuṣ</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(ucchuṣyati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>ucchuṣka</i><i>)</i> se dessécher — ca. <i>(ucchoṣayati)</i> dessécher; parcheminer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ucchu_ska">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उच्छुष्क</span> <i>ucchuṣka</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-ucchu_s" class="skt-link">ucchuṣ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ucchu_ska" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ucchu_ska" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ucchu_skaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ucchuṣkā</i> sec; desséché; racorni.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ucchvaasa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उच्छ्वास</span> <i>ucchvāsa</i> [<a href="#entry-ucchvas" class="skt-link">ucchvas</a>] <a href="#entry-ucchvaasa" class="skt-link">m.</a> respiration; inspiration profonde; pause pour respirer <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit. chapitre d'un ouvrage (not. le <a href="#entry-Udazakumaaracarita" class="skt-link">Daśakumāracarita</a>).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ucchvaasita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उच्छ्वासित</span> <i>ucchvāsita</i> [ca. pp. <a href="#entry-ucchvas" class="skt-link">ucchvas</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ucchvaasita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ucchvaasita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ucchvaasitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ucchvāsitā</i> délié, détaché; remis sur pied, réjoui <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hors d'haleine.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ucchvas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उच्छ्वस्</span> <i>ucchvas</i> [<a href="#entry-ut" class="skt-link">ut</a>-<a href="#entry-zvas_1" class="skt-link">śvas_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">2</span>] pr. <i>(ucchvasiti)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>ucchvasita</i><i>)</i> respirer, souffler; siffler, haleter; reprendre son souffle — ca. <i>(ucchvāsayati)</i> faire respirer, faire reprendre haleine; délier; calmer, détendre.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ucchvasita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उच्छ्वसित</span> <i>ucchvasita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-ucchvas" class="skt-link">ucchvas</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ucchvasita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ucchvasita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ucchvasitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ucchvasitā</i> qui souffle, qui palpite; calme, réconforté — <a href="#entry-ucchvasita" class="skt-link">n.</a> souffle vital, soupir.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ucci">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उच्चि</span> <i>ucci</i> [<a href="#entry-ut" class="skt-link">ut</a>-<a href="#entry-ci" class="skt-link">ci</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">5</span>] pr. <i>(uccinoti)</i> pr. md. <i>(uccinute)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>uccita</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>sam</i><i>)</i> rassembler, réunir; empiler.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ucita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उचित</span> <i>ucita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-uc" class="skt-link">uc</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ucita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ucita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ucitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ucitā</i> agréable; usuel, habituel; approprié à, convenable <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> habitué à, accoutumé.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ud">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उद्</span> <i>ud</i> cf. <a href="#entry-und" class="skt-link">und</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ud_r">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उदृ</span> <i>udṛ</i> [<a href="#entry-ut" class="skt-link">ut</a>-<a href="#entry-_r" class="skt-link">ṛ</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">3</span>] pr. <i>(udiyarti)</i> s'élever.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uda">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उद</span> <i>uda</i> [<a href="#entry-und" class="skt-link">und</a>] <a href="#entry-uda" class="skt-link">n.</a> eau.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-udaacar">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उदाचर्</span> <i>udācar</i> [<a href="#entry-ut" class="skt-link">ut</a>-<a href="#entry-aacar" class="skt-link">ācar</a>] pf. <i>(</i><i>sam</i><i>)</i> [inusité sans pf.].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-udaah_r">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उदाहृ</span> <i>udāhṛ</i> [<a href="#entry-ut" class="skt-link">ut</a>-<a href="#entry-aah_r" class="skt-link">āhṛ</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(udāharati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>udāhṛta</i><i>)</i> exprimer; annoncer, citer, mentionner; donner comme exemple.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-udaah_rta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उदाहृत</span> <i>udāhṛta</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-udaah_r" class="skt-link">udāhṛ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-udaah_rta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-udaah_rta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-udaah_rtaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>udāhṛtā</i> dit, exprimé; mentionné; donné en exemple <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> appelé, nommé, intitulé.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-udaahaara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उदाहार</span> <i>udāhāra</i> [act. <a href="#entry-udaah_r" class="skt-link">udāhṛ</a>] <a href="#entry-udaahaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> exemple; illustration.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-udaahara_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उदाहरण</span> <i>udāharaṇa</i> [act. <a href="#entry-udaah_r" class="skt-link">udāhṛ</a>] <a href="#entry-udaahara_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> déclaration; récit, mention; exemple, illustration <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. [<a href="#entry-nyaaya" class="skt-link">nyāya</a>] l'exemple à l'appui du syllogisme [<a href="#entry-pa_ncaavayava" class="skt-link">pañcāvayava</a>] <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gram. exemple d'application d'une règle; opp. <a href="#entry-pratyudaahara_na" class="skt-link">pratyudāharaṇa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-udaahara_nakoza">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>udāharaṇakośa</i> [<a href="#entry-koza" class="skt-link">kośa</a>] <a href="#entry-udaahara_nakoza" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. np. de l'Udāharaṇakośa, recueil des exemples de l'<a href="#entry-Ua_s_taadhyaayii" class="skt-link">Aṣṭādhyāyī</a> et de ses commentaires.<br> <a href="#entry-udaahara_na" class="skt-link">udāharaṇa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-udaahara_niiya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उदाहरणीय</span> <i>udāharaṇīya</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">2</span>] <a href="#entry-udaah_r" class="skt-link">udāhṛ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-udaahara_niiya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-udaahara_niiya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-udaahara_niiyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>udāharaṇīyā</i> à citer en exemple; à mentioner <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gram. devant être mentionné (<a href="#entry-suutra" class="skt-link">sūtra</a>).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-udaana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उदान</span> <i>udāna</i> [<a href="#entry-ut" class="skt-link">ut</a>-<a href="#entry-aana" class="skt-link">āna</a>] <a href="#entry-udaana" class="skt-link">m.</a> phil. [<a href="#entry-yoga" class="skt-link">yoga</a>] le flux d'élévation, l'un des 5 souffles vitaux [<a href="#entry-praa_na" class="skt-link">prāṇa</a>].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-udaanavarga">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>udānavarga</i> [<a href="#entry-varga" class="skt-link">varga</a>] <a href="#entry-udaanavarga" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. np. de l'Udānavarga, texte bd.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-udaanavargavivara_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>udānavargavivaraṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-vivara_na" class="skt-link">vivaraṇa</a>] <a href="#entry-udaanavargavivara_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. np. de l'Udānavargavivaraṇa, commentaire de l'<a href="#entry-Uudaanavarga" class="skt-link">Udānavarga</a> dû à <a href="#entry-Upraj_naavarmaa" class="skt-link">Prajñāvarmā</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-udaanavarga" class="skt-link">udānavarga</a> <br> <a href="#entry-udaana" class="skt-link">udāna</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-udaara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उदार</span> <i>udāra</i> [<a href="#entry-ut" class="skt-link">ut</a>-<a href="#entry-_r" class="skt-link">ṛ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-udaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-udaara" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-udaaraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>udārā</i> grand, noble, majestueux, magnifique, excellent, illustre; énergique <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> honnête, agréable; généreux, magnanime — a. <a href="#entry-udaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-udaara" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-udaarii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>udārī</i> id.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-udaaracarita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>udāracarita</i> [<a href="#entry-carita" class="skt-link">carita</a>] a. <a href="#entry-udaaracarita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-udaaracarita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-udaaracaritaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>udāracaritā</i> qui se comporte avec noblesse.<br> <a href="#entry-udaara" class="skt-link">udāra</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-udaaraka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>udāraka</i> [<a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] a. <a href="#entry-udaaraka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-udaaraka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-udaarikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>udārikā</i> généreux — <a href="#entry-udaaraka" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. [DKC.] np. d'Udāraka «Généreux», épith. de <a href="#entry-Udhanamitra" class="skt-link">Dhanamitra</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-udaasa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उदास</span> <i>udāsa</i> [<a href="#entry-udas" class="skt-link">udas</a>] <a href="#entry-udaasa" class="skt-link">m.</a> lancer, jet; expulsion.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-udaatta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उदात्त</span> <i>udātta</i> [<a href="#entry-ut" class="skt-link">ut</a>-<a href="#entry-aatta" class="skt-link">ātta</a>] a. <a href="#entry-udaatta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-udaatta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-udaattaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>udāttā</i> élevé, haut; noble — <a href="#entry-udaatta" class="skt-link">m.</a> phon. accent aigu (opp. <a href="#entry-svarita" class="skt-link">svarita</a>).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-udaavah">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उदावह्</span> <i>udāvah</i> [<a href="#entry-ut" class="skt-link">ut</a>-<a href="#entry-aavah" class="skt-link">āvah</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(udāvahati)</i> emporter, traîner hors de; épouser.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-udaavas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उदावस्</span> <i>udāvas</i> [<a href="#entry-ut" class="skt-link">ut</a>-<a href="#entry-aavas" class="skt-link">āvas</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(udāvasati)</i> déménager; émigrer — ca. <i>(udāvāsayati)</i> expulser.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-udaavasu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उदावसु</span> <i>udāvasu</i> [<a href="#entry-udaavas" class="skt-link">udāvas</a>] <a href="#entry-udaavasu" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. [BhP.] np. d'Udāvasu, fils du roi <a href="#entry-Umithi" class="skt-link">Mithi</a> <a href="#entry-Ujanaka" class="skt-link">Janaka</a> de <a href="#entry-Umithilaa" class="skt-link">Mithilā</a>, père de <a href="#entry-Unandivardhana" class="skt-link">Nandivardhana</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-udaaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उदाय</span> <i>udāya</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-udi" class="skt-link">udi</a>] <a href="#entry-udaaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> émergent, qui s'avance.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-udac">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उदच्</span> <i>udac</i> [<a href="#entry-ut" class="skt-link">ut</a>-<a href="#entry-ac" class="skt-link">ac</a>] a. <a href="#entry-udac" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-udac" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-udiicii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>udīcī</i> (tourné) vers le haut <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> (tourné) vers le Nord — i. <i>uccā</i> adv. en haut — <a href="#entry-udiicii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>udīcī</i> la direction du Nord.<br> <i>udak</i> adv. en haut, vers le haut; vers le Nord.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-udadhi">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>udadhi</i> [<a href="#entry-dhi" class="skt-link">dhi</a>] <a href="#entry-udadhi" class="skt-link">m.</a> mer, océan.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-udaf">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उदङ्</span> <i>udaṅ</i> iic. de <a href="#entry-udac" class="skt-link">udac</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-udafmukha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>udaṅmukha</i> [<a href="#entry-mukha" class="skt-link">mukha</a>] a. <a href="#entry-udafmukha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-udafmukha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-udafmukhii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>udaṅmukhī</i> qui est face au Nord.<br> <a href="#entry-udaf" class="skt-link">udaṅ</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-udag">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उदग्</span> <i>udag</i> iic. de <a href="#entry-udac" class="skt-link">udac</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-udagayana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>udagayana</i> [<a href="#entry-ayana" class="skt-link">ayana</a>] <a href="#entry-udagayana" class="skt-link">n.</a> syn. <a href="#entry-uttaraaya_na" class="skt-link">uttarāyaṇa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-udagdaza">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>udagdaśa</i> [<a href="#entry-dazaa" class="skt-link">daśā</a>] a. <a href="#entry-udagdaza" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-udagdaza" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-udagdazaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>udagdaśā</i> qui a les coutures vers le haut, dont on voit la trame.<br> <a href="#entry-udag" class="skt-link">udag</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-udagra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उदग्र</span> <i>udagra</i> [<a href="#entry-ut" class="skt-link">ut</a>-<a href="#entry-agra" class="skt-link">agra</a>] a. <a href="#entry-udagra" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-udagra" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-udagraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>udagrā</i> vaste, puissant, intense <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> excité, furieux.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-udaj">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उदज्</span> <i>udaj</i> [<a href="#entry-ut" class="skt-link">ut</a>-<a href="#entry-aj" class="skt-link">aj</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(udajati)</i> pr. md. <i>(udajate)</i> expulser de.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-udaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उदक</span> <i>udaka</i> [<a href="#entry-uda" class="skt-link">uda</a><a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] <a href="#entry-udaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> eau — v. [<span class="skt-grammar">11</span>] pr. <i>(udakīyati)</i> vouloir de l'eau.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-udakaaghaata">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>udakāghāta</i> [<a href="#entry-aaghaata" class="skt-link">āghāta</a>] <a href="#entry-udakaaghaata" class="skt-link">m.</a> le jeu de bataille dans l'eau, l'un des 64 arts [<a href="#entry-kalaa" class="skt-link">kalā</a>].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-udakaa_njali">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>udakāñjali</i> [<a href="#entry-a_njali" class="skt-link">añjali</a>] <a href="#entry-udakaa_njali" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. position des mains jointes remplies d'eau (pour l'aspersion rituelle).<br> <a href="#entry-udaka" class="skt-link">udaka</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-udakavaadya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>udakavādya</i> [<a href="#entry-vaadya" class="skt-link">vādya</a>] <a href="#entry-udakavaadya" class="skt-link">n.</a> la musique exécutée dans l'eau, l'un des 64 arts [<a href="#entry-kalaa" class="skt-link">kalā</a>].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-udakumbha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>udakumbha</i> [<a href="#entry-kumbha" class="skt-link">kumbha</a>] <a href="#entry-udakumbha" class="skt-link">m.</a> pot à eau <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> jarre pleine d'eau.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-udakya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उदक्य</span> <i>udakya</i> [<a href="#entry-udaka" class="skt-link">udaka</a><a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-udakya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-udakya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-udakyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>udakyā</i> qui est dans l'eau — <a href="#entry-udakyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>udakyā</i> femme qui menstrue.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-udan">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उदन्</span> <i>udan</i> [relié à <a href="#entry-udaka" class="skt-link">udaka</a>] <a href="#entry-udan" class="skt-link">n.</a> eau — v. [<span class="skt-grammar">11</span>] pr. <i>(udanyati)</i> désirer boire de l'eau.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-udanta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उदन्त</span> <i>udanta</i> [<a href="#entry-ut" class="skt-link">ut</a>-<a href="#entry-anta" class="skt-link">anta</a>] a. <a href="#entry-udanta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-udanta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-udantaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>udantā</i> qui atteint la fin ou le bord; qui dépasse, qui déborde <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> excellent — <a href="#entry-udanta" class="skt-link">m.</a> fin du travail, repos; temps de la récolte <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> compréhension totale; fin mot, information complète <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> informations, nouvelles.<br> <i>udantam</i> adv. jusqu'au bord; jusqu'au bout, jusqu'à la fin.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-udantaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>udantaka</i> [<a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] <a href="#entry-udantaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> informations, nouvelles — <a href="#entry-udantikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>udantikā</i> satisfaction, plénitude, satiété.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-udantya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>udantya</i> [<a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-udantya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-udantya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-udantyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>udantyā</i> hors des limites (aryennes).<br> <a href="#entry-udanta" class="skt-link">udanta</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-udapaana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>udapāna</i> [<a href="#entry-paana" class="skt-link">pāna</a>] <a href="#entry-udapaana" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-udapaana" class="skt-link">n.</a> puits.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-udapaanama_n_duuka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>udapānamaṇḍūka</i> [<a href="#entry-ma_n_duuka" class="skt-link">maṇḍūka</a>] <a href="#entry-udapaanama_n_duuka" class="skt-link">m.</a> [«grenouille de puits»] casanier, borné; syn. <a href="#entry-kuupama_n_duuka" class="skt-link">kūpamaṇḍūka</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-udapaana" class="skt-link">udapāna</a> <br> <a href="#entry-uda" class="skt-link">uda</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-udara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उदर</span> <i>udara</i> [<a href="#entry-ut" class="skt-link">ut</a>-<a href="#entry-_r" class="skt-link">ṛ</a>] <a href="#entry-udara" class="skt-link">n.</a> ventre; estomac; sein, ventre maternel <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lat. <i>uterus</i>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-udas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उदस्</span> <i>udas</i> [<a href="#entry-ut" class="skt-link">ut</a>-<a href="#entry-as_2" class="skt-link">as_2</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">4</span>] pr. <i>(udasyati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>udasta</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>pari</i><i>)</i> lancer en l'air, jeter vers le haut; lancer (une arme de jet) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> élever, ériger <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> jeter dehors, expulser.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-udasta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उदस्त</span> <i>udasta</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-udas" class="skt-link">udas</a>] a. <a href="#entry-udasta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-udasta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-udastaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>udastā</i> lancé, jeté; expulsé.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-udaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उदय</span> <i>udaya</i> [act. <a href="#entry-udi" class="skt-link">udi</a>] <a href="#entry-udaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> lever; lever du soleil ou de la lune <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> astr. ascendant d'un signe du zodiaque [<a href="#entry-raazi" class="skt-link">rāśi</a>] <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> apparition, arrivée; ascension, succès, avantage <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> conclusion, conséquence; ce qui suit.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-udayaaditya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>udayāditya</i> [<a href="#entry-aaditya" class="skt-link">āditya</a>] <a href="#entry-udayaaditya" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. d'Udayāditya «Soleil levant», roi <a href="#entry-Uparamaara" class="skt-link">Paramāra</a> qui régnait à <a href="#entry-Udhaaraa" class="skt-link">Dhārā</a> au 11⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle, fils de <a href="#entry-Usindhuraaja" class="skt-link">Sindhurāja</a>, frère cadet de <a href="#entry-Ubhoja_1" class="skt-link">Bhoja_1</a> auquel il succéda; <a href="#entry-Unaravarmaa" class="skt-link">Naravarmā</a> est son fils.<br> <a href="#entry-udaya" class="skt-link">udaya</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-udayagiri">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>udayagiri</i> [<a href="#entry-giri" class="skt-link">giri</a>] <a href="#entry-udayagiri" class="skt-link">m.</a> montagne de l'Est (derrière laquelle on dit que le Soleil se lève) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> géo. np. de la ville fortifiée d'Udayagiri, capitale de <a href="#entry-Ulaafguula" class="skt-link">Lāṅgūla</a> <a href="#entry-Ugajapati" class="skt-link">Gajapati</a>, en pays <a href="#entry-Uandhra" class="skt-link">Andhra</a>; sa citadelle, réputée imprenable, fut conquise par <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_nadeva" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇadeva</a> <a href="#entry-Uraaya" class="skt-link">Rāya</a> en 1513, après un siège de 18 mois; une tradition locale l'associe au mont <a href="#entry-Umahodaya" class="skt-link">Mahodaya</a> où <a href="#entry-Uhanumaan" class="skt-link">Hanumān</a> trouva l'herbe de résurrection [<a href="#entry-sa_njiivanii" class="skt-link">saṃjīvanī</a>] <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> géo. np. de la colline d'Udayagiri_1, en Orissa; cf. <a href="#entry-Upu_spagiri" class="skt-link">Puṣpagiri</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> géo. np. de la colline d'Udayagiri_2, jumelle de celle de <a href="#entry-Ukha_n_dagiri" class="skt-link">Khaṇḍagiri</a>, à 8 km au nord de <a href="#entry-Ubhubanezvar" class="skt-link">Bhubaneśvar</a>, Orissa; c'est un site de cavernes creusées par des moines jaïnes (2⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle ant.); la cave [<a href="#entry-gumpha" class="skt-link">gumpha</a>] de la Reine [<a href="#entry-raa_nii" class="skt-link">rāṇī</a>] contient des sculptures; la cave de l'éléphant [<a href="#entry-haathiigumpha" class="skt-link">hāthīgumpha</a>] porte une inscription du roi <a href="#entry-Ukhaaravela" class="skt-link">Khāravela</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> géo. np. de la colline d'Udayagiri_3 de <a href="#entry-Uvidizaa" class="skt-link">Vidiśā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-udayana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उदयन</span> <i>udayana</i> [act. <a href="#entry-udi" class="skt-link">udi</a>] <a href="#entry-udayana" class="skt-link">n.</a> lever (not. du soleil) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> sortie; résultat, conclusion — <a href="#entry-udayana" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. d'Udayana, roi du pays <a href="#entry-Uvatsa" class="skt-link">Vatsa</a>, fils de <a href="#entry-Usahasraaniika" class="skt-link">Sahasrānīka</a>, descendant présumé des <a href="#entry-Upaa_n_davaas" class="skt-link">Pāṇḍavās</a>; la légende raconte que sa mère la reine <a href="#entry-Um_rgaavatii" class="skt-link">Mṛgāvatī</a> fut enlevée par un oiseau qui l'abandonna dans les bois, et recueillie par <a href="#entry-Ujamadagni" class="skt-link">Jamadagni</a> dans son ermitage, où elle accoucha d'un fils d'une merveilleuse beauté qu'elle nomma <a href="#entry-Uudayana" class="skt-link">Udayana</a> «Soleil levant»; un jour, <a href="#entry-Uudayana" class="skt-link">Udayana</a> à la chasse rencontra un montagnard qui avait capturé un serpent, qu'il libéra en échange d'un bracelet d'or que lui avait confié sa mère; le serpent, un <a href="#entry-naaga" class="skt-link">nāga</a> nommé <a href="#entry-Uvasunemi" class="skt-link">Vasunemi</a>, lui remit un luth merveilleux <a href="#entry-Ugho_savatii" class="skt-link">Ghoṣavatī</a> et lui apprit l'art de tresser des guirlandes qui ne se fanent jamais; le montagnard vendit le bracelet, gravé au nom de <a href="#entry-Usahasraaniika" class="skt-link">Sahasrānīka</a>, ce qui permit à son père de le retrouver; plus tard <a href="#entry-Usahasraaniika" class="skt-link">Sahasrānīka</a> abdiqua en sa faveur, et se retira dans l'<a href="#entry-Uhimaalaya" class="skt-link">Himālaya</a>; contemporain du <a href="#entry-Ubuddha" class="skt-link">Buddha</a>, dont il était disciple [<a href="#entry-upaasaka" class="skt-link">upāsaka</a>], il régna à <a href="#entry-Ukauzaambii" class="skt-link">Kauśāmbī</a> vers le 5⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle ant.; il ne songeait qu'à la chasse, au vin et aux femmes; il charmait les éléphants sauvages avec son luth magique; le <a href="#entry-Ukathaasaritsaagara" class="skt-link">Kathāsaritsāgara</a> et la <a href="#entry-Upriyadarzikaa" class="skt-link">Priyadarśikā</a> vantent ses conquêtes; le roi <a href="#entry-Uca_n_damahaasena" class="skt-link">Caṇḍamahāsena</a>-<a href="#entry-Upradyota" class="skt-link">Pradyota</a> d'<a href="#entry-Uavanti" class="skt-link">Avanti</a> voulut lui faire épouser sa fille <a href="#entry-Uvaasavadattaa_2" class="skt-link">Vāsavadattā_2</a>; il construisit un éléphant de bois plein de soldats pour le capturer et l'emmener à <a href="#entry-Uujjayinii" class="skt-link">Ujjayinī</a>; il promit la vie sauve à <a href="#entry-Uudayana" class="skt-link">Udayana</a> s'il apprenait la musique à la princesse avec son luth magique; tombé amoureux de <a href="#entry-Uvaasavadattaa_2" class="skt-link">Vāsavadattā_2</a>, <a href="#entry-Uudayana" class="skt-link">Udayana</a> s'évada en l'enlevant et l'épousa; il était réputé volage, et de nombreuses histoires amoureuses lui sont attribuées, not. la <a href="#entry-Uratnaavalii_1" class="skt-link">Ratnāvalī_1</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hist. np. d'Udayana_1 ou Udayanācārya «Maître de l'inférence», célèbre logicien du 11⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle (~1050–1100), précurseur du <a href="#entry-navyanyaaya" class="skt-link">navyanyāya</a>; il unifia l'épistémologie et la logique du <a href="#entry-nyaaya" class="skt-link">nyāya</a> avec l'ontologie du <a href="#entry-vaize_sika" class="skt-link">vaiśeṣika</a>; il est l'auteur de nombreux ouvrages, not. la <a href="#entry-Ukira_naavalii" class="skt-link">Kiraṇāvalī</a>, la <a href="#entry-Ulak_sa_naavalii" class="skt-link">Lakṣaṇāvalī</a>, le <a href="#entry-Unyaayakusumaa_njali" class="skt-link">Nyāyakusumāñjali</a> et la <a href="#entry-Unyaayavaarttikataatparyaparizuddhi" class="skt-link">Nyāyavārttikatātparyapariśuddhi</a> ou <a href="#entry-Unibandha" class="skt-link">Nibandha</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-udayapura">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>udayapura</i> [<a href="#entry-pura" class="skt-link">pura</a>] <a href="#entry-udayapura" class="skt-link">n.</a> géo. np. d'Udayapura, capitale du royaume Mewar, fondée en 1559 par <a href="#entry-Umahaaraa_naa" class="skt-link">Mahārāṇā</a> Udaya Siṃha II; mod. Udaïpur, Rajasthan.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-udayin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उदयिन्</span> <i>udayin</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-udi" class="skt-link">udi</a>] a. <a href="#entry-udayin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-udayin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-udayinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>udayinī</i> qui monte, qui s'élève <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> prospère — <a href="#entry-udayin" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. du roi Udayī ou Udayibhadra «Prospère», fils d'<a href="#entry-Uajaatazatru" class="skt-link">Ajātaśatru</a>, qu'il assassina en 459 ant.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uda_nc">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उदञ्च्</span> <i>udañc</i> [<a href="#entry-ut" class="skt-link">ut</a>-<a href="#entry-a_nc" class="skt-link">añc</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(udañcati)</i> pr. md. <i>(udacyate)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>udañcita</i><i>)</i> élever, s'élever; émaner de.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-udbaa_spa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उद्बाष्प</span> <i>udbāṣpa</i> [<a href="#entry-ut" class="skt-link">ut</a>-<a href="#entry-baa_spa" class="skt-link">bāṣpa</a>] a. <a href="#entry-udbaa_spa" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-udbaa_spa" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-udbaa_spaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>udbāṣpā</i> rempli de larmes.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-udbaahuka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उद्बाहुक</span> <i>udbāhuka</i> [<a href="#entry-ut" class="skt-link">ut</a>-<a href="#entry-baahuka" class="skt-link">bāhuka</a>] a. <a href="#entry-udbaahuka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-udbaahuka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-udbaahukaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>udbāhukā</i> aux bras étendus.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-udbha_ta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उद्भट</span> <i>udbhaṭa</i> <a href="#entry-udbha_ta" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. du poète <a href="#entry-Ubha_t_ta" class="skt-link">Bhaṭṭa</a> Udbhaṭa ou Bhaṭṭodhbaṭa, directeur de l'Académie [<a href="#entry-sabhaapati" class="skt-link">sabhāpati</a>] du roi du <a href="#entry-Ukazmiira" class="skt-link">Kaśmīra</a> <a href="#entry-Ujayaapii_da" class="skt-link">Jayāpīḍa</a>; premier théoricien de l'expérience esthétique [<a href="#entry-rasa" class="skt-link">rasa</a>], il est l'auteur du <a href="#entry-Ukaavyaalafkaarasaarasafgraha" class="skt-link">Kāvyālaṅkārasārasaṅgraha</a>, et d'un commentaire du <a href="#entry-Unaa_tyazaastra" class="skt-link">Nāṭyaśāstra</a> aujourd'hui perdu; cf. <a href="#entry-sthaayibhaava" class="skt-link">sthāyibhāva</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-udbhaas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उद्भास्</span> <i>udbhās</i> [<a href="#entry-ut" class="skt-link">ut</a>-<a href="#entry-bhaas_1" class="skt-link">bhās_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(udbhāsati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>udbhāsita</i><i>)</i> briller, illuminer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-udbhava">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उद्भव</span> <i>udbhava</i> [act. <a href="#entry-udbhuu" class="skt-link">udbhū</a>] <a href="#entry-udbhava" class="skt-link">m.</a> naissance, origine; patrie, lieu de naissance — ifc. provenant de, ayant pour origine.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-udbhid_1">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उद्भिद्</span> <i>udbhid_1</i> [<a href="#entry-ut" class="skt-link">ut</a>-<a href="#entry-bhid_1" class="skt-link">bhid_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">7</span>] pr. <i>(udbhinatti)</i> pr. md. <i>(bhintte)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>udbhinna</i><i>)</i> surgir; devenir visible.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-udbhid_2">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उद्भिद्</span> <i>udbhid_2</i> [<a href="#entry-udbhid_1" class="skt-link">udbhid_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-udbhid_2" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-udbhid_2" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-udbhid_2" class="skt-link">f.</a> qui pénètre; qui déborde; qui germe; abondant — <a href="#entry-udbhid_2" class="skt-link">f.</a> germe, bourgeon; pousse.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-udbhijja">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>udbhijja</i> [<a href="#entry-ja" class="skt-link">ja</a>] a. <a href="#entry-udbhijja" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-udbhijja" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-udbhijjaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>udbhijjā</i> qui germe, qui pousse.<br> <a href="#entry-udbhid_2" class="skt-link">udbhid_2</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-udbhraanta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उद्भ्रान्त</span> <i>udbhrānta</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-udbhram" class="skt-link">udbhram</a>] a. <a href="#entry-udbhraanta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-udbhraanta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-udbhraantaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>udbhrāntā</i> monté, perché; agité, brandi <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> troublé; périlleux — <a href="#entry-udbhraanta" class="skt-link">n.</a> fait de brandir (not. une épée).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-udbhram">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उद्भ्रम्</span> <i>udbhram</i> [<a href="#entry-ut" class="skt-link">ut</a>-<a href="#entry-bhram" class="skt-link">bhram</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(udbhramati)</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">4</span>] pr. <i>(udbhrāmyati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>udbhrānta</i><i>)</i> s'élever, s'agiter dans les airs, être brandi — ca. <i>(udbhrāmayati)</i> brandir.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-udbhuu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उद्भू</span> <i>udbhū</i> [<a href="#entry-ut" class="skt-link">ut</a>-<a href="#entry-bhuu_1" class="skt-link">bhū_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(udbhavati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>udbhūta</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>sam</i><i>)</i> atteindre, égaler <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> se lever; surgir, apparaître; croître — ca. <i>(ubbhāvayati)</i> produire, créer <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> expliquer, rendre apparent.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-udbhuuta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उद्भूत</span> <i>udbhūta</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-udbhuu" class="skt-link">udbhū</a>] a. <a href="#entry-udbhuuta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-udbhuuta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-udbhuutaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>udbhūtā</i> produit, apparu, né; surgi; élevé, poussé, grandi; perceptible.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-udbuddha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उद्बुद्ध</span> <i>udbuddha</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-udbudh" class="skt-link">udbudh</a>] a. <a href="#entry-udbuddha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-udbuddha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-udbuddhaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>udbuddhā</i> réveillé <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> excité <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> rappelé; souvenu; auquel on a fait penser à.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-udbudh">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उद्बुध्</span> <i>udbudh</i> [<a href="#entry-ut" class="skt-link">ut</a>-<a href="#entry-budh_1" class="skt-link">budh_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">4</span>] pr. md. <i>(udbudhyate)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>udbuddha</i><i>)</i> s'éveiller — ca. <i>(udbodhayati)</i> faire comprendre; informer; instruire.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-udda_n_da">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उद्दण्ड</span> <i>uddaṇḍa</i> [<a href="#entry-ut" class="skt-link">ut</a>-<a href="#entry-da_n_da" class="skt-link">daṇḍa</a>] a. <a href="#entry-udda_n_da" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-udda_n_da" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-udda_n_daa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>uddaṇḍā</i> qui tient une bannière, une massue ou une canne (se dit des gardiens) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> qui a un bâton en main; qui tient le sceptre <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hist. np. du poète Uddaṇḍa <a href="#entry-Uzaastrii" class="skt-link">Śāstrī</a> (15⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle); né dans un village près de <a href="#entry-Ukaa_nciipuram" class="skt-link">Kāñcīpuram</a>, il s'établit au <a href="#entry-Ukerala" class="skt-link">Kerala</a>; il est l'auteur not. du <a href="#entry-Ukokilasandeza" class="skt-link">Kokilasandeśa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uddaalaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उद्दालक</span> <i>uddālaka</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-uddal" class="skt-link">uddal</a>] <a href="#entry-uddaalaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> phil. [ChU.] np. d'Uddālaka <a href="#entry-Uaaru_ni" class="skt-link">Āruṇi</a>, philosophe du clan <a href="#entry-Ugautama" class="skt-link">Gautama</a> qui enseigna le vrai sens du <a href="#entry-vedaanta" class="skt-link">vedānta</a> à son fils <a href="#entry-Uzvetaketu" class="skt-link">Śvetaketu</a>; [BU.] il débattit sur le <a href="#entry-brahman" class="skt-link">brahman</a> avec <a href="#entry-Uyaaj_navalkya" class="skt-link">Yājñavalkya</a> et fut instruit sur la transmigration par <a href="#entry-Upravaaha_na" class="skt-link">Pravāhaṇa</a> Jaivali.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uddaanta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उद्दान्त</span> <i>uddānta</i> [<a href="#entry-ut" class="skt-link">ut</a>-<a href="#entry-daanta" class="skt-link">dānta</a>] a. <a href="#entry-uddaanta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-uddaanta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-uddaantaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>uddāntā</i> humble.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uddaantapurii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>uddāntapurī</i> [<a href="#entry-purii" class="skt-link">purī</a>] <a href="#entry-uddaantapurii" class="skt-link">f.</a> géo. np. d'Uddāntapurī, monastère et université bd. du <a href="#entry-Umagadha" class="skt-link">Magadha</a>, proche de <a href="#entry-Unaalandaa" class="skt-link">Nālandā</a>, établi par le roi <a href="#entry-Udharmapaala_1" class="skt-link">Dharmapāla_1</a> au 8⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle; il fut détruit avec <a href="#entry-Unaalandaa" class="skt-link">Nālandā</a> par les musulmans en 1193.<br> <a href="#entry-uddaanta" class="skt-link">uddānta</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uddal">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उद्दल्</span> <i>uddal</i> [<a href="#entry-ut" class="skt-link">ut</a>-<a href="#entry-dal" class="skt-link">dal</a>] ca. <i>(uddālayati)</i> faire éclater, déchirer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uddeza">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उद्देश</span> <i>uddeśa</i> [<a href="#entry-uddiz" class="skt-link">uddiś</a>] <a href="#entry-uddeza" class="skt-link">m.</a> mention, allusion; stipulation, prescription <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lieu, emplacement, siège.<br> <i>uddeśena</i> var. <i>uddeśāt</i> ind. ifc. en vue de, à propos de.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uddezaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>uddeśaka</i> [<a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] a. <a href="#entry-uddezaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-uddezaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-uddezikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>uddeśikā</i> illustratif, explicatoire — <a href="#entry-uddezaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> illustration, exemple <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> math. question, problème.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uddezatas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>uddeśatas</i> [<a href="#entry--tas" class="skt-link">-tas</a>] adv. typiquement; par exemple, en illustration; en bref.<br> <a href="#entry-uddeza" class="skt-link">uddeśa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uddezya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उद्देश्य</span> <i>uddeśya</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] <a href="#entry-uddiz" class="skt-link">uddiś</a>] a. <a href="#entry-uddezya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-uddezya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-uddezyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>uddeśyā</i> à expliquer <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> ce dont il est question; ce qui est exprimé en premier <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> destiné à — <a href="#entry-uddezya" class="skt-link">n.</a> but; stimulant <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gram. sujet (opp. prédicat [<a href="#entry-vidheya" class="skt-link">vidheya</a>]); thème <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. [<a href="#entry-miimaa_msaa" class="skt-link">mīmāṃsā</a>] but principal d'une injonction.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uddh_r">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उद्धृ</span> <i>uddhṛ</i> [<a href="#entry-ut" class="skt-link">ut</a>-<a href="#entry-h_r_1" class="skt-link">hṛ_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(uddharati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>uddhṛta</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>sam</i><i>)</i> tirer, extraire; arracher <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> arracher au danger (not. aux flammes); secourir, sauver.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uddh_r_s">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उद्धृष्</span> <i>uddhṛṣ</i> [<a href="#entry-ut" class="skt-link">ut</a>-<a href="#entry-dh_r_s" class="skt-link">dhṛṣ</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. md. <i>(uddharṣate)</i> se réjouir; se faire une joie de — v. [<span class="skt-grammar">4</span>] pr. <i>(uddhṛṣyati)</i> être en joie; s'épanouir — ca. <i>(uddharṣayati)</i> réjouir: encourager.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uddh_rta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उद्धृत</span> <i>uddhṛta</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-uddh_r" class="skt-link">uddhṛ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-uddh_rta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-uddh_rta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-uddh_rtaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>uddhṛtā</i> tiré, extrait, arraché, enlevé <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> secouru, sauvé.
  </div>
</div>
