<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uddh_rtasneha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>uddhṛtasneha</i> [<a href="#entry-sneha" class="skt-link">sneha</a>] a. <a href="#entry-uddh_rtasneha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-uddh_rtasneha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-uddh_rtasnehaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>uddhṛtasnehā</i> dont on a extrait l'huile.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uddh_rtoddhaara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>uddhṛtoddhāra</i> [<a href="#entry-uddhaara" class="skt-link">uddhāra</a>] a. <a href="#entry-uddh_rtoddhaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-uddh_rtoddhaara" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-uddh_rtoddhaaraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>uddhṛtoddhārā</i> sauf exception; exceptionnel.<br> <a href="#entry-uddh_rta" class="skt-link">uddhṛta</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uddhaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उद्धा</span> <i>uddhā</i> [<a href="#entry-ut" class="skt-link">ut</a>-<a href="#entry-haa_2" class="skt-link">hā_2</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">3</span>] pr. md. <i>(ujjihīte)</i> ppr. md. <i>(ujjihāna)</i> s'élever; se lever <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> s'ouvrir (porte) — ca. <i>(uddhāṭayati)</i> ouvrir (avec ⟨i.⟩).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uddhaara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उद्धार</span> <i>uddhāra</i> [<a href="#entry-uddh_r" class="skt-link">uddhṛ</a>] <a href="#entry-uddhaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> fait de tirer, d'arracher; sauvetage <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> discernement, choix, sélection; déduction; rachat; exception.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uddhama">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उद्धम</span> <i>uddhama</i> [<a href="#entry-ut" class="skt-link">ut</a>-<a href="#entry-dhama" class="skt-link">dhama</a>] a. <a href="#entry-uddhama" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-uddhama" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-uddhamaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>uddhamā</i> qui sonne (not. la conque [<a href="#entry-zafkha" class="skt-link">śaṅkha</a>]).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uddhan">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उद्धन्</span> <i>uddhan</i> [<a href="#entry-ut" class="skt-link">ut</a>-<a href="#entry-han_1" class="skt-link">han_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">2</span>] pr. <i>(uddhanti)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>uddhata</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>upa</i><i>)</i> déraciner, arracher; creuser — pr. md. <i>(uddhante)</i> se pendre, se suicider.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uddhar_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उद्धर्ष</span> <i>uddharṣa</i> [<a href="#entry-uddh_r_s" class="skt-link">uddhṛṣ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-uddhar_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-uddhar_sa" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-uddhar_saa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>uddharṣā</i> joyeux; heureux, satisfait — <a href="#entry-uddhar_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> jaillissement (feu); grande joie <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. festival religieux.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uddhata">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उद्धत</span> <i>uddhata</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-uddhan" class="skt-link">uddhan</a>] a. <a href="#entry-uddhata" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-uddhata" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-uddhataa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>uddhatā</i> élevé, haut; violent, intense <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> fier, vain, enorgueilli; arrogant.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uddhava">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उद्धव</span> <i>uddhava</i> [<a href="#entry-ut" class="skt-link">ut</a>-<a href="#entry-hava" class="skt-link">hava</a>] <a href="#entry-uddhava" class="skt-link">m.</a> feu sacrificiel; festival; joie <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. [BhP.] np. d'Uddhava, cousin, ami d'enfance et confident de <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_na" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇa</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hist. np. de l'ordre religieux [<a href="#entry-sampradaaya" class="skt-link">saṃpradāya</a>] Uddhava_1 fondé par <a href="#entry-Uraamaananda_1" class="skt-link">Rāmānanda_1</a> <a href="#entry-Usvaamii" class="skt-link">Svāmī</a> au Gujarāt au 18⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle; il fut continué par l'ordre fondé par son élève <a href="#entry-Usvaamiinaaraaya_na" class="skt-link">Svāmīnārāyaṇa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uddhavagiitaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>uddhavagītā</i> [<a href="#entry-giitaa" class="skt-link">gītā</a>] <a href="#entry-uddhavagiitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> myth. np. de l'Uddhavagītā «Geste d'<a href="#entry-Uuddhava" class="skt-link">Uddhava</a>», où <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_na" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇa</a> révèle ses dernières paroles à <a href="#entry-Uuddhava" class="skt-link">Uddhava</a> avant de quitter ce monde; elle fait partie du BhP.<br> <a href="#entry-uddhava" class="skt-link">uddhava</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uddhaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उद्धय</span> <i>uddhaya</i> [<a href="#entry-ut" class="skt-link">ut</a>-<a href="#entry-dhaya" class="skt-link">dhaya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-uddhaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-uddhaya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-uddhayaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>uddhayā</i> qui boit d'un trait.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uddhuu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उद्धू</span> <i>uddhū</i> [<a href="#entry-ut" class="skt-link">ut</a>-<a href="#entry-dhuu_1" class="skt-link">dhū_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">5</span>] pr. <i>(uddhunoti)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>uddhūta</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>sam</i><i>)</i> agiter, exciter; soulever, lancer en l'air.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uddi_s_ta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उद्दिष्ट</span> <i>uddiṣṭa</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-uddiz" class="skt-link">uddiś</a>] a. <a href="#entry-uddi_s_ta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-uddi_s_ta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-uddi_s_taa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>uddiṣṭā</i> assigné, prescrit.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uddiip">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उद्दीप्</span> <i>uddīp</i> [<a href="#entry-ut" class="skt-link">ut</a>-<a href="#entry-diip" class="skt-link">dīp</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">4</span>] pr. md. <i>(uddīpyate)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>uddīpta</i><i>)</i> s'enflammer, flamboyer — ca. <i>(uddīpayati)</i> s'allumer, s'enflammer; illuminer <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> provoquer, inciter, exciter.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uddiipana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उद्दीपन</span> <i>uddīpana</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-uddiip" class="skt-link">uddīp</a>] a. <a href="#entry-uddiipana" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-uddiipana" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-uddiipanaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>uddīpanā</i> excitant; affectant violemment (not. poison) — <a href="#entry-uddiipana" class="skt-link">n.</a> acte d'enflammer (not. une passion); incitation, excitation; stimulus, inspiration; circonstance aggravante.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uddiz">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उद्दिश्</span> <i>uddiś</i> [<a href="#entry-ut" class="skt-link">ut</a>-<a href="#entry-diz_1" class="skt-link">diś_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(uddiśati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>uddiṣṭa</i><i>)</i> abs. <i>(</i><i>uddiśya</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>sam</i><i>)</i> désigner, montrer, indiquer; enseigner, expliquer; prescrire.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uddizya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उद्दिश्य</span> <i>uddiśya</i> [abs. <a href="#entry-uddiz" class="skt-link">uddiś</a>] ind. ayant visé, ayant vu, ayant montré — cl. dans la direction de, vers; en vue de, à cause de ⟨acc.⟩.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uddyota">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उद्द्योत</span> <i>uddyota</i> [<a href="#entry-uddyut" class="skt-link">uddyut</a>] <a href="#entry-uddyota" class="skt-link">m.</a> éclair, lumière, radiance — ifc. lit. éclaircissement de, titre d'ouvrages <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gram. np. de l'Uddyota de <a href="#entry-Unaageza" class="skt-link">Nāgeśa</a> <a href="#entry-Ubha_t_ta" class="skt-link">Bhaṭṭa</a>, commentaire du <a href="#entry-Upradiipa" class="skt-link">Pradīpa</a> de <a href="#entry-Ukaiya_ta" class="skt-link">Kaiyaṭa</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-Ubhaa_syapradiipoddyota" class="skt-link">Bhāṣyapradīpoddyota</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uddyotakara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>uddyotakara</i> [<a href="#entry-kara_1" class="skt-link">kara_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-uddyotakara" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-uddyotakara" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-uddyotakaraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>uddyotakarā</i> qui illumine, radieux — <a href="#entry-uddyotakara" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. du logicien <a href="#entry-paazupata" class="skt-link">pāśupata</a> Uddyotakara «Lumineux» (7⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle); il est l'auteur du commentaire <a href="#entry-Unyaayavaarttika" class="skt-link">Nyāyavārttika</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uddyotana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>uddyotana</i> [<a href="#entry--na" class="skt-link">-na</a>] <a href="#entry-uddyotana" class="skt-link">n.</a> illumination <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gram. np. de l'Uddyotana «Élucidation», commentaire du <a href="#entry-Upradiipa" class="skt-link">Pradīpa</a> dû à Annaṃbhaṭṭa (17⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle).<br> <a href="#entry-uddyota" class="skt-link">uddyota</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uddyut">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उद्द्युत्</span> <i>uddyut</i> [<a href="#entry-ut" class="skt-link">ut</a>-<a href="#entry-dyut_1" class="skt-link">dyut_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. md. <i>(uddyotate)</i> briller, resplendir — ca. <i>(uddyotayati)</i> illuminer, irradier <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> élucider, rendre lumineux.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-udejaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उदेजय</span> <i>udejaya</i> [<a href="#entry-ut" class="skt-link">ut</a>-<a href="#entry-ejaya" class="skt-link">ejaya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-udejaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-udejaya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-udejayaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>udejayā</i> qui secoue, qui dérange.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-udg_rr">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उद्गॄ</span> <i>udgṝ</i> [<a href="#entry-ut" class="skt-link">ut</a>-<a href="#entry-g_rr_1" class="skt-link">gṝ_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">6</span>] pr. <i>(udgirati)</i> rejeter, vomir, cracher.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-udgaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उद्गा</span> <i>udgā</i> [<a href="#entry-ut" class="skt-link">ut</a>-<a href="#entry-gaa_2" class="skt-link">gā_2</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(udgāyati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>udgīta</i><i>)</i> commencer à chanter, entonner <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> chanter fort, couvrir de sa voix.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-udgaara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उद्गार</span> <i>udgāra</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-udg_rr" class="skt-link">udgṝ</a>] <a href="#entry-udgaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> rejet du corps; crachat, vomissement <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> fait de vomir, de roter, de cracher, de péter <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> récit ressassé; diarrhée verbale.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-udgaarin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उद्गारिन्</span> <i>udgārin</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-udg_rr" class="skt-link">udgṝ</a>] ifc. a. <a href="#entry-udgaarin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-udgaarin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-udgaari_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>udgāriṇī</i> qui émet, qui éjecte; qui expectore, qui crache, qui vomit <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> qui décharge; qui rote.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-udgaat_r">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उद्गातृ</span> <i>udgātṛ</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-udgaa" class="skt-link">udgā</a>] <a href="#entry-udgaat_r" class="skt-link">m.</a> véd. prêtre-chantre de <a href="#entry-saaman" class="skt-link">sāman</a> dans le rite solennel; ses trois aides sont le <a href="#entry-prastot_r" class="skt-link">prastotṛ</a>, le <a href="#entry-pratihart_r" class="skt-link">pratihartṛ</a>, et le <a href="#entry-subrahma_nya" class="skt-link">subrahmaṇya</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-udgam">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उद्गम्</span> <i>udgam</i> [<a href="#entry-ut" class="skt-link">ut</a>-<a href="#entry-gam" class="skt-link">gam</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(udgacchati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>udgata</i><i>)</i> apparaître, surgir, jaillir; s'élever, s'étendre.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-udgama">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उद्गम</span> <i>udgama</i> [<a href="#entry-udgam" class="skt-link">udgam</a>] <a href="#entry-udgama" class="skt-link">m.</a> apparition, origine, départ; production, déploiement <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> croissance; bourgeon.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-udgata">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उद्गत</span> <i>udgata</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-udgam" class="skt-link">udgam</a>] a. <a href="#entry-udgata" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-udgata" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-udgataa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>udgatā</i> sorti, jailli; apparu, surgi <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> étendu, large.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-udgati">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उद्गति</span> <i>udgati</i> [<a href="#entry-udgam" class="skt-link">udgam</a>] <a href="#entry-udgati" class="skt-link">f.</a> apparition.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-udgha_t">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उद्घट्</span> <i>udghaṭ</i> [<a href="#entry-ut" class="skt-link">ut</a>-<a href="#entry-gha_t" class="skt-link">ghaṭ</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. md. <i>(udghaṭate)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>udghaṭita</i><i>)</i> s'ouvrir — ca. <i>(udghāṭayati)</i> ouvrir; peler.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-udgha_tita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उद्घटित</span> <i>udghaṭita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-udgha_t" class="skt-link">udghaṭ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-udgha_tita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-udgha_tita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-udgha_titaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>udghaṭitā</i> ouvert.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-udghaa_ta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उद्घाट</span> <i>udghāṭa</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-udgha_t" class="skt-link">udghaṭ</a>] <a href="#entry-udghaa_ta" class="skt-link">m.</a> fait d'ouvrir, de montrer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-udghaa_taka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>udghāṭaka</i> [<a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] <a href="#entry-udghaa_taka" class="skt-link">m.</a> clé.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-udghaa_tana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>udghāṭana</i> [<a href="#entry--na" class="skt-link">-na</a>] a. <a href="#entry-udghaa_tana" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-udghaa_tana" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-udghaa_tanaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>udghāṭanā</i> qui ouvre; qui déverrouille — <a href="#entry-udghaa_tana" class="skt-link">n.</a> fait d'ouvrir; fait de révéler.<br> <a href="#entry-udghaa_ta" class="skt-link">udghāṭa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-udghaata">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उद्घात</span> <i>udghāta</i> [<a href="#entry-uddhan" class="skt-link">uddhan</a>] <a href="#entry-udghaata" class="skt-link">m.</a> fait de frapper, de blesser <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> blessure, coup <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> massue, maillet; arme.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-udghu_s">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उद्घुष्</span> <i>udghuṣ</i> [<a href="#entry-ut" class="skt-link">ut</a>-<a href="#entry-ghu_s" class="skt-link">ghuṣ</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(udghoṣati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>udghuṣṭa</i><i>)</i> crier — ca. <i>(udghoṣayati)</i> proclamer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-udgiita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उद्गीत</span> <i>udgīta</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-udgaa" class="skt-link">udgā</a>] a. <a href="#entry-udgiita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-udgiita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-udgiitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>udgītā</i> chanté; annoncé, célébré — <a href="#entry-udgiita" class="skt-link">n.</a> chant.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-udgiitha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उद्गीथ</span> <i>udgītha</i> [<a href="#entry-udgiita" class="skt-link">udgīta</a>] <a href="#entry-udgiitha" class="skt-link">m.</a> véd. chant du <a href="#entry-Usaamaveda" class="skt-link">Sāmaveda</a> par le prêtre <a href="#entry-udgaat_r" class="skt-link">udgātṛ</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. la syllabe <a href="#entry-om" class="skt-link">om</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-udgiiti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उद्गीति</span> <i>udgīti</i> [act. <a href="#entry-udgaa" class="skt-link">udgā</a>] <a href="#entry-udgiiti" class="skt-link">f.</a> véd. chant du <a href="#entry-Usaamaveda" class="skt-link">Sāmaveda</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> chant à voix forte.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-udi">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उदि</span> <i>udi</i> [<a href="#entry-ut" class="skt-link">ut</a>-<a href="#entry-i" class="skt-link">i</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">2</span>] pr. <i>(udeti)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>udita_1</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>abhi</i>, <i>sam</i><i>)</i> s'avancer; se lever (corps célestes), apparaître; s'accroître.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-udiicii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उदीची</span> <i>udīcī</i> [f. <a href="#entry-udac" class="skt-link">udac</a>] <a href="#entry-udiicii" class="skt-link">f.</a> la direction du Nord.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-udiicya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उदीच्य</span> <i>udīcya</i> [<a href="#entry-udiicii" class="skt-link">udīcī</a>] <a href="#entry-udiicya" class="skt-link">m.</a> pays du Nord; pl. ses habitants.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-udiir">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उदीर्</span> <i>udīr</i> [<a href="#entry-ut" class="skt-link">ut</a>-<a href="#entry-iir" class="skt-link">īr</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">2</span>] pr. md. <i>(udīrte)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>udīrita</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>sam</i><i>)</i> s'élancer — ca. <i>(udīrayati)</i> dresser, exciter; envoyer, lancer <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> annoncer, proclamer; glorifier — ps. <i>(udīryate)</i> être lancé <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> passer pour.<br> <i>jayamudīrayet</i> [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] Que soit proclamée la victoire.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-udiir_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उदीर्ण</span> <i>udīrṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-udiir" class="skt-link">udīr</a><a href="#entry--na" class="skt-link">-na</a>] a. <a href="#entry-udiir_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-udiir_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-udiir_naa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>udīrṇā</i> excité; élevé <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> fier <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> proclamé.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-udiirita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उदीरित</span> <i>udīrita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-udiir" class="skt-link">udīr</a>] a. <a href="#entry-udiirita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-udiirita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-udiiritaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>udīritā</i> excité, agité; élevé <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> proclamé; glorifié.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-udita_1">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उदित</span> <i>udita_1</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-udi" class="skt-link">udi</a>] a. <a href="#entry-udita_1" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-udita_1" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-uditaa_1" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>uditā_1</i> émergent; levé; haut; accru; fier.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-udita_2">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उदित</span> <i>udita_2</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-vad" class="skt-link">vad</a>] a. <a href="#entry-udita_2" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-udita_2" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-uditaa_2" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>uditā_2</i> dit, parlé; déclaré <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> qui fait autorité; juste.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-udra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उद्र</span> <i>udra</i> [<a href="#entry-uda" class="skt-link">uda</a>] <a href="#entry-udra" class="skt-link">m.</a> otarie — <a href="#entry-udra" class="skt-link">n.</a> eau.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-udreka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उद्रेक</span> <i>udreka</i> [<a href="#entry-udric" class="skt-link">udric</a>] <a href="#entry-udreka" class="skt-link">m.</a> abondance; excès; superflu <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> supériorité, proéminence; prépondérence, prédominance.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-udric">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उद्रिच्</span> <i>udric</i> [<a href="#entry-ut" class="skt-link">ut</a>-<a href="#entry-ric" class="skt-link">ric</a>] ps. <i>(udricyate)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>udrikta</i><i>)</i> exceller, se distinguer <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> augmenter; abonder en <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> laisser, lâcher, vider; évacuer, relâcher — ca. <i>(udrecayati)</i> améliorer; accroître.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-udrikta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उद्रिक्त</span> <i>udrikta</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-udric" class="skt-link">udric</a>] a. <a href="#entry-udrikta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-udrikta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-udriktaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>udriktā</i> proéminent, qui se distingue <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> accru, augmenté; amélioré; abondant; prédominant <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> en excès; superflu; résidu.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-udriktacetas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>udriktacetas</i> [<a href="#entry-cetas" class="skt-link">cetas</a>] a. <a href="#entry-udriktacetas" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-udriktacetas" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-udriktacetas" class="skt-link">f.</a> aux pensées élevées <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hautain.<br> <a href="#entry-udrikta" class="skt-link">udrikta</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-udriktacitta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>udriktacitta</i> [<a href="#entry-citta" class="skt-link">citta</a>] <a href="#entry-udriktacitta" class="skt-link">n.</a> grande âme, garnd esprit — a. <a href="#entry-udriktacitta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-udriktacitta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-udriktacittaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>udriktacittā</i> fier, arrogant, imbu de soi-même.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-udumbala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उदुम्बल</span> <i>udumbala</i> a. <a href="#entry-udumbala" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-udumbala" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-udumbalaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>udumbalā</i> aux pouvoirs étendus <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> brun.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-udumbara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उदुम्बर</span> <i>udumbara</i> var. <i>uḍumbara</i> <a href="#entry-udumbara" class="skt-link">m.</a> natu. bot. <i>Ficus glometara</i>, figuier Gulār à l'épais feuillage; ses figues [<a href="#entry-sadaaphala" class="skt-link">sadāphala</a>] sont comestibles, au goût délicieux [<a href="#entry-svaadurasa" class="skt-link">svādurasa</a>]; son latex est aphrodisiaque; ses fruits, feuilles, écorce et racines ont de nombreux usages médicinaux; il est associé à la vigueur nutritive et à la fertilité; véd. on en fait un trône sur lequel s'assied un <a href="#entry-vraatya" class="skt-link">vrātya</a> pendant la cérémonie du <a href="#entry-mahaavrata" class="skt-link">mahāvrata</a>; on en fait une coupe pour contenir l'onction du sacre lors de la consécration du <a href="#entry-raajasuuya" class="skt-link">rājasūya</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> variété de lèpre provoquant des lésions rougeâtres <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> verge — <a href="#entry-udumbara" class="skt-link">n.</a> natu. la figue, fruit du bot. <i>Ficus glometara</i> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pl. les fruits des 5 ficus: <a href="#entry-udumbara" class="skt-link">udumbara</a>, <a href="#entry-va_ta" class="skt-link">vaṭa</a>-<a href="#entry-nyagrodha" class="skt-link">nyagrodha</a>, <a href="#entry-pippala" class="skt-link">pippala</a>-<a href="#entry-azvattha" class="skt-link">aśvattha</a>, <a href="#entry-plak_sa" class="skt-link">plakṣa</a> et <a href="#entry-kaakodumbara" class="skt-link">kākodumbara</a>; leur consommation est interdite au <a href="#entry-jaina" class="skt-link">jaina</a>.<br> <i>caran</i> <i>vai</i> <i>madhu</i> <i>vindati</i> <i>caran</i> <i>svādum</i> <i>udumbaram</i> [AB.] C'est en marchant, certes, qu'on obtient le miel, et en marchant encore la douce figue.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-udv_rt">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उद्वृत्</span> <i>udvṛt</i> [<a href="#entry-ut" class="skt-link">ut</a>-<a href="#entry-v_rt_1" class="skt-link">vṛt_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. md. <i>(udvartate)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>udvṛtta</i><i>)</i> déborder, s'élever, grossir (flots); inonder <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> être arrogant.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-udv_rtta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उद्वृत्त</span> <i>udvṛtta</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-udv_rt" class="skt-link">udvṛt</a>] a. <a href="#entry-udv_rtta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-udv_rtta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-udv_rttaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>udvṛttā</i> grossi, agité; qui déborde; déchaîné <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> extravagant; insolent, malpoli.<br> <i>udvṛttagaṅgā</i> le Gange en crue.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-udvaaha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उद्वाह</span> <i>udvāha</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-udvah" class="skt-link">udvah</a>] <a href="#entry-udvaaha" class="skt-link">m.</a> fait d'emmener la nouvelle épouse, mariage.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-udvaapa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उद्वाप</span> <i>udvāpa</i> [<a href="#entry-ut" class="skt-link">ut</a>-<a href="#entry-vaapa_2" class="skt-link">vāpa_2</a>] <a href="#entry-udvaapa" class="skt-link">m.</a> fait de jeter, d'enlever <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> éjection <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. [<a href="#entry-nyaaya" class="skt-link">nyāya</a>] non existence d'une conséquence en l'absence d'une hypothèse <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gram. rejet d'un sens du mot qui précède.<br> <i>āvāpa</i> <i>udvāpa</i> gram. paire constrastante.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-udvah">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उद्वह्</span> <i>udvah</i> [<a href="#entry-ut" class="skt-link">ut</a>-<a href="#entry-vah_1" class="skt-link">vah_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(udvahati)</i> élever, tirer hors de; not. tirer des flèches d'un carquois; épouser <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> apporter; manifester, montrer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-udvaha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उद्वह</span> <i>udvaha</i> [<a href="#entry-udvah" class="skt-link">udvah</a>] a. <a href="#entry-udvaha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-udvaha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-udvahaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>udvahā</i> qui transporte dans les airs; qui emporte au loin <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> éminent, supérieur — <a href="#entry-udvaha" class="skt-link">m.</a> fait d'amener chez soi la nouvelle épouse, mariage <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> fils; progéniture <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. d'Udvaha, l'un des 7 Vents célestes; il supporte les maisons lunaires [<a href="#entry-nak_satra" class="skt-link">nakṣatra</a>] et assure leur rotation.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-udvahana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>udvahana</i> [<a href="#entry--na" class="skt-link">-na</a>] <a href="#entry-udvahana" class="skt-link">n.</a> transport; fait de soulever, d'emporter <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> transport par ⟨i.⟩ <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> mariage <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> arch. support de pilier.<br> <a href="#entry-udvaha" class="skt-link">udvaha</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-udvam">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उद्वम्</span> <i>udvam</i> [<a href="#entry-ut" class="skt-link">ut</a>-<a href="#entry-vam" class="skt-link">vam</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(udvamati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>udvānta</i><i>)</i> vomir, cracher.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-udvega">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उद्वेग</span> <i>udvega</i> [<a href="#entry-udvij" class="skt-link">udvij</a>] <a href="#entry-udvega" class="skt-link">m.</a> excitation, trouble; nervosité; agitation, tremblement; détresse.<br> <i>udvegaṃ</i> <i>kṛ</i> troubler.<br> <i>sodvegam</i> avec nervosité.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-udvigna">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उद्विग्न</span> <i>udvigna</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-udvij" class="skt-link">udvij</a>] a. <a href="#entry-udvigna" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-udvigna" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-udvignaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>udvignā</i> effrayé; anxieux, affligé.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-udvignacitta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>udvignacitta</i> [<a href="#entry-citta" class="skt-link">citta</a>] a. <a href="#entry-udvignacitta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-udvignacitta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-udvignacittaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>udvignacittā</i> anxieux, en peine.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-udvignamanas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>udvignamanas</i> [<a href="#entry-manas" class="skt-link">manas</a>] a. <a href="#entry-udvignamanas" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-udvignamanas" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-udvignamanas" class="skt-link">f.</a> terrifié; anxieux, en peine, en détresse.<br> <a href="#entry-udvigna" class="skt-link">udvigna</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-udviik_s">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उद्वीक्ष्</span> <i>udvīkṣ</i> [<a href="#entry-ut" class="skt-link">ut</a>-<a href="#entry-viik_s" class="skt-link">vīkṣ</a>] pf. <i>(</i><i>sam</i><i>)</i> [inusité sans pf.].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-udvij">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उद्विज्</span> <i>udvij</i> [<a href="#entry-ut" class="skt-link">ut</a>-<a href="#entry-vij" class="skt-link">vij</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">6</span>] pr. md. <i>(udvijate)</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">6</span>] pr. <i>(udvijati)</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">7</span>] pr. <i>(udvinakti)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>udvigna</i><i>)</i> trembler, craindre ⟨abl.⟩ — ca. <i>(udvejayati)</i> effrayer, affliger, terrifier; provoquer du dégoût.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-udya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उद्य</span> <i>udya</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] <a href="#entry-vad" class="skt-link">vad</a>] a. <a href="#entry-udya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-udya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-udyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>udyā</i> qui doit être prononcé; à dire.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-udyaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उद्या</span> <i>udyā</i> [<a href="#entry-ut" class="skt-link">ut</a>-<a href="#entry-yaa_1" class="skt-link">yā_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">2</span>] pr. <i>(udyāti)</i> se lever (Soleil); s'élever; provenir de <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> sortir, partir; partir de <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> exceller; surpasser ⟨acc.⟩.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-udyaana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उद्यान</span> <i>udyāna</i> [act. <a href="#entry-udyaa" class="skt-link">udyā</a>] <a href="#entry-udyaana" class="skt-link">n.</a> élévation <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> sortie <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> (lieu de sortie) jardin, parc, promenade; not. jardin de palais <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> géo. np. de l'Udyāna ou Uḍḍiyāna ou Oḍḍiyāna, pays du Nord de la vallée de l'Indus (mod. vallée du Swat et Cachemire); ce fut un centre de diffusion du bd. (1⟨sup⟩er⟨/sup⟩ siècle), associé au <a href="#entry-Ugandhaara" class="skt-link">Gandhāra</a>; au 11⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle il fut un centre d'enseignement du tantrisme avec <a href="#entry-Utilopa" class="skt-link">Tilopa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-udyam">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उद्यम्</span> <i>udyam</i> [<a href="#entry-ut" class="skt-link">ut</a>-<a href="#entry-yam" class="skt-link">yam</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(udyacchati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>udyata</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>sam</i><i>)</i> lever, élever; entreprendre, commencer <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> s'efforcer de ⟨dat. acc.⟩.<br> <i>udyacchati</i> <i>cikitsāṃ</i> <i>vaidyaḥ</i> le médeçin se consacre à la médecine.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-udyama">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उद्यम</span> <i>udyama</i> [act. <a href="#entry-udyam" class="skt-link">udyam</a>] <a href="#entry-udyama" class="skt-link">m.</a> fait de lever; not. de lever les mains pour se mettre au travail; fait d'entreprendre; entreprise, tentative; effort, zèle.<br> <i>udyamena</i> par zèle.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-udyamabh_rt">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>udyamabhṛt</i> [<a href="#entry-bh_rt" class="skt-link">bhṛt</a>] a. <a href="#entry-udyamabh_rt" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-udyamabh_rt" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-udyamabh_rt" class="skt-link">f.</a> qui fait des efforts; zélé, entreprenant.<br> <a href="#entry-udyama" class="skt-link">udyama</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-udyamana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उद्यमन</span> <i>udyamana</i> [act. <a href="#entry-udyam" class="skt-link">udyam</a>] <a href="#entry-udyamana" class="skt-link">n.</a> fait de s'élever <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> effort, application.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-udyata">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उद्यत</span> <i>udyata</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-udyam" class="skt-link">udyam</a>] a. <a href="#entry-udyata" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-udyata" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-udyataa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>udyatā</i> levé, haut; actif, prêt à agir.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-udyoga">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उद्योग</span> <i>udyoga</i> [<a href="#entry-udyuj" class="skt-link">udyuj</a>] <a href="#entry-udyoga" class="skt-link">m.</a> zèle, effort, diligence; préparation; entreprise.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-udyogaparvan">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>udyogaparvan</i> [<a href="#entry-parvan" class="skt-link">parvan</a>] <a href="#entry-udyogaparvan" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. 5⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ livre du <a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>, «des négociations», contant les préparatifs pour la guerre.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-udyogin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>udyogin</i> [<a href="#entry--in" class="skt-link">-in</a>] a. <a href="#entry-udyogin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-udyogin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-udyoginii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>udyoginī</i> diligent, actif, énergique.<br> <a href="#entry-udyoga" class="skt-link">udyoga</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-udyuj">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उद्युज्</span> <i>udyuj</i> [<a href="#entry-ut" class="skt-link">ut</a>-<a href="#entry-yuj_1" class="skt-link">yuj_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">7</span>] pr. <i>(udyunakti)</i> pr. md. <i>(udyuṅkte)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>udyukta</i><i>)</i> entreprendre, se préparer à, être actif.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-udyukta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उद्युक्त</span> <i>udyukta</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-udyuj" class="skt-link">udyuj</a>] a. <a href="#entry-udyukta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-udyukta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-udyuktaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>udyuktā</i> actif, entreprenant, zélé; prêt, actif.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ugra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उग्र</span> <i>ugra</i> [<a href="#entry-vaj" class="skt-link">vaj</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ugra" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ugra" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ugraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ugrā</i> très fort, puissant; dur, rude <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> terrible, redoutable; violent, impétueux <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> passionné; en colère; furieux <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> cruel, féroce; sauvage <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. se dit des divinités malveillantes; opp. <a href="#entry-prasanna" class="skt-link">prasanna</a> — <a href="#entry-ugra" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. d'Ugra, épith. de <a href="#entry-Urudra" class="skt-link">Rudra</a>-<a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> — <a href="#entry-ugra" class="skt-link">n.</a> rudesse, colère.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ugragandha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ugragandha</i> [<a href="#entry-gandha" class="skt-link">gandha</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ugragandha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ugragandha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ugragandhaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ugragandhā</i> à l'odeur forte; fétide — <a href="#entry-ugragandha" class="skt-link">m.</a> ail — <a href="#entry-ugragandha" class="skt-link">n.</a> asa foetida (poudre de résine de bot. <i>Ferula assafoetida</i> utilisée comme condiment) — <a href="#entry-ugragandhaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ugragandhā</i> racine d'iris; diverses plantes.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ugrasena">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ugrasena</i> [<a href="#entry-senaa" class="skt-link">senā</a>] <a href="#entry-ugrasena" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] np. du prince <a href="#entry-yaadava" class="skt-link">yādava</a> Ugrasena «à la Terrible armée», roi <a href="#entry-v_r_s_ni" class="skt-link">vṛṣṇi</a> régnant à <a href="#entry-Umathuraa" class="skt-link">Mathurā</a>, frère de <a href="#entry-Udevaka" class="skt-link">Devaka</a>; son épouse <a href="#entry-Ukar_nii" class="skt-link">Karṇī</a> était très belle, et un démon se présenta à elle sous les traits d'<a href="#entry-Uugrasena" class="skt-link">Ugrasena</a> pour l'abuser; de cette union naquit <a href="#entry-Uka_msa" class="skt-link">Kaṃsa</a> qui détrôna son père et l'emprisonna; <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_na" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇa</a> lui redonna son royaume après avoir tué <a href="#entry-Uka_msa" class="skt-link">Kaṃsa</a>; les héros <a href="#entry-Ukafka_1" class="skt-link">Kaṅka_1</a>, <a href="#entry-Uzafku_1" class="skt-link">Śaṅku_1</a> et <a href="#entry-Uanaadh_r_s_ti" class="skt-link">Anādhṛṣṭi</a> sont ses fils <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] np. d'Ugrasena_1, épith. de <a href="#entry-Ucitrasena_1" class="skt-link">Citrasena_1</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-ugra" class="skt-link">ugra</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ugrataa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ugratā</i> [<a href="#entry--taa" class="skt-link">-tā</a>] <a href="#entry-ugrataa" class="skt-link">f.</a> état colérique.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ugratama">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ugratama</i> [<a href="#entry--tama" class="skt-link">-tama</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ugratama" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ugratama" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ugratamaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ugratamā</i> super. le plus terrible.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ugratara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ugratara</i> [<a href="#entry--tara" class="skt-link">-tara</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ugratara" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ugratara" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ugrataraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ugratarā</i> compar. plus terrible.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ugrazaanti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ugraśānti</i> [<a href="#entry-zaanti" class="skt-link">śānti</a>] <a href="#entry-ugrazaanti" class="skt-link">f.</a> soc. rite pour éloigner la mort, effectué à l'âge de 60 ans.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ugrazaasana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ugraśāsana</i> [<a href="#entry-zaasana" class="skt-link">śāsana</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ugrazaasana" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ugrazaasana" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ugrazaasanii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ugraśāsanī</i> qui dirige par la terreur — <a href="#entry-ugrazaasana" class="skt-link">m.</a> tyran impitoyable.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ugrazravas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ugraśravas</i> [<a href="#entry-zravas" class="skt-link">śravas</a>] <a href="#entry-ugrazravas" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] np. d'Ugraśravā «à la Terrible renommée», fils du <a href="#entry-suuta_2" class="skt-link">sūta_2</a> <a href="#entry-Ulomahar_sa_na" class="skt-link">Lomaharṣaṇa</a> (et ainsi appelé <a href="#entry-Usauti_2" class="skt-link">Sauti_2</a>); il est le second récitant du <a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a> auprès des brâhmanes réunis pour un <a href="#entry-sattra" class="skt-link">sattra</a> dans la forêt <a href="#entry-Unaimi_sa" class="skt-link">Naimiṣa</a>; [BrP.] son épouse est la fidèle <a href="#entry-Uziilavatii" class="skt-link">Śīlavatī</a>; un jour qu'elle l'emmenait chez une prostituée, il vit <a href="#entry-Umaa_n_davya" class="skt-link">Māṇḍavya</a> empalé par l'ordre du roi, et se moqua de lui; celui-ci le maudit, et proféra qu'il mourrait avant le prochain lever du soleil; <a href="#entry-Uziilavatii" class="skt-link">Śīlavatī</a> proféra en retour que le Soleil ne se lèverait pas le lendemain, ce qui provoqua l'inquiétude des dieux; <a href="#entry-Ubrahmaa" class="skt-link">Brahmā</a>, <a href="#entry-Uvi_s_nu" class="skt-link">Viṣṇu</a> et <a href="#entry-Umahezvara" class="skt-link">Maheśvara</a> furent mandatés par eux, et demandèrent à <a href="#entry-Uanasuuyaa" class="skt-link">Anasūyā</a> d'intercéder auprès de <a href="#entry-Uziilavatii" class="skt-link">Śīlavatī</a>, qui rétracta sa malédiction avec l'assurance qu'<a href="#entry-Uugrazravaa" class="skt-link">Ugraśravā</a> ne mourrait pas; comme récompense de son aide, <a href="#entry-Uanasuuyaa" class="skt-link">Anasūyā</a> demanda que la <a href="#entry-trimuurti" class="skt-link">trimūrti</a> soit réincarnée comme ses enfants <a href="#entry-Ucandra" class="skt-link">Candra</a>, <a href="#entry-Udattaatreya" class="skt-link">Dattātreya</a> et <a href="#entry-Udurvaasaa" class="skt-link">Durvāsā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uhya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उह्य</span> <i>uhya</i> cf. <a href="#entry-vah_1" class="skt-link">vah_1</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uj">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उज्</span> <i>uj</i> cf. <a href="#entry-ut" class="skt-link">ut</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ujj_rmbh">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उज्जृम्भ्</span> <i>ujjṛmbh</i> [<a href="#entry-ut" class="skt-link">ut</a>-<a href="#entry-j_rmbh" class="skt-link">jṛmbh</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. md. <i>(ujjṛmbhate)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>ujjṛmbhita</i><i>)</i> s'ouvrir, s'entrouvrir; apparaître.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ujj_rmbhita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उज्जृम्भित</span> <i>ujjṛmbhita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-ujj_rmbh" class="skt-link">ujjṛmbh</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ujj_rmbhita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ujj_rmbhita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ujj_rmbhitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ujjṛmbhitā</i> entrouvert; apparu.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ujjan">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उज्जन्</span> <i>ujjan</i> [<a href="#entry-ut" class="skt-link">ut</a>-<a href="#entry-jan" class="skt-link">jan</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">4</span>] pr. md. <i>(ujjāyate)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>ujjāta</i><i>)</i> véd. naître; être issu de ⟨abl.⟩.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ujjayin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उज्जयिन्</span> <i>ujjayin</i> [<a href="#entry-ut" class="skt-link">ut</a>-<a href="#entry-jayin" class="skt-link">jayin</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ujjayin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ujjayin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ujjayinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ujjayinī</i> victorieux — f. cf. <a href="#entry-ujjayinii" class="skt-link">ujjayinī</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ujjayinii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उज्जयिनी</span> <i>ujjayinī</i> [f. <a href="#entry-ujjayin" class="skt-link">ujjayin</a>] <a href="#entry-ujjayinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> géo. np. d'Ujjayinī «Victoire», ville sacrée sur la <a href="#entry-Usipraa" class="skt-link">Siprā</a>, capitale du <a href="#entry-Umaalava" class="skt-link">Mālava</a>, d'abord appelée <a href="#entry-Uavantikaa" class="skt-link">Avantikā</a>, au nord de la mod. Ujjain au Madhya Pradesh; elle était un centre artistique renommé sous la dynastie <a href="#entry-Ugupta" class="skt-link">Gupta</a>; elle fut la capitale de <a href="#entry-Uvikramaaditya" class="skt-link">Vikramāditya</a>, qui la conquit par sa victoire sur les <a href="#entry-Uzakaas" class="skt-link">Śakās</a>; myth. on dit que <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> y vainquit les démons [<a href="#entry-asura" class="skt-link">asura</a>] de <a href="#entry-Utripura" class="skt-link">Tripura</a>; c'est une ville de pélerinage, not. de <a href="#entry-kumbhamela" class="skt-link">kumbhamela</a>, et l'une des 7 villes sacrées [<a href="#entry-saptapurii" class="skt-link">saptapurī</a>]; cf. <a href="#entry-Umahaakaalezvara" class="skt-link">Mahākāleśvara</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gr. Οζενε.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ujjh">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">उज्झ्</span> <i>ujjh</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">6</span>] pr. <i>(ujjhati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>ujjhita</i><i>)</i> s'éloigner de, quitter, abandonner; renoncer à, éviter.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ujjhita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उज्झित</span> <i>ujjhita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-ujjh" class="skt-link">ujjh</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ujjhita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ujjhita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ujjhitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ujjhitā</i> laissé, abandonné; déchargé, émis.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ujji">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उज्जि</span> <i>ujji</i> [<a href="#entry-ut" class="skt-link">ut</a>-<a href="#entry-ji" class="skt-link">ji</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(ujjayati)</i> pr. md. <i>(ujjayate)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>ujjita</i><i>)</i> vaincre, conquérir.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ujjighra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उज्जिघ्र</span> <i>ujjighra</i> [<a href="#entry-ut" class="skt-link">ut</a>-<a href="#entry-jighra" class="skt-link">jighra</a>] ifc. a. <a href="#entry-ujjighra" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ujjighra" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ujjighraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ujjighrā</i> qui sent ⟨iic.⟩.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ujjiiv">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उज्जीव्</span> <i>ujjīv</i> [<a href="#entry-ut" class="skt-link">ut</a>-<a href="#entry-jiiv" class="skt-link">jīv</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(ujjīvati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>ujjīvita</i><i>)</i> revivre, revenir à la vie — ca. <i>(ujjīvayati)</i> réanimer, faire revenir à la vie.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ujjiivana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उज्जीवन</span> <i>ujjīvana</i> [act. <a href="#entry-ujjiiv" class="skt-link">ujjīv</a>] <a href="#entry-ujjiivana" class="skt-link">n.</a> renaissance; résurrection.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ujjiivita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उज्जीवित</span> <i>ujjīvita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-ujjiiv" class="skt-link">ujjīv</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ujjiivita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ujjiivita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ujjiivitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ujjīvitā</i> revenu à la vie.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ujjval">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उज्ज्वल्</span> <i>ujjval</i> [<a href="#entry-ut" class="skt-link">ut</a>-<a href="#entry-jval" class="skt-link">jval</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(ujjvalati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>ujjvalita</i><i>)</i> s'enflammer, briller — ca. <i>(ujjvalayati)</i> enflammer, illuminer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ujjvala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उज्ज्वल</span> <i>ujjvala</i> [<a href="#entry-ujjval" class="skt-link">ujjval</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ujjvala" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ujjvala" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ujjvalaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ujjvalā</i> brillant, radieux.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ujjvalataa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ujjvalatā</i> [<a href="#entry--taa" class="skt-link">-tā</a>] <a href="#entry-ujjvalataa" class="skt-link">f.</a> radiance; splendeur, beauté.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ujjvalatva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ujjvalatva</i> [<a href="#entry--tva" class="skt-link">-tva</a>] <a href="#entry-ujjvalatva" class="skt-link">n.</a> radiance; splendeur, beauté.<br> <a href="#entry-ujjvala" class="skt-link">ujjvala</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uk_s">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">उक्ष्</span> <i>ukṣ</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">6</span>] pr. <i>(ukṣati)</i> impft. <i>(aukṣat)</i> aor. [<span class="skt-grammar">5</span>] <i>(aukṣīt)</i> fut. <i>(</i><i>ukṣiṣyati</i><i>)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>ukṣita</i><i>)</i> abs. <i>(</i><i>-ukṣya</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>pra</i><i>)</i> asperger, mouiller; imprégner; uriner; arroser.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uk_san">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उक्षन्</span> <i>ukṣan</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-uk_s" class="skt-link">ukṣ</a>] <a href="#entry-uk_san" class="skt-link">m.</a> taureau (qui imprègne le troupeau) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> bœuf <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> all. auhsa; ang. ox.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ukaara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उकार</span> <i>ukāra</i> [<i>(u)</i>-<a href="#entry-kaara" class="skt-link">kāra</a>] <a href="#entry-ukaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> le son ou la lettre ‘<i>u</i>’.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ukha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उख</span> <i>ukha</i> <a href="#entry-ukha" class="skt-link">m.</a> chaudron, pot — <a href="#entry-ukhaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ukhā</i> soc. terrine rituelle obtenue en mélangeant à la terre de l'eau bouillie avec la résine de l'arbre <a href="#entry-palaaza" class="skt-link">palāśa</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pot, casserole, bouilloire; écuelle.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ukta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उक्त</span> <i>ukta</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-vac" class="skt-link">vac</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ukta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ukta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-uktaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>uktā</i> dit, prononcé, parlé <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> à qui on s'est adressé — <a href="#entry-ukta" class="skt-link">n.</a> mot, phrase.<br> <i>evam</i> <i>ukte</i> ceci dit (locatif absolu).<br> <i>uktaṃ</i> <i>ca</i> et il a été dit.<br> <i>tenedam</i> <i>uktam</i> il a dit ceci.<br> <i>evam</i> <i>ukte</i> <i>'pi</i> en dépit de ce qui vient d'être dit.<br> <i>yat</i> <i>tvayoktaṃ</i> <i>tad</i> <i>asatyam</i> <i>eva</i> ce que tu as dit est sûrement inexact.<br> <i>evam</i> <i>uktā</i> <i>rākṣasena</i> <i>sītā</i> <i>bhītābhavat</i> ainsi adressée par le vampire, <a href="#entry-Usiitaa" class="skt-link">Sītā</a> prit peur.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uktaartha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>uktārtha</i> [<a href="#entry-artha" class="skt-link">artha</a>] <a href="#entry-uktaartha" class="skt-link">m.</a> gram. sens exprimé.<br> <i>uktārthānām</i> <i>aprayogaḥ</i> [<a href="#entry-Uvaakyapadiiya" class="skt-link">Vākyapadīya</a>] Il n'y a pas d'emploi (d'une forme) pour les sens déjà exprimés.<br> <a href="#entry-ukta" class="skt-link">ukta</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uktavat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उक्तवत्</span> <i>uktavat</i> [ppa. <a href="#entry-vac" class="skt-link">vac</a>] a. <a href="#entry-uktavat" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-uktavat" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-uktavatii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>uktavatī</i> qui a dit.<br> <i>uktavān</i> dit-il.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uktha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उक्थ</span> <i>uktha</i> [<a href="#entry-vac" class="skt-link">vac</a>] <a href="#entry-uktha" class="skt-link">n.</a> énonciation, not. dévotionnelle; elle est récitée, en opposition au chant [<a href="#entry-saaman" class="skt-link">sāman</a>] et au murmure [<a href="#entry-yajus" class="skt-link">yajus</a>] <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. récitation rituelle, invocation; hymne de louange — <a href="#entry-ukthaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ukthā</i> phon. mètre de 4 fois une syllabe longue ou deux courtes.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ukthavardhana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ukthavardhana</i> [<a href="#entry-vardhana" class="skt-link">vardhana</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ukthavardhana" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ukthavardhana" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ukthavardhanaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ukthavardhanā</i> se réconfortant, se revigorant (en récitant des louanges).<br> <a href="#entry-uktha" class="skt-link">uktha</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ukti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उक्ति</span> <i>ukti</i> [<a href="#entry-ukta" class="skt-link">ukta</a>] <a href="#entry-ukti" class="skt-link">f.</a> mot, phrase; expression; parole, déclaration.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uktvaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उक्त्वा</span> <i>uktvā</i> [abs. <a href="#entry-vac" class="skt-link">vac</a>] ind. ayant parlé.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ulapa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उलप</span> <i>ulapa</i> [<a href="#entry-val" class="skt-link">val</a>] <a href="#entry-ulapa" class="skt-link">m.</a> natu. variété d'herbe tendre — <a href="#entry-ulapaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ulapā</i> id.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ulba">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उल्ब</span> <i>ulba</i> var. <i>ulva</i> [<a href="#entry-v_r_1" class="skt-link">vṛ_1</a>] <a href="#entry-ulba" class="skt-link">n.</a> enveloppe; not. membrane entourant l'embryon <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lat. <i>volva</i>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ulba_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उल्बण</span> <i>ulbaṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-ulba" class="skt-link">ulba</a><a href="#entry--na" class="skt-link">-na</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ulba_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ulba_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ulba_naa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ulbaṇā</i> abondant, massif, épais; gros; grossier, noueux, grumeleux — ifc. abondamment muni de, foisonnant de.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ulkaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उल्का</span> <i>ulkā</i> [<a href="#entry-u_s" class="skt-link">uṣ</a>] <a href="#entry-ulkaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> météore; nuée ardente <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> torche; feu de paille <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> astr. l'un des 8 aspects de corps célestes utilisés pour décrire une configuration de naissance [<a href="#entry-dazaa" class="skt-link">daśā</a>] en astrologie prédictive [<a href="#entry-jyoti_sa" class="skt-link">jyotiṣa</a>] <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. [tantr.] np. de Ulkā «Astrale», l'une des 8 puissances divines [<a href="#entry-yoginii" class="skt-link">yoginī</a>].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ullaasa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उल्लास</span> <i>ullāsa</i> [<a href="#entry-ullas" class="skt-link">ullas</a>] <a href="#entry-ullaasa" class="skt-link">m.</a> lumière, splendeur; apparition <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> plaisir; joie, bonheur; prospérité <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit. rhét. mise en valeur par comparaison <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit. division d'un ouvrage.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ullafg">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उल्लङ्ग्</span> <i>ullaṅg</i> [<a href="#entry-ut" class="skt-link">ut</a>-<a href="#entry-lafg" class="skt-link">laṅg</a>] ca. <i>(ullaṅgayati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>ullaṅgita</i><i>)</i> abs. <i>(</i><i>ullaṅgya</i><i>)</i> sauter par dessus, enjamber; transgressser.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ullamphana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उल्लम्फन</span> <i>ullamphana</i> [<a href="#entry-ut" class="skt-link">ut</a>-<a href="#entry-lamphana" class="skt-link">lamphana</a>] <a href="#entry-ullamphana" class="skt-link">n.</a> saut
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ullas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उल्लस्</span> <i>ullas</i> [<a href="#entry-ut" class="skt-link">ut</a>-<a href="#entry-las" class="skt-link">las</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(ullasati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>ullasita</i><i>)</i> briller, irradier; se manifester, apparaître <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> danser; jouer, être joyeux <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> s'agiter, trembler, sautiller — ca. <i>(ullāsayati)</i> faire briller; divertir, amuser <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> faire danser, agiter.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ullasita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उल्लसित</span> <i>ullasita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-ullas" class="skt-link">ullas</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ullasita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ullasita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ullasitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ullasitā</i> brillant; manifeste; tirée (épée).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ullikh">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उल्लिख्</span> <i>ullikh</i> [<a href="#entry-ut" class="skt-link">ut</a>-<a href="#entry-likh" class="skt-link">likh</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">6</span>] pr. <i>(ullikhati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>ullikhita</i><i>)</i> inciser, graver; gratter; ciseler <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> tailler, polir; élucider, faire apparaître.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ullikhita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उल्लिखित</span> <i>ullikhita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-ullikh" class="skt-link">ullikh</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ullikhita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ullikhita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ullikhitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ullikhitā</i> incisé; ciselé; gratté, poli, taillé.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uluuka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उलूक</span> <i>ulūka</i> <a href="#entry-uluuka" class="skt-link">m.</a> natu. hibou <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] np. d'Ulūka «Oiseau de malheur», fils de <a href="#entry-Uzakuni" class="skt-link">Śakuni</a>, envoyé par <a href="#entry-Uduryodhana" class="skt-link">Duryodhana</a> pour provoquer les <a href="#entry-paa_n_davaas" class="skt-link">pāṇḍavās</a> à la guerre.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uluukhala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उलूखल</span> <i>ulūkhala</i> <a href="#entry-uluukhala" class="skt-link">n.</a> mortier (à piler le riz); bol en bois <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> véd. mortier à préparer le <a href="#entry-soma" class="skt-link">soma</a>.<br> <i>avahananāaya</i> <i>ulūkhala</i> mortier à battre (le riz).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uluupa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उलूप</span> <i>ulūpa</i> <a href="#entry-uluupa" class="skt-link">m.</a> cf. <a href="#entry-ulapa" class="skt-link">ulapa</a> — <a href="#entry-uluupii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ulūpī</i> myth. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] np. d'Ulūpī, fille du roi <a href="#entry-naaga" class="skt-link">nāga</a> <a href="#entry-Ukauravya" class="skt-link">Kauravya</a>; lors de son exil, <a href="#entry-Uarjuna" class="skt-link">Arjuna</a> l'épousa, et elle lui donna le pouvoir de vaincre les créatures aquatiques; <a href="#entry-Uiraavaan" class="skt-link">Irāvān</a> est leur fils; lorsque <a href="#entry-Ubabhruvaahana" class="skt-link">Babhruvāhana</a> tua son père <a href="#entry-Uarjuna" class="skt-link">Arjuna</a> qui l'avait mis au défi de s'opposer au sacrifice du cheval de <a href="#entry-Uyudhi_s_thira" class="skt-link">Yudhiṣṭhira</a>, elle ressuscita celui-ci grâce à un joyau magique.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-umaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उमा</span> <i>umā</i> <a href="#entry-umaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> myth. np. d'Umā «Lumière», épith. de <a href="#entry-Udurgaa" class="skt-link">Durgā</a>-<a href="#entry-Upaarvatii" class="skt-link">Pārvatī</a>, épouse de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a>-<a href="#entry-Umahezvara" class="skt-link">Maheśvara</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-umaamahezvara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>umāmaheśvara</i> [<a href="#entry-mahezvara" class="skt-link">maheśvara</a>] <a href="#entry-umaamahezvara" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. représentation iconographique d'<a href="#entry-Uumaa" class="skt-link">Umā</a> enlacée avec <a href="#entry-Umahezvara" class="skt-link">Maheśvara</a>, symbolisant la dissolution de la dualité issue de l'ignorance.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-umaapati">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>umāpati</i> [<a href="#entry-pati" class="skt-link">pati</a>] <a href="#entry-umaapati" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. d'Umāpati, épith. de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> «époux d'<a href="#entry-Uumaa" class="skt-link">Umā</a>».
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-umaasa_mhitaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>umāsaṃhitā</i> [<a href="#entry-sa_mhitaa" class="skt-link">saṃhitā</a>] <a href="#entry-umaasa_mhitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> lit. np. de l'Umāsaṃhitā, section du <a href="#entry-Uzivapuraa_na" class="skt-link">Śivapurāṇa</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-Upa_ncacuu_daa" class="skt-link">Pañcacūḍā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-umaasahaaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>umāsahāya</i> [<a href="#entry-sahaaya" class="skt-link">sahāya</a>] <a href="#entry-umaasahaaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. d'Umāsahāya, épith. de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> «Compagnon d'<a href="#entry-Uumaa" class="skt-link">Umā</a>».
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-umaasuta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>umāsuta</i> [<a href="#entry-suta_2" class="skt-link">suta_2</a>] <a href="#entry-umaasuta" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. d'Umāsuta, épith. de <a href="#entry-Uskanda" class="skt-link">Skanda</a> «Fils d'<a href="#entry-Uumaa" class="skt-link">Umā</a>».
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-umbh">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">उम्भ्</span> <i>umbh</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">9</span>] pr. <i>(ubhnāti)</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">6</span>] pr. <i>(umbhati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>ubdha</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>apa</i><i>)</i> unir; accoupler <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> couvrir; confiner.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-umeza">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>umeśa</i> [<a href="#entry-iiza" class="skt-link">īśa</a>] <a href="#entry-umeza" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. d'Umeśa, épith. de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> «Seigneur d'<a href="#entry-Uumaa" class="skt-link">Umā</a>» <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> image représentant <a href="#entry-Uumaa" class="skt-link">Umā</a> et <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> ensemble.<br> <a href="#entry-umaa" class="skt-link">umā</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-und">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">उन्द्</span> <i>und</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">7</span>] pr. <i>(unatti)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>unna</i>, <i>utta</i><i>)</i> mouiller, arroser; jaillir, couler; rendre humide, baigner <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lat. <i>unda</i>; slave voda; ang. water; all. Wasser; fr. onde.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-unmaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उन्मा</span> <i>unmā</i> [<a href="#entry-ut" class="skt-link">ut</a>-<a href="#entry-maa_1" class="skt-link">mā_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">3</span>] pr. md. <i>(unmimīte)</i> mesurer la hauteur.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-unmaada">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उन्माद</span> <i>unmāda</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-unmad" class="skt-link">unmad</a>] <a href="#entry-unmaada" class="skt-link">m.</a> enivrement, affolement; folie, ivresse, passion.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-unmaana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उन्मान</span> <i>unmāna</i> [<a href="#entry-unmaa" class="skt-link">unmā</a><a href="#entry--na" class="skt-link">-na</a>] <a href="#entry-unmaana" class="skt-link">n.</a> altitude, hauteur <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> épaisseur.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-unmaarga">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उन्मार्ग</span> <i>unmārga</i> [<a href="#entry-ut" class="skt-link">ut</a>-<a href="#entry-maarga" class="skt-link">mārga</a>] <a href="#entry-unmaarga" class="skt-link">m.</a> chemin détourné, mauvaise voie <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> comportement déviant — a. <a href="#entry-unmaarga" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-unmaarga" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-unmaargaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>unmārgā</i> qui se trompe de chemin.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-unmaargagaamin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>unmārgagāmin</i> [<a href="#entry-gaamin" class="skt-link">gāmin</a>] a. <a href="#entry-unmaargagaamin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-unmaargagaamin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-unmaargagaaminii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>unmārgagāminī</i> dévoyé; malfaisant.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-unmaargav_rtti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>unmārgavṛtti</i> [<a href="#entry-v_rtti" class="skt-link">vṛtti</a>] a. <a href="#entry-unmaargav_rtti" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-unmaargav_rtti" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-unmaargav_rtti" class="skt-link">f.</a> dévoyé, qui se conduit mal; rebelle.<br> <a href="#entry-unmaarga" class="skt-link">unmārga</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-unmad">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उन्मद्</span> <i>unmad</i> [<a href="#entry-ut" class="skt-link">ut</a>-<a href="#entry-mad_1" class="skt-link">mad_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">4</span>] pr. <i>(unmādyati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>unmatta</i><i>)</i> devenir fou, s'affoler; s'enivrer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-unmada">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उन्मद</span> <i>unmada</i> [<a href="#entry-unmad" class="skt-link">unmad</a>] a. <a href="#entry-unmada" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-unmada" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-unmadaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>unmadā</i> fou, furieux <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> qui intoxique, qui rend fou — <a href="#entry-unmada" class="skt-link">m.</a> folie, démence.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-unmanas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उन्मनस्</span> <i>unmanas</i> [<a href="#entry-ut" class="skt-link">ut</a>-<a href="#entry-manas" class="skt-link">manas</a>] a. <a href="#entry-unmanas" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-unmanas" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-unmanas" class="skt-link">f.</a> perplexe; agité, excité; désirant violemment — v. [<span class="skt-grammar">11</span>] pr. md. <i>(unmanāyate)</i> prendre intérêt à.<br> <i>unmanīkṛ</i> exciter, mettre hors de soi.<br> <i>unmanībhū</i> se faire du souci, s'inquiéter.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-unmanaska">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उन्मनस्क</span> <i>unmanaska</i> [<a href="#entry-unmanas" class="skt-link">unmanas</a><a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] a. <a href="#entry-unmanaska" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-unmanaska" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-unmanaskaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>unmanaskā</i> agité.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-unmanaskataa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>unmanaskatā</i> [<a href="#entry--taa" class="skt-link">-tā</a>] <a href="#entry-unmanaskataa" class="skt-link">f.</a> perplexité.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-unmanaskatva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>unmanaskatva</i> [<a href="#entry--tva" class="skt-link">-tva</a>] <a href="#entry-unmanaskatva" class="skt-link">n.</a> trouble d'esprit.<br> <a href="#entry-unmanaska" class="skt-link">unmanaska</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-unmatta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उन्मत्त</span> <i>unmatta</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-unmad" class="skt-link">unmad</a>] a. <a href="#entry-unmatta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-unmatta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-unmattaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>unmattā</i> égaré, affolé; fou, dément; enivré, ivre.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-unmattadarzana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>unmattadarśana</i> [<a href="#entry-darzana" class="skt-link">darśana</a>] a. <a href="#entry-unmattadarzana" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-unmattadarzana" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-unmattadarzanaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>unmattadarśanā</i> au regard affolé.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-unmattavrata">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>unmattavrata</i> [<a href="#entry-vrata" class="skt-link">vrata</a>] <a href="#entry-unmattavrata" class="skt-link">n.</a> soc. «vœu de démence» de sectes tantriques.<br> <a href="#entry-unmatta" class="skt-link">unmatta</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-unme_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उन्मेष</span> <i>unmeṣa</i> [<a href="#entry-unmi_s" class="skt-link">unmiṣ</a>] <a href="#entry-unme_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> fait d'ouvrir les yeux; fait de regarder <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> fait de fleurir (fleur) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> apparition, développement.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-unme_sa_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>unmeṣaṇa</i> [<a href="#entry--na" class="skt-link">-na</a>] <a href="#entry-unme_sa_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> développement; apparition; parution.<br> <a href="#entry-unme_sa" class="skt-link">unmeṣa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-unmi_s">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उन्मिष्</span> <i>unmiṣ</i> [<a href="#entry-ut" class="skt-link">ut</a>-<a href="#entry-mi_s" class="skt-link">miṣ</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">6</span>] pr. <i>(unmiṣati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>unmiṣita</i><i>)</i> ouvrir les yeux; soulever les paupières <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> s'ouvrir (yeux, bourgeons); naître, se développer; devenir brillant.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-unmi_sita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उन्मिषित</span> <i>unmiṣita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-unmi_s" class="skt-link">unmiṣ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-unmi_sita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-unmi_sita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-unmi_sitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>unmiṣitā</i> ouvert (œil, fleur); souriant (visage) — <a href="#entry-unmi_sita" class="skt-link">n.</a> fait d'ouvrir les yeux.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-unmiil">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उन्मील्</span> <i>unmīl</i> [<a href="#entry-ut" class="skt-link">ut</a>-<a href="#entry-miil" class="skt-link">mīl</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(unmīlati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>unmīlita</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>sam</i><i>)</i> ouvrir les yeux, cligner; se fermer (yeux, fleurs).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-unmiilana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उन्मीलन</span> <i>unmīlana</i> [act. <a href="#entry-unmiil" class="skt-link">unmīl</a>] <a href="#entry-unmiilana" class="skt-link">n.</a> ouverture des yeux; not. lors de la cérémonie de consécration d'une idole.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-unna">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उन्न</span> <i>unna</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-und" class="skt-link">und</a>] a. <a href="#entry-unna" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-unna" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-unnaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>unnā</i> mouillé, humide.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-unnaddha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उन्नद्ध</span> <i>unnaddha</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-unnah" class="skt-link">unnah</a>] a. <a href="#entry-unnaddha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-unnaddha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-unnaddhaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>unnaddhā</i> lié <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> délié; illimité; démesuré, excessif; arrogant.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-unnah">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उन्नह्</span> <i>unnah</i> [<a href="#entry-ut" class="skt-link">ut</a>-<a href="#entry-nah" class="skt-link">nah</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">4</span>] pr. <i>(unnahyati)</i> pr. md. <i>(unnahyate)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>unnaddha</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>sam</i><i>)</i> lier, ceindre <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> se libérer de ses liens, s'échapper.
  </div>
</div>
