<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-unnam">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उन्नम्</span> <i>unnam</i> [<a href="#entry-ut" class="skt-link">ut</a>-<a href="#entry-nam" class="skt-link">nam</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(unnamati)</i> pr. md. <i>(unnamate)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>unnata</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>sam</i><i>)</i> se lever, se dresser — ca. <i>(unnamayati)</i> ériger, dresser.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-unnata">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उन्नत</span> <i>unnata</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-unnam" class="skt-link">unnam</a>] a. <a href="#entry-unnata" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-unnata" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-unnataa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>unnatā</i> élevé, haut, grand; noble.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-unnati">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उन्नति</span> <i>unnati</i> [act. <a href="#entry-unnam" class="skt-link">unnam</a>] <a href="#entry-unnati" class="skt-link">f.</a> élévation; prospérité, grandeur <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. d'Unnati «Prospérité», fille de <a href="#entry-Udak_sa" class="skt-link">Dakṣa</a>, une des épouses de <a href="#entry-Udharma" class="skt-link">Dharma</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-unnaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उन्नय</span> <i>unnaya</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-unnii" class="skt-link">unnī</a>] <a href="#entry-unnaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> levage.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-unnayana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उन्नयन</span> <i>unnayana</i> [<a href="#entry-unnaya" class="skt-link">unnaya</a><a href="#entry--na" class="skt-link">-na</a>] <a href="#entry-unnayana" class="skt-link">n.</a> fait de lever <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> partition des cheveux, not. rituelle <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. inférence.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-unnidra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उन्निद्र</span> <i>unnidra</i> [<a href="#entry-ut" class="skt-link">ut</a>-<a href="#entry-nidraa_1" class="skt-link">nidrā_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-unnidra" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-unnidra" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-unnidraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>unnidrā</i> réveillé, éveillé; qui renonce au sommeil <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> ouvert (fleur).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-unnii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उन्नी</span> <i>unnī</i> [<a href="#entry-ut" class="skt-link">ut</a>-<a href="#entry-nii_1" class="skt-link">nī_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(unnayati)</i> lever, dresser; mettre au-dessus <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> séparer; déceler; inférer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-unniiya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उन्नीय</span> <i>unnīya</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] <a href="#entry-unnii" class="skt-link">unnī</a>] a. <a href="#entry-unniiya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-unniiya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-unniiyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>unnīyā</i> à conduire en haut
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उप</span> <i>upa</i> prép. près de, vers ⟨acc.⟩; sous; dans ⟨loc.⟩; opp. <a href="#entry-apa" class="skt-link">apa</a> — pf. près, auprès, vers, proche, contigu; subordonné <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pf. d'hommage <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pf. de nom de cadet, junior <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gr. υπο; lat. <i>sub</i>.<br> <i>upo</i> [<a href="#entry-u" class="skt-link">u</a>] adv. (<a href="#entry-prag_rhya" class="skt-link">pragṛhya</a>) et vers.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upaa_mzu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपांशु</span> <i>upāṃśu</i> [<a href="#entry-upa" class="skt-link">upa</a>-<a href="#entry-a_mzu" class="skt-link">aṃśu</a>] adv. [«en attendant le <a href="#entry-soma" class="skt-link">soma</a>»] à voix basse <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> en secret — <a href="#entry-upaa_mzu" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. prière murmurée (dans la liturgie véd., avant d'amener le <a href="#entry-soma" class="skt-link">soma</a>).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upaadaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपादा</span> <i>upādā</i> [<a href="#entry-upa" class="skt-link">upa</a>-<a href="#entry-aadaa" class="skt-link">ādā</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">3</span>] pr. md. <i>(upādadate)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>upātta</i><i>)</i> pfp. <i>(</i><i>upādeya</i><i>)</i> abs. <i>(</i><i>upādāya</i><i>)</i> recevoir, accepter; saisir, prendre.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upaadaana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपादान</span> <i>upādāna</i> [act. <a href="#entry-upaadaa" class="skt-link">upādā</a>] <a href="#entry-upaadaana" class="skt-link">n.</a> fait de s'approprier; acquisition <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> bd. l'attachement, 9⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ cause de souffrance [<a href="#entry-pratiityasamutpaada" class="skt-link">pratītyasamutpāda</a>] <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. [<a href="#entry-nyaaya" class="skt-link">nyāya</a>] cause matérielle (opp. <a href="#entry-nimitta" class="skt-link">nimitta</a>, cause efficace) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. le prélude au don, un <a href="#entry-pa_ncopaasana" class="skt-link">pañcopāsana</a> selon <a href="#entry-Uraamaanuja" class="skt-link">Rāmānuja</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upaadaanalak_sa_naa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>upādānalakṣaṇā</i> [<a href="#entry-lak_sa_naa" class="skt-link">lakṣaṇā</a>] <a href="#entry-upaadaanalak_sa_naa" class="skt-link">f.</a> gram. rhét. signification figurative «avec inclusion».
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upaadaanopaadeya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>upādānopādeya</i> [<a href="#entry-upaadeya" class="skt-link">upādeya</a>] <a href="#entry-upaadaanopaadeya" class="skt-link">m.</a> phil. [<a href="#entry-nyaaya" class="skt-link">nyāya</a>] la cause et l'effet.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upaadaanopaadeyabhaava">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>upādānopādeyabhāva</i> [<a href="#entry-bhaava" class="skt-link">bhāva</a>] <a href="#entry-upaadaanopaadeyabhaava" class="skt-link">m.</a> phil. [<a href="#entry-nyaaya" class="skt-link">nyāya</a>] relation de cause à effet.<br> <a href="#entry-upaadaanopaadeya" class="skt-link">upādānopādeya</a> <br> <a href="#entry-upaadaana" class="skt-link">upādāna</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upaadaaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपादाय</span> <i>upādāya</i> [abs. <a href="#entry-upaadaa" class="skt-link">upādā</a>] ind. ayant pris; ayant reçu — prép. y compris; grâce à ⟨acc.⟩.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upaadeya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपादेय</span> <i>upādeya</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] <a href="#entry-upaadaa" class="skt-link">upādā</a>] a. <a href="#entry-upaadeya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-upaadeya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-upaadeyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>upādeyā</i> qui doit être pris; nécessaire — <a href="#entry-upaadeya" class="skt-link">n.</a> phil. [<a href="#entry-nyaaya" class="skt-link">nyāya</a>] l'effet.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upaadhaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपाधा</span> <i>upādhā</i> [<a href="#entry-upa" class="skt-link">upa</a>-<a href="#entry-aadhaa" class="skt-link">ādhā</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">3</span>] pr. <i>(upādadhāti)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>upāhita</i><i>)</i> placer sur — pr. md. <i>(upādhatte)</i> saisir; prendre; séduire (une femme).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upaadhi">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपाधि</span> <i>upādhi</i> [act. <a href="#entry-upaadhaa" class="skt-link">upādhā</a>] <a href="#entry-upaadhi" class="skt-link">m.</a> substitut, substitution; condition, postulat; attribut, appellation; titre <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> apparence, déguisement; tromperie <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. condition contingente de l'être <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. [<a href="#entry-nyaaya" class="skt-link">nyāya</a>] propriété imposée, qualification particulière; opp. <a href="#entry-jaati" class="skt-link">jāti</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upaadhii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपाधी</span> <i>upādhī</i> [<a href="#entry-upa" class="skt-link">upa</a>-<a href="#entry-adhii" class="skt-link">adhī</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">2</span>] pr. <i>(upādhyeti)</i> pr. md. <i>(upādhīte)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>upādhīta</i><i>)</i> répéter, lire; apprendre.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upaadhyaaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपाध्याय</span> <i>upādhyāya</i> [act. <a href="#entry-upaadhii" class="skt-link">upādhī</a>] <a href="#entry-upaadhyaaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> maître, précepteur, directeur spirituel — ifc. titre de lettré, formant des noms propres.<br> <i>upādhyāyāt</i> <i>purāṇaṃ</i> <i>śṛṇoti</i> Il écoute le <a href="#entry-puraa_na" class="skt-link">purāṇa</a> de la bouche du maître.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upaafga">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपाङ्ग</span> <i>upāṅga</i> [<a href="#entry-upa" class="skt-link">upa</a>-<a href="#entry-afga_1" class="skt-link">aṅga_1</a>] <a href="#entry-upaafga" class="skt-link">n.</a> subdivision; complément, annexe; partie auxiliaire d'un savoir <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit. littérature auxiliaire du <a href="#entry-Uveda" class="skt-link">Veda</a>; not. légendes mythologiques [<a href="#entry-puraa_na" class="skt-link">purāṇa</a>], textes de lois [<a href="#entry-dharmazaastra" class="skt-link">dharmaśāstra</a>], traités annexes [<a href="#entry-vedaafga" class="skt-link">vedāṅga</a>] <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> jn. classe de textes sacrés jaïns.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upaagam">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपागम्</span> <i>upāgam</i> [<a href="#entry-upa" class="skt-link">upa</a>-<a href="#entry-aagam" class="skt-link">āgam</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(upāgacchati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>upāgata</i><i>)</i> s'approcher, venir vers <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> revenir, retourner <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> être sujet à; échoir, se produire.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upaagama">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपागम</span> <i>upāgama</i> [<a href="#entry-upa" class="skt-link">upa</a>-<a href="#entry-aagama" class="skt-link">āgama</a>] <a href="#entry-upaagama" class="skt-link">m.</a> arrivée; approche; occurrence <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> accord.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upaagni">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपाग्नि</span> <i>upāgni</i> [<a href="#entry-upa" class="skt-link">upa</a>-<a href="#entry-agni" class="skt-link">agni</a>] ind. sur le feu; près du feu.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upaak_r">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपाकृ</span> <i>upākṛ</i> [<a href="#entry-upa" class="skt-link">upa</a>-<a href="#entry-aak_r" class="skt-link">ākṛ</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">8</span>] pr. <i>(upākaroti)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>upākṛta</i><i>)</i> donner, transmettre; se procurer, obtenir <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> préparer (not. une cérémonie sacrée); entreprendre.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upaakara_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपाकरण</span> <i>upākaraṇa</i> [act. <a href="#entry-upaak_r" class="skt-link">upākṛ</a>] <a href="#entry-upaakara_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> mise en train, commencement <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. début de la lecture du <a href="#entry-Uveda" class="skt-link">Veda</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upaakarman">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपाकर्मन्</span> <i>upākarman</i> [<a href="#entry-upaak_r" class="skt-link">upākṛ</a><a href="#entry--man" class="skt-link">-man</a>] <a href="#entry-upaakarman" class="skt-link">n.</a> soc. début des études védiques; cf. <a href="#entry-utsarjana" class="skt-link">utsarjana</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upaakhyaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपाख्या</span> <i>upākhyā</i> [<a href="#entry-upa" class="skt-link">upa</a>-<a href="#entry-aakhyaa_2" class="skt-link">ākhyā_2</a>] <a href="#entry-upaakhyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> surnom.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upaakhyaana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपाख्यान</span> <i>upākhyāna</i> [<a href="#entry-upa" class="skt-link">upa</a>-<a href="#entry-aakhyaana" class="skt-link">ākhyāna</a>] <a href="#entry-upaakhyaana" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. court récit auxiliaire, épisode.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upaalabh">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपालभ्</span> <i>upālabh</i> [<a href="#entry-upa" class="skt-link">upa</a>-<a href="#entry-aalabh" class="skt-link">ālabh</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. md. <i>(upālabhate)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>upālabdha</i><i>)</i> réprimander, blâmer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upaalambha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपालम्भ</span> <i>upālambha</i> [<a href="#entry-upaalabh" class="skt-link">upālabh</a>] <a href="#entry-upaalambha" class="skt-link">m.</a> reproche, blâme, réprimande.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upaalambhana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपालम्भन</span> <i>upālambhana</i> [<a href="#entry--na" class="skt-link">-na</a>] <a href="#entry-upaalambhana" class="skt-link">n.</a> reproche; censure.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upaali">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपालि</span> <i>upāli</i> <a href="#entry-upaali" class="skt-link">m.</a> bd. np. d'Upāli, disciple principal de <a href="#entry-Ubuddha" class="skt-link">Buddha</a>; il récita les règles monastiques [<a href="#entry-vinaya" class="skt-link">vinaya</a>] lors du premier concile bd. de Rājagaha, 3 mois après son <a href="#entry-parinirvaa_na" class="skt-link">parinirvāṇa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upaanah">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपानह्</span> <i>upānah</i> [<a href="#entry-upanah" class="skt-link">upanah</a>] <a href="#entry-upaanah" class="skt-link">f.</a> sandale; chaussure — <a href="#entry-upaanaha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <i>upānaha</i> ifc. id.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upaanii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपानी</span> <i>upānī</i> [<a href="#entry-upa" class="skt-link">upa</a>-<a href="#entry-aanii" class="skt-link">ānī</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(upānayati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>upānīta</i><i>)</i> amener à proximité; rapprocher; introduire <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> écarter, emporter.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upaanta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपान्त</span> <i>upānta</i> [<a href="#entry-upa" class="skt-link">upa</a>-<a href="#entry-anta" class="skt-link">anta</a>] a. <a href="#entry-upaanta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-upaanta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-upaantaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>upāntā</i> près de la fin; avant-dernier — <a href="#entry-upaanta" class="skt-link">n.</a> [«proximité de la limite»] voisinage, bord.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upaar">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपार्</span> <i>upār</i> [<a href="#entry-upa" class="skt-link">upa</a>-<a href="#entry-_r" class="skt-link">ṛ</a>] pft. <i>(upāra)</i> s'approcher de; se hâter vers <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> faire une erreur; transgresser.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upaara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपार</span> <i>upāra</i> [<a href="#entry-upaar" class="skt-link">upār</a>] <a href="#entry-upaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> transgression; péché.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upaarj">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपार्ज्</span> <i>upārj</i> [<a href="#entry-upa" class="skt-link">upa</a>-<a href="#entry-_rj" class="skt-link">ṛj</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(upārjati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>upārjita</i><i>)</i> ca. <i>(upārjayati)</i> procurer; acquérir, obtenir, gagner.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upaarjita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपार्जित</span> <i>upārjita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-upaarj" class="skt-link">upārj</a>] a. <a href="#entry-upaarjita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-upaarjita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-upaarjitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>upārjitā</i> obtenu, gagné.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upaas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपास्</span> <i>upās</i> [<a href="#entry-upa" class="skt-link">upa</a>-<a href="#entry-aas_2" class="skt-link">ās_2</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">2</span>] pr. md. <i>(upāste)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>upāsita</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>pari</i><i>)</i> être assis auprès de; honorer, servir, se dévouer à <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> être en attente (not. d'instructions); s'attendre à <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> se soumettre à; endurer, subir <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> s'approcher de, entrer dans l'état de; considérer, savoir, connaître.<br> <i>andhaṃ</i> <i>tamaḥ</i> <i>praviśanti</i> <i>ye'sambhūtimupāsate</i> [IU.] Ceux qui adorent le Non-manifesté entrent dans les ténèbres qui rendent aveugle.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upaasaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपासक</span> <i>upāsaka</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-upaas" class="skt-link">upās</a>] a. <a href="#entry-upaasaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-upaasaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-upaasikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>upāsikā</i> serviteur <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> adepte; fidèle, disciple <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> occupé de; ayant l'intention de <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> bd. disciple laïc du <a href="#entry-Ubuddha" class="skt-link">Buddha</a>; opp. <a href="#entry-bhik_su" class="skt-link">bhikṣu</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upaasana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपासन</span> <i>upāsana</i> [act. <a href="#entry-upaas" class="skt-link">upās</a>] <a href="#entry-upaasana" class="skt-link">n.</a> hommage, respect; soumission à; adoration, dévotion, service <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. rite d'adoration d'une divinité, not de son <a href="#entry-i_s_tadevataa" class="skt-link">iṣṭadevatā</a>; <a href="#entry-Uraamaanuja" class="skt-link">Rāmānuja</a> l'a codifié en 5 étapes [<a href="#entry-pa_ncopaasana" class="skt-link">pañcopāsana</a>] — ifc. connaissance de — <a href="#entry-upaasanaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>upāsanā</i> id.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upaasita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपासित</span> <i>upāsita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-upaas" class="skt-link">upās</a>] a. <a href="#entry-upaasita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-upaasita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-upaasitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>upāsitā</i> servi, honoré, adoré.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upaasitavya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपासितव्य</span> <i>upāsitavya</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">3</span>] <a href="#entry-upaas" class="skt-link">upās</a>] a. <a href="#entry-upaasitavya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-upaasitavya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-upaasitavyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>upāsitavyā</i> qui doit être honoré ou révéré.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upaasya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपास्य</span> <i>upāsya</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] <a href="#entry-upaas" class="skt-link">upās</a>] a. <a href="#entry-upaasya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-upaasya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-upaasyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>upāsyā</i> respectable, à honorer; vénérable, qu'on doit adorer, qui fait l'objet d'un culte <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> qu'on doit approcher; qu'on doit servir; qu'il faut subir <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> à connaître, à savoir — ind. ayant honoré, ayant rendu hommage à.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upaatii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपाती</span> <i>upātī</i> [<a href="#entry-upa" class="skt-link">upa</a>-<a href="#entry-atii" class="skt-link">atī</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">2</span>] pr. <i>(upātyeti)</i> omettre; négliger; transgresser.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upaatta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपात्त</span> <i>upātta</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-upaadaa" class="skt-link">upādā</a>] a. <a href="#entry-upaatta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-upaatta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-upaattaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>upāttā</i> résultant de.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upaatyaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपात्यय</span> <i>upātyaya</i> [act. <a href="#entry-upaatii" class="skt-link">upātī</a>] <a href="#entry-upaatyaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> transgression; non conformité aux usages.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upaaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपाय</span> <i>upāya</i> [<a href="#entry-upe_1" class="skt-link">upe_1</a>] <a href="#entry-upaaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> approche, moyen, procédé; obtention; expédient, stratagème, ruse; avantage <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> ce par quoi on atteint le but, avantage, score (cf. <a href="#entry-apaaya" class="skt-link">apāya</a>) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. rituel conjurant une occasion défavorable <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> bd. vérité conventionnelle; cf. <a href="#entry-sa_mv_rti" class="skt-link">saṃvṛti</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. stratagème pour subjuguer un adversaire; on en compte 4: la négociation [<a href="#entry-saaman" class="skt-link">sāman</a>], la corruption [<a href="#entry-daana" class="skt-link">dāna</a>], la trahison [<a href="#entry-bheda" class="skt-link">bheda</a>] et l'attaque frontale [<a href="#entry-da_n_da" class="skt-link">daṇḍa</a>]; cf. <a href="#entry-bala" class="skt-link">bala</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit. [<a href="#entry-tantra" class="skt-link">tantra</a>] désigne le <a href="#entry-yogin" class="skt-link">yogin</a> dans la symbolique bd. érotique.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upaayana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>upāyana</i> [<a href="#entry--na" class="skt-link">-na</a>] <a href="#entry-upaayana" class="skt-link">n.</a> approche; arrivée.<br> <a href="#entry-upaaya" class="skt-link">upāya</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upaayatas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>upāyatas</i> [<a href="#entry--tas" class="skt-link">-tas</a>] adv. par un expédient; par la ruse.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upaaz">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपाश्</span> <i>upāś</i> [<a href="#entry-upa" class="skt-link">upa</a>-<a href="#entry-az_1" class="skt-link">aś_1</a>] pf. <i>(</i><i>sam</i><i>)</i> [inusité sans pf.].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upaazraya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपाश्रय</span> <i>upāśraya</i> [<a href="#entry-upaazri" class="skt-link">upāśri</a>] <a href="#entry-upaazraya" class="skt-link">m.</a> appui, support; coussin <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. courroie de méditation, utilisée pour retenir les jambes dans des postures de <a href="#entry-yoga" class="skt-link">yoga</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-yogapa_t_taka" class="skt-link">yogapaṭṭaka</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upaazri">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपाश्रि</span> <i>upāśri</i> [<a href="#entry-upa" class="skt-link">upa</a>-<a href="#entry-aazri" class="skt-link">āśri</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(upāśrayati)</i> s'appuyer sur, se reposer sur <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> chercher refuge en, s'abandonner à.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upabh_rt">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपभृत्</span> <i>upabhṛt</i> [<a href="#entry-upa" class="skt-link">upa</a>-<a href="#entry-bh_rt" class="skt-link">bhṛt</a>] <a href="#entry-upabh_rt" class="skt-link">f.</a> soc. cuillère à oblation en bois; syn. <a href="#entry-sruc" class="skt-link">sruc</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upabhoga">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपभोग</span> <i>upabhoga</i> [<a href="#entry-upabhuj" class="skt-link">upabhuj</a>] <a href="#entry-upabhoga" class="skt-link">m.</a> plaisir, jouissance; consommation, plaisir de table; plaisir sexuel <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit. np. de l'Upabhoga, 3⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ livre du <a href="#entry-Umaanasollaasa" class="skt-link">Mānasollāsa</a>, en 20 chapitres [<a href="#entry-adhyaaya" class="skt-link">adhyāya</a>], traitant des plaisirs royaux: le palais [Gṛhopabhoga], le bain [Snānabhoga], les sandales [Pādukabhoga], le bétel [Tāmbulabhoga], les pommades [Vilepanabhoga], les vêtements [Vastropabhoga], les guirlandes [Mālyopabhoga], les ornements [Bhūṣopabhoga], les postures [Āsanopabhoga], les chasse-mouches [Camarabhoga], les audiences [Aasthānabhoga], l'héritier [Putrabhoga], les mets [Annabhoga], les boissons [Pānīyabhoga], le massage [Padābhyaṅgopabhoga], les véhicules [Yānopabhoga], les parasols [Chattrabhoga], la couche [Śayyabhoga], l'encens [Dhupabhoga], et les femmes [Yoṣidbhoga].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upabhuj">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपभुज्</span> <i>upabhuj</i> [<a href="#entry-upa" class="skt-link">upa</a>-<a href="#entry-bhuj_2" class="skt-link">bhuj_2</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">7</span>] pr. <i>(upabhunakti)</i> pr. md. <i>(upabhuṅkte)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>upabhukta</i><i>)</i> jouir de, consommer, se servir de; manger, boire; recevoir en récompense.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upacaara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपचार</span> <i>upacāra</i> [<a href="#entry-upacar" class="skt-link">upacar</a>] <a href="#entry-upacaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> révérence, hommage, compliment; cadeau; requête; fait de servir, rôle de courtisan <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> acte de politesse (on en dénombre 64) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> traitement (not. médical); conduite; comportement correct; emploi, cérémonie <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. hommage à une divinité; on en dénombre 16 (<i>ṣoḍaśopacāra</i>): la méditation [<a href="#entry-dhyaana" class="skt-link">dhyāna</a>], la posture [<a href="#entry-aasana" class="skt-link">āsana</a>], le lavage des pieds [<a href="#entry-paadya" class="skt-link">pādya</a>], l'oblation d'eau [<a href="#entry-arghya" class="skt-link">arghya</a>], la purification de la bouche [<a href="#entry-aacamana" class="skt-link">ācamana</a>], le bain rituel [<a href="#entry-snaana" class="skt-link">snāna</a>], le vêtement [<a href="#entry-vastra" class="skt-link">vastra</a>], le port du cordon sacré [<a href="#entry-yaj_nopaviita" class="skt-link">yajñopavīta</a>], le parfum [<a href="#entry-gandha" class="skt-link">gandha</a>], les fleurs [<a href="#entry-pu_spa" class="skt-link">puṣpa</a>], l'encens [<a href="#entry-dhuupa" class="skt-link">dhūpa</a>], la lampe [<a href="#entry-diipa" class="skt-link">dīpa</a>], l'offrande de nourriture [<a href="#entry-naivedya" class="skt-link">naivedya</a>], le bétel [<a href="#entry-taambuula" class="skt-link">tāmbūla</a>], l'illumination par une lampe de camphre [<a href="#entry-niiraajana" class="skt-link">nīrājana</a>] et la récitation d'un texte sacré en offrant des fleurs [<a href="#entry-mantrapu_spa" class="skt-link">mantrapuṣpa</a>]; on effectue les 16 hommages lors de la cérémonie de <a href="#entry-pa_ncaayatana" class="skt-link">pañcāyatana</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gram. métaphore.<br> <i>upacāramiśrā</i> gram. «mélangée à une métaphore», se dit d'une figure [<a href="#entry-lak_sa_naa" class="skt-link">lakṣaṇā</a>]; opp. <a href="#entry-zuddhaa" class="skt-link">śuddhā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upacaarin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपचारिन्</span> <i>upacārin</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-upacar" class="skt-link">upacar</a>] a. <a href="#entry-upacaarin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-upacaarin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-upacaari_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>upacāriṇī</i> qui aide, qui sert; qui honore, qui révère.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upacaayya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपचाय्य</span> <i>upacāyya</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] <a href="#entry-upaci" class="skt-link">upaci</a>] a. <a href="#entry-upacaayya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-upacaayya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-upacaayyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>upacāyyā</i> à accumuler; à renforcer — <a href="#entry-upacaayya" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. foyer du sacrifice.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upacaayyap_r_da">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>upacāyyapṛḍa</i> <a href="#entry-upacaayyap_r_da" class="skt-link">n.</a> or [«que l'on cherche à accumuler»].<br> <a href="#entry-upacaayya" class="skt-link">upacāyya</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upacar">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपचर्</span> <i>upacar</i> [<a href="#entry-upa" class="skt-link">upa</a>-<a href="#entry-car" class="skt-link">car</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(upacarati)</i> pr. md. <i>(upacarate)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>upacarita</i><i>)</i> assister, aider; servir, honorer; prendre soin de; soigner.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upacarita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपचरित</span> <i>upacarita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-upacar" class="skt-link">upacar</a>] a. <a href="#entry-upacarita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-upacarita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-upacaritaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>upacaritā</i> assisté, servi; soigné.
  </div>
</div>
