    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपगु</span> <i>upagu</i> [<a href="#entry-upa" class="skt-link">upa</a>-<a href="#entry-gu" class="skt-link">gu</a>] <a href="#entry-upagu" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. du roi Upagu «Proche des vaches».
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upah_r">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपहृ</span> <i>upahṛ</i> [<a href="#entry-upa" class="skt-link">upa</a>-<a href="#entry-h_r_1" class="skt-link">hṛ_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(upaharati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>upahṛta</i><i>)</i> tirer à soi; offrir — ca. <i>(upahārayati)</i> présenter, offrir.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upah_rta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपहृत</span> <i>upahṛta</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-upah_r" class="skt-link">upahṛ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-upah_rta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-upah_rta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-upah_rtaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>upahṛtā</i> offert.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upahaasa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपहास</span> <i>upahāsa</i> [<a href="#entry-upa" class="skt-link">upa</a>-<a href="#entry-haasa" class="skt-link">hāsa</a>] <a href="#entry-upahaasa" class="skt-link">m.</a> raillerie, affront.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upahan">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपहन्</span> <i>upahan</i> [<a href="#entry-upa" class="skt-link">upa</a>-<a href="#entry-han_1" class="skt-link">han_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">2</span>] pr. <i>(upahanti)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>upahata</i><i>)</i> frapper, heurter; empêcher, endommager.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upahata">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपहत</span> <i>upahata</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-upahan" class="skt-link">upahan</a>] a. <a href="#entry-upahata" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-upahata" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-upahataa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>upahatā</i> atteint de, affecté par, gâté par <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> empêché, contesté, contredit.<br> <i>vetālopahata</i> hanté par un revenant.<br> <i>kāmopahatacetana</i> dont les pensées sont remplies de concupiscence.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upahita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपहित</span> <i>upahita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-upadhaa_1" class="skt-link">upadhā_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-upahita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-upahita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-upahitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>upahitā</i> placé sur, déposé; placé dans, près de; installé, connecté à <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> utilisé à <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> apporté, remis, donné <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> dépendant de, ayant comme condition <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gram. immédiatement précédé par ⟨iic.⟩.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upajaata">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपजात</span> <i>upajāta</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-upajan" class="skt-link">upajan</a>] a. <a href="#entry-upajaata" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-upajaata" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-upajaataa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>upajātā</i> ajouté, additionnel; produit, engendré; excité.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upajaati">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपजाति</span> <i>upajāti</i> [<a href="#entry-upajan" class="skt-link">upajan</a>] <a href="#entry-upajaati" class="skt-link">f.</a> lit. mètre poétique homosyllabique de 4 pieds de 11 syllabes; il combine les mètres <a href="#entry-indravajraa" class="skt-link">indravajrā</a> et <a href="#entry-upendravajraa" class="skt-link">upendravajrā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upajan">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपजन्</span> <i>upajan</i> [<a href="#entry-upa" class="skt-link">upa</a>-<a href="#entry-jan" class="skt-link">jan</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">4</span>] pr. md. <i>(upajāyate)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>upajāta</i><i>)</i> naître, se produire, avoir lieu, exister — ca. <i>(upajanayati)</i> produire.<br> <i>kāmeṣu</i> <i>saṅgāt</i> <i>krodha</i> <i>upajāyate</i> La colère naît d'un attachement aux objets du désir.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upaji">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपजि</span> <i>upaji</i> [<a href="#entry-upa" class="skt-link">upa</a>-<a href="#entry-ji" class="skt-link">ji</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(upajayati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>upajita</i><i>)</i> conquérir, gagner.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upajiiv">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपजीव्</span> <i>upajīv</i> [<a href="#entry-upa" class="skt-link">upa</a>-<a href="#entry-jiiv" class="skt-link">jīv</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(upajīvati)</i> pr. md. <i>(upajīvate)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>upajīvita</i><i>)</i> vivre de, se nourrir de ⟨i.⟩.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upajiivaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपजीवक</span> <i>upajīvaka</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-upajiiv" class="skt-link">upajīv</a>] a. <a href="#entry-upajiivaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-upajiivaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-upajiivikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>upajīvikā</i> qui dépend de, qui vit de ⟨i. iic.⟩ — <a href="#entry-upajiivaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> subsistance personnelle — <a href="#entry-upajiivikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>upajīvikā</i> id.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upak_r">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपकृ</span> <i>upakṛ</i> [<a href="#entry-upa" class="skt-link">upa</a>-<a href="#entry-k_r_1" class="skt-link">kṛ_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">8</span>] pr. <i>(upakaroti)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>upakṛta</i><i>)</i> assister, servir; secourir, soigner, faire du bien; obliger, rendre service à qqn. ⟨g. loc.⟩ — v. [<span class="skt-grammar">8</span>] pr. md. <i>(upaskurute)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>upaskṛta</i><i>)</i> faire effort; transformer; suppléer (une proposition qui manque).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upak_r_s_naat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपकृष्णात्</span> <i>upakṛṣṇāt</i> [<a href="#entry-upa" class="skt-link">upa</a>-<a href="#entry-k_r_s_na" class="skt-link">kṛṣṇa</a>] adv. soc. ayant pris <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_na" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇa</a> comme refuge.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upak_rti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपकृति</span> <i>upakṛti</i> [act. <a href="#entry-upak_r" class="skt-link">upakṛ</a>] <a href="#entry-upak_rti" class="skt-link">f.</a> secours, aide, assistance; service rendu ou reçu.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upakaara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपकार</span> <i>upakāra</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-upak_r" class="skt-link">upakṛ</a>] <a href="#entry-upakaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> service (rendu), aide, soin; bénéfice, faveur; bonté <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> ornement, décoration (guirlandes, etc.) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gram. subdivision fine des rôles sémantiques [<a href="#entry-kaaraka" class="skt-link">kāraka</a>].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upakaaraka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपकारक</span> <i>upakāraka</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-upak_r" class="skt-link">upakṛ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-upakaaraka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-upakaaraka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-upakaarikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>upakārikā</i> serviable, qui aide; bienfaiteur <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> approprié; requis, nécessaire; subsidiaire, accessoire.<br> <i>sarvaśāstropakāraka</i> utile à toutes les sciences.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upakaarin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपकारिन्</span> <i>upakārin</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-upak_r" class="skt-link">upakṛ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-upakaarin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-upakaarin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-upakaari_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>upakāriṇī</i> serviable, qui aide; bienfaiteur.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upakak_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपकक्ष</span> <i>upakakṣa</i> [<a href="#entry-upa" class="skt-link">upa</a>-<a href="#entry-kak_sa" class="skt-link">kakṣa</a>] a. <a href="#entry-upakak_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-upakak_sa" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-upakak_saa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>upakakṣā</i> sous les aisselles — <a href="#entry-upakak_sa" class="skt-link">n.</a> touffe de poils sous les aisselles.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upakak_sadaghna">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>upakakṣadaghna</i> [<a href="#entry-daghna" class="skt-link">daghna</a>] a. <a href="#entry-upakak_sadaghna" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-upakak_sadaghna" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-upakak_sadaghnaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>upakakṣadaghnā</i> qui a (de l'eau) jusqu'aux aisselles.<br> <a href="#entry-upakak_sa" class="skt-link">upakakṣa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upakani_s_thikaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपकनिष्ठिका</span> <i>upakaniṣṭhikā</i> [<a href="#entry-upa" class="skt-link">upa</a>-<a href="#entry-kani_s_thaa" class="skt-link">kaniṣṭhā</a>] <a href="#entry-upakani_s_thikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> (av. <a href="#entry-afguli" class="skt-link">aṅguli</a>) annulaire.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upakara_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपकरण</span> <i>upakaraṇa</i> [act. <a href="#entry-upak_r" class="skt-link">upakṛ</a>] <a href="#entry-upakara_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> aide, assistance <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> outil, machine, appareil, instruments <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> expédient, ressource, moyen de subsistance <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> objet fabriqué.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upakart_r">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपकर्तृ</span> <i>upakartṛ</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-upak_r" class="skt-link">upakṛ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-upakart_r" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-upakart_r" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-upakartrii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>upakartrī</i> qui fait une bonne action; bienfaiteur.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upakram">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपक्रम्</span> <i>upakram</i> [<a href="#entry-upa" class="skt-link">upa</a>-<a href="#entry-kram" class="skt-link">kram</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(upakrāmati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>upakrānta</i><i>)</i> s'approcher; arriver <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> se jeter sur, attaquer <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> méd. traiter, soigner (un patient) — pr. md. <i>(upakramate)</i> aborder, avoir recours à, se mettre à ⟨acc. dat. inf.⟩.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upakrama">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपक्रम</span> <i>upakrama</i> [<a href="#entry-upakram" class="skt-link">upakram</a>] <a href="#entry-upakrama" class="skt-link">m.</a> approche; commencement; entreprise, plan <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> moyen, expédient; stratagème <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> méd. remède; traitement médical.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upakrii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपक्री</span> <i>upakrī</i> [<a href="#entry-upa" class="skt-link">upa</a>-<a href="#entry-krii" class="skt-link">krī</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">9</span>] pr. <i>(upakrīṇāti)</i> acheter.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upakumbham">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपकुम्भम्</span> <i>upakumbham</i> [<a href="#entry-upa" class="skt-link">upa</a>-<a href="#entry-kumbha" class="skt-link">kumbha</a>] adv. près de la cruche.<br> <i>upakumbhād</i> <i>āgataḥ</i> venu d'auprès de la cruche.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upakurvaa_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपकुर्वाण</span> <i>upakurvāṇa</i> [ppr. md. <a href="#entry-upak_r" class="skt-link">upakṛ</a>] <a href="#entry-upakurvaa_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. [«qui rend service»] étudiant brâmanique [<a href="#entry-brahmacarya" class="skt-link">brahmacarya</a>] optant à la fin de ses études pour la vie de famille [<a href="#entry-g_rhastha" class="skt-link">gṛhastha</a>]; opp. <a href="#entry-nai_s_thika" class="skt-link">naiṣṭhika</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपल</span> <i>upala</i> <a href="#entry-upala" class="skt-link">m.</a> pierre, rocher <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pierre précieuse, joyau — <a href="#entry-upalaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>upalā</i> meule mobile (pierre supérieure d'un moulin, reposant sur son socle [<a href="#entry-d_r_sad" class="skt-link">dṛṣad</a>]).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upalaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपला</span> <i>upalā</i> <a href="#entry-upalaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> meule (supérieure).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upalabdha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपलब्ध</span> <i>upalabdha</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-upalabh" class="skt-link">upalabh</a>] a. <a href="#entry-upalabdha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-upalabdha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-upalabdhaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>upalabdhā</i> saisi; compris; perçu, reconnu.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upalabdhi">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपलब्धि</span> <i>upalabdhi</i> [act. <a href="#entry-upalabh" class="skt-link">upalabh</a>] <a href="#entry-upalabdhi" class="skt-link">f.</a> obtention, acquisition, gain <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> observation, perception; prise de conscience <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> compréhension, connaissance <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> fait d'être perceptible.<br> <i>garbhasya</i> <i>upalabdhi</i> conception.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upalabdhimat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>upalabdhimat</i> [<a href="#entry--mat" class="skt-link">-mat</a>] a. <a href="#entry-upalabdhimat" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-upalabdhimat" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-upalabdhimatii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>upalabdhimatī</i> qui perçoit, qui comprend <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> perceptible, intelligible.<br> <a href="#entry-upalabdhi" class="skt-link">upalabdhi</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upalabh">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपलभ्</span> <i>upalabh</i> [<a href="#entry-upa" class="skt-link">upa</a>-<a href="#entry-labh" class="skt-link">labh</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. md. <i>(upalabhate)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>upalabdha</i><i>)</i> prendre, s'emparer de, saisir; obtenir <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> percevoir, comprendre, reconnaître — ca. <i>(upalambhayati)</i> faire obtenir; faire comprendre.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upalabhya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपलभ्य</span> <i>upalabhya</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] <a href="#entry-upalabh" class="skt-link">upalabh</a>] a. <a href="#entry-upalabhya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-upalabhya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-upalabhyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>upalabhyā</i> qui peut être obtenu, à prendre; atteignable <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> perceptible, compréhensible, intelligible — ind. ayant obtenu, ayant perçu.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upalak_s">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपलक्ष्</span> <i>upalakṣ</i> [<a href="#entry-upa" class="skt-link">upa</a>-<a href="#entry-lak_s" class="skt-link">lakṣ</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">10</span>] pr. <i>(upalakṣayati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>upalakṣita</i><i>)</i> pfp. <i>(</i><i>upalakṣya</i><i>)</i> observer; percevoir; faire attention à <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> considérer comme; évaluer; distinguer; indiquer, désigner; impliquer — ps. <i>(upalakṣyate)</i> être observé; être désigné.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upalak_sa_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपलक्षण</span> <i>upalakṣaṇa</i> [act. <a href="#entry-upalak_s" class="skt-link">upalakṣ</a>] <a href="#entry-upalak_sa_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> fait d'observer; désignation; implication <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> indicateur <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. usage métaphorique; sous-entendu; synecdoche.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upalak_sa_nataa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>upalakṣaṇatā</i> [<a href="#entry--taa" class="skt-link">-tā</a>] <a href="#entry-upalak_sa_nataa" class="skt-link">f.</a> phil. [<a href="#entry-nyaaya" class="skt-link">nyāya</a>] fait d'être un indicateur.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upalak_sa_natva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>upalakṣaṇatva</i> [<a href="#entry--tva" class="skt-link">-tva</a>] <a href="#entry-upalak_sa_natva" class="skt-link">n.</a> id.<br> <a href="#entry-upalak_sa_na" class="skt-link">upalakṣaṇa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upalak_sita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपलक्षित</span> <i>upalakṣita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-upalak_s" class="skt-link">upalakṣ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-upalak_sita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-upalak_sita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-upalak_sitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>upalakṣitā</i> observé; perçu; regardé; estimé <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> caractérisé, marqué, distingué <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> inclus, impliqué <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. exprimé par implication; sous-entendu; compris.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upalak_sya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपलक्ष्य</span> <i>upalakṣya</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] <a href="#entry-upalak_s" class="skt-link">upalakṣ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-upalak_sya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-upalak_sya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-upalak_syaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>upalakṣyā</i> à comprendre par implication, inférable.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upalambh">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपलम्भ्</span> <i>upalambh</i> [ca. <a href="#entry-upalabh" class="skt-link">upalabh</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">10</span>] pr. <i>(upalambhayati)</i> faire obtenir; faire comprendre.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upalambha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपलम्भ</span> <i>upalambha</i> [act. <a href="#entry-upalambh" class="skt-link">upalambh</a>] <a href="#entry-upalambha" class="skt-link">m.</a> obtention <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> perception, compréhension; reconnaissance.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upalambhaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपलम्भक</span> <i>upalambhaka</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-upalambh" class="skt-link">upalambh</a>] a. <a href="#entry-upalambhaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-upalambhaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-upalambhakaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>upalambhakā</i> qui obtient <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> qui perçoit <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> qui fait percevoir; qui rappelle.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upalambhana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपलम्भन</span> <i>upalambhana</i> [act. <a href="#entry-upalambh" class="skt-link">upalambh</a>] <a href="#entry-upalambhana" class="skt-link">n.</a> perception rapide; intelligence.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upalip">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपलिप्</span> <i>upalip</i> [<a href="#entry-upa" class="skt-link">upa</a>-<a href="#entry-lip" class="skt-link">lip</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">6</span>] pr. <i>(upalimpati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>upalipta</i><i>)</i> enduire, oindre; salir; épandre, lisser (not. la bouse [<a href="#entry-gomaya" class="skt-link">gomaya</a>]).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upama">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपम</span> <i>upama</i> [<a href="#entry-upamaa_1" class="skt-link">upamā_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-upama" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-upama" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-upamaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>upamā</i> meilleur, suprême; excellent <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> premier; plus proche, suivant — ifc. qui ressemble à, semblable à, pareil à — f. cf. <a href="#entry-upamaa_2" class="skt-link">upamā_2</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upamaa_1">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपमा</span> <i>upamā_1</i> [<a href="#entry-upa" class="skt-link">upa</a>-<a href="#entry-maa_1" class="skt-link">mā_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">3</span>] pr. md. <i>(upamimīte)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>upamita</i><i>)</i> [«mesurer une chose avec une autre»] comparer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upamaa_2">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपमा</span> <i>upamā_2</i> [f. <a href="#entry-upama" class="skt-link">upama</a>] <a href="#entry-upamaa_2" class="skt-link">f.</a> comparaison; ressemblance <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> similarité <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gram. [<a href="#entry-alafkaara" class="skt-link">alaṃkāra</a>] synonyme; rhét. métaphore, <i>simile</i> — ifc. similaire à, qui ressemble à, tel un, comme.<br> <i>amaropamā</i> a. m. n. tel un immortel.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upamaana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपमान</span> <i>upamāna</i> [<a href="#entry-upamaa_1" class="skt-link">upamā_1</a><a href="#entry--na" class="skt-link">-na</a>] <a href="#entry-upamaana" class="skt-link">n.</a> standard de comparaison; objet d'une comparaison; opp. <a href="#entry-upameya" class="skt-link">upameya</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. [<a href="#entry-nyaaya" class="skt-link">nyāya</a>] l'identification, un des 4 moyens de connaissance légitime [<a href="#entry-pramaa_na" class="skt-link">pramāṇa</a>].<br> <i>upamānopameyabhāva</i> gram. relation d'analogie ou de comparaison.<br> <i>upamitikaraṇam</i> <i>upamānam</i> [<a href="#entry-Utarkasafgraha" class="skt-link">Tarkasaṃgraha</a>] L'identification est la cause efficiente de la connaissance analogique.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upamaanabahuvriihi">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>upamānabahuvrīhi</i> [<a href="#entry-bahuvriihi" class="skt-link">bahuvrīhi</a>] <a href="#entry-upamaanabahuvriihi" class="skt-link">m.</a> gram. composé adjectival de comparaison.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upamaanapuurvapada">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>upamānapūrvapada</i> [<a href="#entry-puurvapada" class="skt-link">pūrvapada</a>] <a href="#entry-upamaanapuurvapada" class="skt-link">n.</a> gram. composé <a href="#entry-karmadhaaraya" class="skt-link">karmadhāraya</a> dont le constituant gauche est une comparaison (comme <a href="#entry-analo_s_na" class="skt-link">analoṣṇa</a>).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upamaanottarapada">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>upamānottarapada</i> [<a href="#entry-uttarapada" class="skt-link">uttarapada</a>] <a href="#entry-upamaanottarapada" class="skt-link">n.</a> gram. composé <a href="#entry-karmadhaaraya" class="skt-link">karmadhāraya</a> dont le constituant droit est une comparaison (comme <a href="#entry-puru_savyaaghra" class="skt-link">puruṣavyāghra</a>).<br> <a href="#entry-upamaana" class="skt-link">upamāna</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upamaaprapa_nca">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>upamāprapañca</i> [<a href="#entry-prapa_nca" class="skt-link">prapañca</a>] <a href="#entry-upamaaprapa_nca" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. [<a href="#entry-alafkaara" class="skt-link">alaṃkāra</a>] utilisation métaphorique.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upamaartha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>upamārtha</i> [<a href="#entry-artha" class="skt-link">artha</a>] <a href="#entry-upamaartha" class="skt-link">m.</a> raison d'une ressemblance; sens d'une image.<br> <i>upamārthena</i> figurativement.<br> <a href="#entry-upamaa_2" class="skt-link">upamā_2</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upamadhyamaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपमध्यमा</span> <i>upamadhyamā</i> [<a href="#entry-upa" class="skt-link">upa</a>-<a href="#entry-madhyamaa" class="skt-link">madhyamā</a>] <a href="#entry-upamadhyamaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> annulaire.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upamadhyamiiya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपमध्यमीय</span> <i>upamadhyamīya</i> [<a href="#entry-upa" class="skt-link">upa</a>-<a href="#entry-madhyamiiya" class="skt-link">madhyamīya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-upamadhyamiiya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-upamadhyamiiya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-upamadhyamiiyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>upamadhyamīyā</i> en dessous du centre — <a href="#entry-upamadhyamiiya" class="skt-link">m.</a> phon. (véd) fricative bilabiale sourde, remplacée en sanskrit classique par le <a href="#entry-visarga" class="skt-link">visarga</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upamanyu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपमन्यु</span> <i>upamanyu</i> [<a href="#entry-upa" class="skt-link">upa</a>-<a href="#entry-manyu" class="skt-link">manyu</a>] a. <a href="#entry-upamanyu" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-upamanyu" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-upamanyu" class="skt-link">f.</a> empressé, zélé, obéissant — <a href="#entry-upamanyu" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. du <a href="#entry-_r_si" class="skt-link">ṛṣi</a> Upamanyu «Obéissant», élève de <a href="#entry-Udhaumya" class="skt-link">Dhaumya</a>; il donnait à son maître la nourriture qu'il recevait en aumônes; ce dernier lui ordonna de ne pas mendier pour son propre compte, ni de boire le lait des vaches, ni même la mousse du lait au muffle des veaux; il mangea d'une écorce empoisonnée, devint aveugle, tomba dans un puits, et recouvra la vue en priant les <a href="#entry-Uazvinau" class="skt-link">Aśvinau</a>; on dit qu'il est fils de <a href="#entry-Uvyaaghrapaada" class="skt-link">Vyāghrapāda</a>, et que bébé il obtint en priant <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> la mer de lait comme nourriture.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upamazravas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>upamaśravas</i> [<a href="#entry-zravas" class="skt-link">śravas</a>] a. <a href="#entry-upamazravas" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-upamazravas" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-upamazravas" class="skt-link">f.</a> à la plus grande gloire — <a href="#entry-upamazravas" class="skt-link">m.</a> [«le plus glorieux»] np. d'homme.<br> <a href="#entry-upama" class="skt-link">upama</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upameya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपमेय</span> <i>upameya</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] <a href="#entry-upamaa_1" class="skt-link">upamā_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-upameya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-upameya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-upameyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>upameyā</i> comparable avec ⟨i. iic.⟩ — <a href="#entry-upameya" class="skt-link">n.</a> sujet d'une comparaison; opp. <a href="#entry-upamaana" class="skt-link">upamāna</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-ruupaka" class="skt-link">rūpaka</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upamita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपमित</span> <i>upamita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-upamaa_1" class="skt-link">upamā_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-upamita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-upamita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-upamitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>upamitā</i> comparé.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upamiti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपमिति</span> <i>upamiti</i> [act. <a href="#entry-upamaa_1" class="skt-link">upamā_1</a>] <a href="#entry-upamiti" class="skt-link">f.</a> phil. [<a href="#entry-nyaaya" class="skt-link">nyāya</a>] connaissance par identification; cf. <a href="#entry-upamaana" class="skt-link">upamāna</a> — <a href="#entry-upamiti" class="skt-link">f.</a> phil. ce qui est identifié.<br> <i>saṃjñāsaṃjñisaṃbandhajñānam</i> <i>upamitiḥ</i> [<a href="#entry-Utarkasafgraha" class="skt-link">Tarkasaṃgraha</a>] La connaissance par identification est la connaissance de la relation entre un nom et sa dénotation.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upanaaha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपनाह</span> <i>upanāha</i> [<a href="#entry-upanah" class="skt-link">upanah</a>] <a href="#entry-upanaaha" class="skt-link">m.</a> faisceau, botte.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upanaaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपनाय</span> <i>upanāya</i> [<a href="#entry-upanii" class="skt-link">upanī</a>] <a href="#entry-upanaaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. initiation.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upanaayana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>upanāyana</i> [<a href="#entry--na" class="skt-link">-na</a>] <a href="#entry-upanaayana" class="skt-link">n.</a> cf. <a href="#entry-upanayana" class="skt-link">upanayana</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-upanaaya" class="skt-link">upanāya</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upanadam">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपनदम्</span> <i>upanadam</i> [<a href="#entry-upa" class="skt-link">upa</a>-<a href="#entry-nadii" class="skt-link">nadī</a>] adv. près de la rivière.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upanah">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपनह्</span> <i>upanah</i> [<a href="#entry-upa" class="skt-link">upa</a>-<a href="#entry-nah" class="skt-link">nah</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">4</span>] pr. <i>(upanahyati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>upanaddha</i><i>)</i> attacher, nouer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upanam">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपनम्</span> <i>upanam</i> [<a href="#entry-upa" class="skt-link">upa</a>-<a href="#entry-nam" class="skt-link">nam</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(upanamati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>upanata</i><i>)</i> échoir à; avoir lieu.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upananda">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपनन्द</span> <i>upananda</i> [<a href="#entry-upa" class="skt-link">upa</a>-<a href="#entry-nanda" class="skt-link">nanda</a>] <a href="#entry-upananda" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] nom d'un tambour de <a href="#entry-Uyudhi_s_thira" class="skt-link">Yudhiṣṭhira</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upanata">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपनत</span> <i>upanata</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-upanam" class="skt-link">upanam</a>] a. <a href="#entry-upanata" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-upanata" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-upanataa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>upanatā</i> échu à, dépendant de; reçu, présenté; existant, présent.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upanaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपनय</span> <i>upanaya</i> [<a href="#entry-upanii" class="skt-link">upanī</a>] <a href="#entry-upanaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> fait de procurer; fait d'atteindre <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. [<a href="#entry-nyaaya" class="skt-link">nyāya</a>] l'application du syllogisme [<a href="#entry-pa_ncaavayava" class="skt-link">pañcāvayava</a>].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upanayana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपनयन</span> <i>upanayana</i> [act. <a href="#entry-upanii" class="skt-link">upanī</a>] <a href="#entry-upanayana" class="skt-link">n.</a> fait de conduire ou d'amener vers <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. initiation religieuse; investiture du cordon sacré [<a href="#entry-upaviita" class="skt-link">upavīta</a>], un <a href="#entry-sa_mskaara" class="skt-link">saṃskāra</a> des 3 premières castes [<a href="#entry-var_na" class="skt-link">varṇa</a>]; cette cérémonie confère au garçon la dignité de <a href="#entry-dvija" class="skt-link">dvija</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upani_sad">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपनिषद्</span> <i>upaniṣad</i> [<a href="#entry-upa" class="skt-link">upa</a>-<a href="#entry-ni_sad" class="skt-link">niṣad</a>] <a href="#entry-upani_sad" class="skt-link">f.</a> phil. connexion; interconnexion (not. entre le microcosme et le macrocosme) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> [«assis près (du maître)»] leçon, session d'initiation; croyance, doctrine, secret <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> <a href="#entry-upani_sad" class="skt-link">upaniṣad</a>, leçon philosophique expliquant le <a href="#entry-Uveda" class="skt-link">Veda</a>; les 13 plus anciennes datent de l'époque védique, et font partie de la <a href="#entry-zruti" class="skt-link">śruti</a>; [Muk.] il y a 108 leçons majeures [<a href="#entry-mahopani_sad" class="skt-link">mahopaniṣad</a>]: <a href="#entry-iiza" class="skt-link">īśa</a>, <a href="#entry-kena" class="skt-link">kena</a>, <a href="#entry-ka_tha" class="skt-link">kaṭha</a>, <a href="#entry-prazna" class="skt-link">praśna</a>, <a href="#entry-mu_n_daka" class="skt-link">muṇḍaka</a>, <a href="#entry-maa_n_duukya" class="skt-link">māṇḍūkya</a>, <a href="#entry-taittiriiya" class="skt-link">taittirīya</a>, <a href="#entry-aitareya" class="skt-link">aitareya</a>, <a href="#entry-chaandogya" class="skt-link">chāndogya</a>, <a href="#entry-b_rhadaara_nyaka" class="skt-link">bṛhadāraṇyaka</a>, <i>brahma</i>, <a href="#entry-kaivalya" class="skt-link">kaivalya</a>, <a href="#entry-jaabaala" class="skt-link">jābāla</a>, <a href="#entry-zvetaazvatara" class="skt-link">śvetāśvatara</a>, <a href="#entry-ha_msa" class="skt-link">haṃsa</a>, <a href="#entry-aaru_neya" class="skt-link">āruṇeya</a>, <a href="#entry-garbha" class="skt-link">garbha</a>, <a href="#entry-naaraaya_na" class="skt-link">nārāyaṇa</a>, <a href="#entry-paramaha_msa" class="skt-link">paramahaṃsa</a>, <a href="#entry-am_rtabindu" class="skt-link">amṛtabindu</a>, <i>amṛtanāda</i>, <i>atharvaśira</i>, <i>atharvaśikha</i>, <i>maitrāyaṇi</i>, <a href="#entry-kau_siitaki" class="skt-link">kauṣītaki</a>, <i>bṛhajjābāla</i>, <i>nṛsiṃhatāpanī</i>, <i>kālāgnirudra</i>, <i>maitreyi</i>, <i>subāla</i>, <i>kśurika</i>, <i>mantrika</i>, <i>sarvasāra</i>, <i>nirālamba</i>, <i>śukarahasya</i>, <i>vajrasūcika</i>, <i>tejobindu</i>, <i>nādabindu</i>, <i>dhyānabindu</i>, <a href="#entry-brahmavidyaa" class="skt-link">brahmavidyā</a>, <i>yogatattva</i>, <i>ātmabodha</i>, <i>parivrāṭ</i> (<a href="#entry-Unaaradaparivraajakaa" class="skt-link">Nāradaparivrājakā</a>), <i>triṣikhi</i>, <i>sītopaniṣad</i> (<i>sītāthavāsītā</i>), <i>yogacūḍāmaṇi</i>, <a href="#entry-nirvaa_na" class="skt-link">nirvāṇa</a>, <i>maṇḍalabrāhmaṇa</i>, <a href="#entry-dak_si_naamuurti" class="skt-link">dakṣiṇāmūrti</a>, <a href="#entry-zarabha" class="skt-link">śarabha</a>, <a href="#entry-skanda" class="skt-link">skanda</a>, (<i>tripādvibhūṭi</i>) <i>mahānārāyaṇa</i>, <i>advayatāraka</i>, <i>rāmarahasya</i>, <i>rāmatāpaṇi</i>, <a href="#entry-vaasudeva" class="skt-link">vāsudeva</a>, <a href="#entry-mudgala" class="skt-link">mudgala</a>, <i>śāṇḍilya</i>, <a href="#entry-paifgala" class="skt-link">paiṅgala</a>, <a href="#entry-bhik_su" class="skt-link">bhikṣu</a>, <i>mahatśārīraka</i>, <i>yogaśikhā</i>, <i>turīyātīta</i>, <a href="#entry-sannyaasa" class="skt-link">saṃnyāsa</a>, <i>paramahaṃsaparivrājaka</i>, <i>akśamālika</i>, <a href="#entry-avyakta" class="skt-link">avyakta</a>, <i>ekākśara</i>, <a href="#entry-annapuur_na" class="skt-link">annapūrṇa</a>, <a href="#entry-suurya" class="skt-link">sūrya</a>, <a href="#entry-ak_si" class="skt-link">akṣi</a>, <i>adhyātmā</i>, <i>kuṇḍikopaniṣad</i>, <a href="#entry-saavitrii" class="skt-link">sāvitrī</a>, <a href="#entry-aatma" class="skt-link">ātma</a>, <a href="#entry-paazupata" class="skt-link">pāśupata</a>, <i>parabrahma</i>, <a href="#entry-avadhuuta" class="skt-link">avadhūta</a>, <i>tripurātapanopaniṣad</i>, <a href="#entry-devii" class="skt-link">devī</a>, <a href="#entry-tripura" class="skt-link">tripura</a>, <a href="#entry-kara_1" class="skt-link">kara_1</a>, <a href="#entry-bhaavana" class="skt-link">bhāvana</a>, <i>rudrahṛdaya</i>, <i>yogakuṇḍalini</i>, <i>bhasmopaniṣad</i>, <a href="#entry-rudraak_sa" class="skt-link">rudrākṣa</a>, <a href="#entry-ga_napati" class="skt-link">gaṇapati</a>, <a href="#entry-darzana" class="skt-link">darśana</a>, <i>tārasāra</i>, <a href="#entry-mahaavaakya" class="skt-link">mahāvākya</a>, <i>pañcabrahma</i>, <i>prāṇāgnihotra</i>, <i>gopālatapaṇi</i>, <a href="#entry-k_r_s_na" class="skt-link">kṛṣṇa</a>, <a href="#entry-yaaj_navalkya" class="skt-link">yājñavalkya</a>, <a href="#entry-varaaha" class="skt-link">varāha</a>, <i>śātyāyani</i>, <a href="#entry-hayagriiva" class="skt-link">hayagrīva</a>, <a href="#entry-dattaatreya" class="skt-link">dattātreya</a>, <a href="#entry-gaaru_da" class="skt-link">gāruḍa</a>, <i>kalisaṇṭāraṇa</i>, <a href="#entry-prazna" class="skt-link">praśna</a>, <a href="#entry-saubhaagya" class="skt-link">saubhāgya</a>, <i>sarasvatīrahasya</i>, <a href="#entry-bahv_rcaa" class="skt-link">bahvṛcā</a>, <a href="#entry-muktikaa" class="skt-link">muktikā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upani_sadratna">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>upaniṣadratna</i> [<a href="#entry-ratna" class="skt-link">ratna</a>] <a href="#entry-upani_sadratna" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. np. de l'Upaniṣadratna «Joyau des leçons»; cf. <a href="#entry-Uaatmapuraa_na" class="skt-link">Ātmapurāṇa</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-upani_sad" class="skt-link">upaniṣad</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upanii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपनी</span> <i>upanī</i> [<a href="#entry-upa" class="skt-link">upa</a>-<a href="#entry-nii_1" class="skt-link">nī_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(upanayati)</i> amener à, emmener, guider, introduire à <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> initier, consacrer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upanimantra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपनिमन्त्र</span> <i>upanimantra</i> [<a href="#entry-upa" class="skt-link">upa</a>-<a href="#entry-nimantra" class="skt-link">nimantra</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">11</span>] pr. md. <i>(upanimantrayate)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>upanimantrita</i><i>)</i> inviter à; offrir.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upanimantra_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपनिमन्त्रण</span> <i>upanimantraṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-upanimantra" class="skt-link">upanimantra</a><a href="#entry--na" class="skt-link">-na</a>] <a href="#entry-upanimantra_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> invitation à, offre de ⟨i.⟩.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upanipaata">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपनिपात</span> <i>upanipāta</i> [<a href="#entry-upanipat" class="skt-link">upanipat</a>] <a href="#entry-upanipaata" class="skt-link">m.</a> occurrence; événement soudain; attaque surprise.<br> <i>anarthopanipāta</i> arrivée subite d'un événement funeste.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upanipaatin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपनिपातिन्</span> <i>upanipātin</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-upanipat" class="skt-link">upanipat</a>] ifc. a. <a href="#entry-upanipaatin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-upanipaatin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-upanipaatinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>upanipātinī</i> qui se précipite vers, qui se jette sur, qui s'empare de.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upanipat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपनिपत्</span> <i>upanipat</i> [<a href="#entry-upa" class="skt-link">upa</a>-<a href="#entry-nipat" class="skt-link">nipat</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(upanipatati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>upanipatita</i><i>)</i> descendre en volant; accéder, se manifester; arriver, s'imposer — ca. <i>(upanipātayati)</i> provoquer un événement soudain.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upanyaasa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपन्यास</span> <i>upanyāsa</i> [<a href="#entry-upanyas" class="skt-link">upanyas</a>] <a href="#entry-upanyaasa" class="skt-link">m.</a> fait de placer à proximité; apport, fourniture; dépôt, gage <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> mention, référence, citation; suggestion; explication.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upanyas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपन्यस्</span> <i>upanyas</i> [<a href="#entry-upa" class="skt-link">upa</a>-<a href="#entry-nyas" class="skt-link">nyas</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">4</span>] pr. <i>(upanyasyati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>upanyasta</i><i>)</i> mettre en avant, vouloir dire; mentionner, annoncer <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> suggérer, expliquer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upap_rc_1">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपपृच्</span> <i>upapṛc_1</i> [<a href="#entry-upa" class="skt-link">upa</a>-<a href="#entry-p_rc" class="skt-link">pṛc</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">7</span>] pr. <i>(upapṛṇakti)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>upapṛkta</i><i>)</i> véd. s'approcher.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upap_rc_2">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपपृच्</span> <i>upapṛc_2</i> [<a href="#entry-upap_rc_1" class="skt-link">upapṛc_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-upap_rc_2" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-upap_rc_2" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-upap_rc_2" class="skt-link">f.</a> véd. qui s'accroche à, qui adhère à ⟨g.⟩.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upapaada">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपपाद</span> <i>upapāda</i> [<a href="#entry-upa" class="skt-link">upa</a>-<a href="#entry-paada" class="skt-link">pāda</a>] <a href="#entry-upapaada" class="skt-link">m.</a> évènement, occurrence <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> accomplissement.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upapaadaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>upapādaka</i> [<a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] a. <a href="#entry-upapaadaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-upapaadaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-upapaadakaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>upapādakā</i> qui fait arriver, qui produit, qui fait apparaître.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upapaadana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>upapādana</i> [<a href="#entry--na" class="skt-link">-na</a>] a. <a href="#entry-upapaadana" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-upapaadana" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-upapaadanaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>upapādanā</i> id. — <a href="#entry-upapaadana" class="skt-link">n.</a> action accomplissante; effet <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> production, présentation <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. [<a href="#entry-nyaaya" class="skt-link">nyāya</a>] établissement de la validité d'un jugement <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> explication.<br> <a href="#entry-upapaada" class="skt-link">upapāda</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upapaaduka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपपादुक</span> <i>upapāduka</i> [<a href="#entry-upa" class="skt-link">upa</a>-<a href="#entry-paaduka" class="skt-link">pāduka</a>] a. <a href="#entry-upapaaduka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-upapaaduka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-upapaadukaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>upapādukā</i> auto-produit, né spontanément — <a href="#entry-upapaaduka" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. être surnaturel.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upapaataka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपपातक</span> <i>upapātaka</i> [<a href="#entry-upa" class="skt-link">upa</a>-<a href="#entry-paataka" class="skt-link">pātaka</a>] <a href="#entry-upapaataka" class="skt-link">n.</a> soc. péché véniel, offense mineure, délit; not. tuer du bétail, offenser son <a href="#entry-guru" class="skt-link">guru</a>, briser son vœu de chasteté, oublier le <a href="#entry-Uveda" class="skt-link">Veda</a>, vendre le <a href="#entry-soma" class="skt-link">soma</a>; opp. <a href="#entry-mahaapaataka" class="skt-link">mahāpātaka</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upapad">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपपद्</span> <i>upapad</i> [<a href="#entry-upa" class="skt-link">upa</a>-<a href="#entry-pad_1" class="skt-link">pad_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">4</span>] pr. md. <i>(upapadyate)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>upapanna</i><i>)</i> avoir lieu, arriver, se manifester <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> atteindre, obtenir, participer à, être apte à ⟨loc.⟩.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upapada">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपपद</span> <i>upapada</i> [<a href="#entry-upapad" class="skt-link">upapad</a>] <a href="#entry-upapada" class="skt-link">n.</a> gram. mot co-occurrent avec un autre mot dont il contrôle la désinence (par ex. <a href="#entry-saha" class="skt-link">saha</a> avec i.); cf. <a href="#entry-vibhakti" class="skt-link">vibhakti</a>; désigne aussi une variété de composés, dont le second membre contrôle le premier.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upapanna">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपपन्न</span> <i>upapanna</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-upapad" class="skt-link">upapad</a>] a. <a href="#entry-upapanna" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-upapanna" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-upapannaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>upapannā</i> ayant acquis; doué de, muni de ⟨i.⟩ <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> obtenu; prêt, à disposition; possible, normal; approprié.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upaparvan">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपपर्वन्</span> <i>upaparvan</i> [<a href="#entry-upa" class="skt-link">upa</a>-<a href="#entry-parvan" class="skt-link">parvan</a>] <a href="#entry-upaparvan" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. sous-section d'un livre; not. l'une des 97 sous-sections du <a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upapatti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपपत्ति</span> <i>upapatti</i> [act. <a href="#entry-upapad" class="skt-link">upapad</a>] <a href="#entry-upapatti" class="skt-link">f.</a> apparition, occurrence, manifestation; œuvre <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. démonstration, preuve, évidence.<br> <i>upapattyā</i> i. adv. convenablement, de façon appropriée.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upaplava">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपप्लव</span> <i>upaplava</i> [<a href="#entry-upaplu" class="skt-link">upaplu</a>] <a href="#entry-upaplava" class="skt-link">m.</a> invasion; inondation; catastrophe, calamité, infortune <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phénomène naturel (not. éclipse) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. épith. de <a href="#entry-Uraahu" class="skt-link">Rāhu</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upaplavin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>upaplavin</i> [<a href="#entry--in" class="skt-link">-in</a>] a. <a href="#entry-upaplavin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-upaplavin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-upaplavinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>upaplavinī</i> affligé par une calamité <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> inondé.<br> <a href="#entry-upaplava" class="skt-link">upaplava</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upaplavya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपप्लव्य</span> <i>upaplavya</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] <a href="#entry-upaplu" class="skt-link">upaplu</a>] a. <a href="#entry-upaplavya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-upaplavya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-upaplavyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>upaplavyā</i> inondable; irriguable; qu'il faut envahir — <a href="#entry-upaplavya" class="skt-link">n.</a> myth. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] np. d'Upaplavya «Soumise aux crues», épith. de <a href="#entry-Uviraa_tanagara" class="skt-link">Virāṭanagara</a>, capitale de <a href="#entry-Uviraa_ta" class="skt-link">Virāṭa</a>, roi des <a href="#entry-matsyaas" class="skt-link">matsyās</a>; les <a href="#entry-Upaa_n_davaas" class="skt-link">Pāṇḍavās</a> s'y réfugièrent pendant leur exil; <a href="#entry-Udh_rtaraa_s_tra" class="skt-link">Dhṛtarāṣṭra</a> y envoya <a href="#entry-Usa_njaya" class="skt-link">Sañjaya</a> comme négociateur.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upaplu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपप्लु</span> <i>upaplu</i> [<a href="#entry-upa" class="skt-link">upa</a>-<a href="#entry-plu" class="skt-link">plu</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(upaplavati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>upapluta</i><i>)</i> pfp. <i>(</i><i>upaplavya</i><i>)</i> déborder, inonder <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> envahir; opprimer — v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. md. <i>(upaplavate)</i> flotter en surface — ca. <i>(upaplāvayati)</i> faire inonder; irriger.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upapluta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपप्लुत</span> <i>upapluta</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-upaplu" class="skt-link">upaplu</a>] a. <a href="#entry-upapluta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-upapluta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-upaplutaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>upaplutā</i> inondé, irrigué; envahi; affligé.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upapuraa_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपपुराण</span> <i>upapurāṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-upa" class="skt-link">upa</a>-<a href="#entry-puraa_na" class="skt-link">purāṇa</a>] <a href="#entry-upapuraa_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. l'un des 18 livres mythologiques mineurs; beaucoup sont de composition tardive (après 1200); opp. <a href="#entry-mahaapuraa_na" class="skt-link">mahāpurāṇa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपर</span> <i>upara</i> [<a href="#entry-upa" class="skt-link">upa</a>] a. <a href="#entry-upara" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-upara" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-uparaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>uparā</i> sous; postérieur; proche — <a href="#entry-upara" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. pierre support de la meule à <a href="#entry-soma" class="skt-link">soma</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uparam">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपरम्</span> <i>uparam</i> [<a href="#entry-upa" class="skt-link">upa</a>-<a href="#entry-ram" class="skt-link">ram</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. md. <i>(uparamate)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>uparata</i><i>)</i> cesser, se reposer, s'arrêter; s'abstenir de, renoncer à ⟨abl.⟩.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uparama">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपरम</span> <i>uparama</i> [<a href="#entry-uparam" class="skt-link">uparam</a>] <a href="#entry-uparama" class="skt-link">m.</a> cessation, arrêt, expiration; abstention <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> abandon, désistement <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. [<a href="#entry-vedaanta" class="skt-link">vedānta</a>] le renoncement au monde, l'une des 6 aptitudes requises [<a href="#entry-_sa_tkasampatti" class="skt-link">ṣaṭkasampatti</a>] de l'aspirant à la libération [<a href="#entry-adhikaarin" class="skt-link">adhikārin</a>].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uparata">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपरत</span> <i>uparata</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-uparam" class="skt-link">uparam</a>] a. <a href="#entry-uparata" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-uparata" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-uparataa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>uparatā</i> cessé; indifférent; disparu, mort.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uparati">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपरति</span> <i>uparati</i> [<a href="#entry-uparam" class="skt-link">uparam</a>] <a href="#entry-uparati" class="skt-link">f.</a> repos; recueillement <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> mort <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. [<a href="#entry-vedaanta" class="skt-link">vedānta</a>] le recueillement, l'une des 6 vertus cardinales [<a href="#entry-_sa_tsampad" class="skt-link">ṣaṭsampad</a>]; sa pratique implique la cessation de tous les devoirs concernant les sacrifices.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uparatna">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपरत्न</span> <i>uparatna</i> [<a href="#entry-upa" class="skt-link">upa</a>-<a href="#entry-ratna" class="skt-link">ratna</a>] <a href="#entry-uparatna" class="skt-link">n.</a> pierre semi-précieuse: <a href="#entry-gomeda" class="skt-link">gomeda</a> l'hyacinthe, <a href="#entry-pu_syaraaga" class="skt-link">puṣyarāga</a> la topaze, <a href="#entry-vai_duurya" class="skt-link">vaiḍūrya</a> l'œil-de-chat et <a href="#entry-pravaala" class="skt-link">pravāla</a> le corail.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upari">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपरि</span> <i>upari</i> [<a href="#entry-upa" class="skt-link">upa</a>] adv. au-dessus; en outre; après — prép. au-dessus de, au delà de ⟨acc. loc. g. abl.⟩; après ⟨abl.⟩ <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> à propos de, vis-à-vis de, en ce qui concerne ⟨g.⟩ — ifc. sur <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> ang. over; all. über.<br> <i>uparyupari</i> [red.] prép. loin au dessus de.<br> <i>kailāsaśikharopari</i> adv. au sommet du Mont <a href="#entry-Ukailaasa" class="skt-link">Kailāsa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upari_s_taat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>upariṣṭāt</i> adv. en haut; de dessus.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upari_s_tha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>upariṣṭha</i> var. <i>uparistha</i> [<a href="#entry-stha" class="skt-link">stha</a>] a. <a href="#entry-upari_s_tha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-upari_s_tha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-upari_s_thaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>upariṣṭhā</i> qui se tient au-dessus, situé sur.<br> <a href="#entry-upari" class="skt-link">upari</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uparibhuumi">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>uparibhūmi</i> [<a href="#entry-bhuumi" class="skt-link">bhūmi</a>] adv. au dessus du sol.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uparicara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>uparicara</i> [<a href="#entry-cara" class="skt-link">cara</a>] <a href="#entry-uparicara" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] np. d'Uparicara «Qui va dans les airs» (avec le char aérien que lui donna <a href="#entry-Uindra" class="skt-link">Indra</a>), épith. du roi <a href="#entry-Uvasu_2" class="skt-link">Vasu_2</a> du pays <a href="#entry-Ucedi" class="skt-link">Cedi</a>; son épouse est <a href="#entry-Ugirikaa" class="skt-link">Girikā</a>, qui lui donna cinq fils dont <a href="#entry-Ub_rhadratha" class="skt-link">Bṛhadratha</a>; un jour de Printemps où la Reine était indisposée, il alla chasser dans la forêt et émit du sperme, qu'il confia à un milan, mais qui tomba dans la <a href="#entry-Uyamunaa" class="skt-link">Yamunā</a> et fut mangé par la nymphe-poisson <a href="#entry-Uadrikaa" class="skt-link">Adrikā</a>; dans son ventre on trouva <a href="#entry-Umatsyagandhii" class="skt-link">Matsyagandhī</a> (future <a href="#entry-Usatyavatii" class="skt-link">Satyavatī</a>) et <a href="#entry-Umatsya_1" class="skt-link">Matsya_1</a>; <a href="#entry-Uindra" class="skt-link">Indra</a> lui donna une guirlande éternellement fraîche [<a href="#entry-vaijayantiimaalaa" class="skt-link">vaijayantīmālā</a>], le protégeant de toute blessure tant qu'il la porterait; ainsi qu'un bâton de bambou [<a href="#entry-ve_nuda_n_da" class="skt-link">veṇudaṇḍa</a>] à planter en terre pour célébrer le festival d'<a href="#entry-Uindra" class="skt-link">Indra</a> [<a href="#entry-indrotsava" class="skt-link">indrotsava</a>], qu'il institua; il bâtit un grand empire, était l'ami d'<a href="#entry-Uindra" class="skt-link">Indra</a> et de <a href="#entry-Ugaru_da" class="skt-link">Garuḍa</a>, un dévot de <a href="#entry-Uvi_s_nu" class="skt-link">Viṣṇu</a>, un membre influent du conseil du <a href="#entry-Uyama" class="skt-link">Yama</a>, et il monta finalement au ciel de <a href="#entry-Ubrahmaa" class="skt-link">Brahmā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uparimartya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>uparimartya</i> [<a href="#entry-martya" class="skt-link">martya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-uparimartya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-uparimartya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-uparimartyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>uparimartyā</i> surhumain — <a href="#entry-uparimartya" class="skt-link">m.</a> sur­homme [«au-dessus des mortels»].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uparodha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपरोध</span> <i>uparodha</i> [<a href="#entry-uparudh" class="skt-link">uparudh</a>] <a href="#entry-uparodha" class="skt-link">m.</a> entrave, blocage, obstruction, obstacle <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> trouble; insulte.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uparodhana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>uparodhana</i> [<a href="#entry--na" class="skt-link">-na</a>] <a href="#entry-uparodhana" class="skt-link">n.</a> entrave, blocage, obstruction, obstacle.<br> <a href="#entry-uparodha" class="skt-link">uparodha</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uparudh">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपरुध्</span> <i>uparudh</i> [<a href="#entry-upa" class="skt-link">upa</a>-<a href="#entry-rudh_2" class="skt-link">rudh_2</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">7</span>] pr. <i>(uparuṇaddhi)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>uparuddha</i><i>)</i> bloquer, arrêter, entraver, obstruer, empêcher; assiéger.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uparudha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपरुध</span> <i>uparudha</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-uparudh" class="skt-link">uparudh</a>] a. <a href="#entry-uparudha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-uparudha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-uparudhaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>uparudhā</i> bloqué, arrêté, empêché.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uparuupaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपरूपक</span> <i>uparūpaka</i> [<a href="#entry-upa" class="skt-link">upa</a>-<a href="#entry-ruupaka" class="skt-link">rūpaka</a>] <a href="#entry-uparuupaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. genre mineur de théâtre.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upas_r">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपसृ</span> <i>upasṛ</i> [<a href="#entry-upa" class="skt-link">upa</a>-<a href="#entry-s_r" class="skt-link">sṛ</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(upasarati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>upasṛta</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>sam</i><i>)</i> s'approcher.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upas_r_s_ta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपसृष्ट</span> <i>upasṛṣṭa</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-upas_rj" class="skt-link">upasṛj</a>] a. <a href="#entry-upas_r_s_ta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-upas_r_s_ta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-upas_r_s_taa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>upasṛṣṭā</i> émis; envoyé, mené <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> not. (veau) mené à sa mère; (lait) suçé par le veau <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> muni de <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gram. pourvu d'un préverbe <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> visité par, affligé de, emcombré de, souffrant de <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> possédé par (dieu, démon) — <a href="#entry-upas_r_s_ta" class="skt-link">n.</a> coït.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upas_rj">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपसृज्</span> <i>upasṛj</i> [<a href="#entry-upa" class="skt-link">upa</a>-<a href="#entry-s_rj_1" class="skt-link">sṛj_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">6</span>] pr. <i>(upasṛjati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>upasṛṣṭa</i><i>)</i> relâcher; répandre sur, arroser; émettre <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> rapprocher; accompagner, mener (un veau à sa mère) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> venir en contact avec, mettre en contact; ajouter à <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> visiter; affliger, tomber sur; assujettir, soumettre, faire subir, contrôler.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upas_rp">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपसृप्</span> <i>upasṛp</i> [<a href="#entry-upa" class="skt-link">upa</a>-<a href="#entry-s_rp" class="skt-link">sṛp</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(upasarpati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>upasṛpta</i><i>)</i> ramper vers, s'approcher en rampant de ⟨acc.⟩; approcher sexuellement; épier <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> se mettre à ⟨inf.⟩ — ca. <i>(upasarpayati)</i> faire s'approcher.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upas_rpta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपसृप्त</span> <i>upasṛpta</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-upas_rp" class="skt-link">upasṛp</a>] a. <a href="#entry-upas_rpta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-upas_rpta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-upas_rptaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>upasṛptā</i> possédé sexuellement.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upasa_mh_r">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपसंहृ</span> <i>upasaṃhṛ</i> [<a href="#entry-upa" class="skt-link">upa</a>-<a href="#entry-sa_mh_r" class="skt-link">saṃhṛ</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. md. <i>(upasaṃharate)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>upasaṃhṛta</i><i>)</i> mettre en contact, assembler <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> retirer, refuser, supprimer, anéantir; absorber; résumer, interrompre <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> approcher de soi.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upasa_mh_rta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपसंहृत</span> <i>upasaṃhṛta</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-upasa_mh_r" class="skt-link">upasaṃhṛ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-upasa_mh_rta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-upasa_mh_rta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-upasa_mh_rtaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>upasaṃhṛtā</i> rapproché, mis en contact <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> retenu, retiré <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> interrompu; arrêté; supprimé; exclu; absorbé; détruit.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upasa_mh_rti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपसंहृति</span> <i>upasaṃhṛti</i> [act. <a href="#entry-upasa_mh_r" class="skt-link">upasaṃhṛ</a>] <a href="#entry-upasa_mh_rti" class="skt-link">f.</a> assemblage <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> conclusion, fin <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit. [théâtre] catastrophe; apothéose; dénouement; syn. <a href="#entry-nirvaha_na" class="skt-link">nirvahaṇa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upasa_mhaara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपसंहार</span> <i>upasaṃhāra</i> [act. <a href="#entry-upasa_mh_r" class="skt-link">upasaṃhṛ</a>] <a href="#entry-upasa_mhaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> fin; conclusion, épilogue <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> résumé.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upasa_mhaara_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपसंहारण</span> <i>upasaṃhāraṇa</i> [act. <a href="#entry-upasa_mh_r" class="skt-link">upasaṃhṛ</a>] <a href="#entry-upasa_mhaara_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> acte de retirer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upasa_mhaarya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपसंहार्य</span> <i>upasaṃhārya</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] <a href="#entry-upasa_mh_r" class="skt-link">upasaṃhṛ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-upasa_mhaarya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-upasa_mhaarya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-upasa_mhaaryaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>upasaṃhāryā</i> phil. [<a href="#entry-vedaanta" class="skt-link">vedānta</a>] qu'il faut supprimer en méditation (se dit des qualités du <a href="#entry-brahman" class="skt-link">brahman</a>).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upasaantvana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपसान्त्वन</span> <i>upasāntvana</i> [<a href="#entry-upa" class="skt-link">upa</a>-<a href="#entry-saantvana" class="skt-link">sāntvana</a>] <a href="#entry-upasaantvana" class="skt-link">n.</a> acte d'apaisement ou de réconciliation; consolation de ⟨g. iic.⟩ <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pl. <i>upasāntvanāni</i> bonnes paroles.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upasad_1">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">

[<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(upasīdati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>upasanna</i><i>)</i> s'asseoir sur <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> s'asseoir près de, s'approcher respectueusement de; solliciter l'instruction d'un maître; révérer — ca. <i>(upasādayati)</i> placer sur ou à côté de.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upasad_2">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपसद्</span> <i>upasad_2</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-upasad_1" class="skt-link">upasad_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-upasad_2" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-upasad_2" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-upasad_2" class="skt-link">f.</a> qui s'asseoit près de, qui s'approche respectueusement de; qui honore.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upasafgam">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपसंगम्</span> <i>upasaṃgam</i> var. <i>upasaṅgam</i> [<a href="#entry-upa" class="skt-link">upa</a>-<a href="#entry-safgam" class="skt-link">saṃgam</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. md. <i>(upasaṃgacchate)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>upasaṃgata</i><i>)</i> s'approcher de, se joindre à.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upasafgrah">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपसंग्रह्</span> <i>upasaṃgrah</i> [<a href="#entry-upa" class="skt-link">upa</a>-<a href="#entry-safgrah" class="skt-link">saṃgrah</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">9</span>] pr. <i>(upasaṃgṛhṇāti)</i> soc. prendre les pieds de qqn. dans ses mains en signe de respect.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upasafgraha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपसंग्रह</span> <i>upasaṃgraha</i> [act. <a href="#entry-upasafgrah" class="skt-link">upasaṃgrah</a>] <a href="#entry-upasa_mgraha" class="skt-link">m.</a> action de saisir ou de toucher en manière de supplication.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upasafgraha_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">

[<span class="skt-grammar">6</span>] pr. <i>(upaspṛśati)</i> pr. md. <i>(upaspṛśate)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>upaspṛṣṭa</i><i>)</i> toucher le haut, atteindre la hauteur de <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> caresser <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> toucher (not. l'eau pour se purifier); se baigner (rite religieux); boire de l'eau dans le creux de ses mains (pour se rincer la bouche) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> toucher une partie du corps ⟨acc.⟩ avec de l'eau ⟨i.⟩.

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upastha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपस्थ</span> <i>upastha</i> [<a href="#entry-upasthaa" class="skt-link">upasthā</a>] <a href="#entry-upastha" class="skt-link">m.</a> sexe; not. vagin, organe génital féminin <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. [<a href="#entry-saafkhya" class="skt-link">sāṃkhya</a>] faculté [<a href="#entry-karmendriya" class="skt-link">karmendriya</a>] de reproduction, dont le régent [<a href="#entry-niyant_r" class="skt-link">niyantṛ</a>] est <a href="#entry-Uprajaapati" class="skt-link">Prajāpati</a>; son élément [<a href="#entry-bhuuta" class="skt-link">bhūta</a>] prédominant est <a href="#entry-ap" class="skt-link">ap</a> l'eau; la perception [<a href="#entry-tanmaatra" class="skt-link">tanmātra</a>] associée est la saveur [<a href="#entry-rasa" class="skt-link">rasa</a>] qui excite le sens [<a href="#entry-buddhiindriya" class="skt-link">buddhīndriya</a>] du goût [<a href="#entry-jihvaa" class="skt-link">jihvā</a>].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upasthaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपस्था</span> <i>upasthā</i> [<a href="#entry-upa" class="skt-link">upa</a>-<a href="#entry-sthaa_1" class="skt-link">sthā_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(upatiṣṭhati)</i> pr. md. <i>(upatiṣṭhate)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>upasthita</i><i>)</i> se tenir auprès de; s'approcher de, rendre hommage à; adorer, prier <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> servir, se tenir aux ordres de; s'allier à <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> tomber au pouvoir de — ca. <i>(upasthāpayati)</i> faire placer auprès de; faire s'allonger auprès de <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> faire venir à proximité, apporter auprès, procurer; aller chercher.<br> <i>ādityam</i> <i>upatiṣṭhate</i> il vénère le Soleil.<br> <i>devadatto</i> <i>yajñadattam</i> <i>upatiṣṭhate</i> <a href="#entry-Udevadatta" class="skt-link">Devadatta</a> s'est lié d'amitié avec <a href="#entry-Uyaj_nadatta" class="skt-link">Yajñadatta</a>.<br> <i>ayaṃ</i> <i>panthā</i> <i>pāṭaliputram</i> <i>upatiṣṭhate</i> cette route mène à <a href="#entry-Upaa_taliputra" class="skt-link">Pāṭaliputra</a>.<br> <i>yāvadbhojanam</i> <i>upatiṣṭhate</i> il est toujours prêt quand il s'agit de manger.<br> <i>sākṣī</i> <i>nopatasthau</i> le témoin n'est pas apparu.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upasthaana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">

  </div>
</div>

[<span class="skt-grammar">10</span>] pr. <i>(upasthāpayati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>upasthāpita</i><i>)</i> faire placer auprès de; faire s'allonger auprès de <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> faire venir à proximité, apporter auprès, procurer; aller chercher <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gram. ajouter la particule <a href="#entry-iti" class="skt-link">iti</a> après un mot.
  </div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upatya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपत्य</span> <i>upatya</i> [<a href="#entry-upa" class="skt-link">upa</a><a href="#entry--tya" class="skt-link">-tya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-upatya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-upatya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-upatyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>upatyā</i> étendu sous <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gr. υπτηος.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upatyakaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>upatyakā</i> [<a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] <a href="#entry-upatyakaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> pays au pied d'une montagne; vallée; opp. <a href="#entry-adhityakaa" class="skt-link">adhityakā</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-upatya" class="skt-link">upatya</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upav_rt">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपवृत्</span> <i>upavṛt</i> [<a href="#entry-upa" class="skt-link">upa</a>-<a href="#entry-v_rt_1" class="skt-link">vṛt_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. md. <i>(upavartate)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>upavṛtta</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>sam</i><i>)</i> approcher; échoir à; se reposer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upavaasa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपवास</span> <i>upavāsa</i> [<a href="#entry-upavas" class="skt-link">upavas</a>] <a href="#entry-upavaasa" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. [<a href="#entry-vinaya" class="skt-link">vinaya</a>] jeûne, fête jeûnée; abstinence de gratifications sensuelles.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upavaasin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपवासिन्</span> <i>upavāsin</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-upavas" class="skt-link">upavas</a>] a. <a href="#entry-upavaasin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-upavaasin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-upavaasinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>upavāsinī</i> qui pratique le jeûne, l'abstinence.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upavah">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपवह्</span> <i>upavah</i> [<a href="#entry-upa" class="skt-link">upa</a>-<a href="#entry-vah_1" class="skt-link">vah_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(upavahati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>upoḍha</i><i>)</i> porter près de, amener; procurer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upavana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपवन</span> <i>upavana</i> [<a href="#entry-upa" class="skt-link">upa</a>-<a href="#entry-vana" class="skt-link">vana</a>] <a href="#entry-upavana" class="skt-link">n.</a> sous-bois; bosquet.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upavar_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपवर्ण</span> <i>upavarṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-upa" class="skt-link">upa</a>-<a href="#entry-var_na" class="skt-link">varṇa</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">11</span>] pr. <i>(upavarṇayati)</i> décrire, détailler, raconter.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upavas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपवस्</span> <i>upavas</i> [<a href="#entry-upa" class="skt-link">upa</a>-<a href="#entry-vas_1" class="skt-link">vas_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(upavasati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>upoṣita</i><i>)</i> rester, demeurer <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> jeûner, s'abstenir de nourriture; pratiquer la continence.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upava_ncana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपवञ्चन</span> <i>upavañcana</i> [<a href="#entry-upa" class="skt-link">upa</a>-<a href="#entry-va_ncana" class="skt-link">vañcana</a>] <a href="#entry-upava_ncana" class="skt-link">n.</a> approche chancelante.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upaveda">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपवेद</span> <i>upaveda</i> [<a href="#entry-upa" class="skt-link">upa</a>-<a href="#entry-veda" class="skt-link">veda</a>] <a href="#entry-upaveda" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. science mineure, art; ensemble de textes complémentaires du <a href="#entry-Uveda" class="skt-link">Veda</a>; on liste: la médecine ou <a href="#entry-Uaayurveda" class="skt-link">Āyurveda</a>, l'archerie ou <a href="#entry-Udhanurveda" class="skt-link">Dhanurveda</a>, la musique ou <a href="#entry-Ugaandharvaveda" class="skt-link">Gāndharvaveda</a>, et la science des armes ou <a href="#entry-Uzastrazaastra" class="skt-link">Śastraśāstra</a>; mais aussi: l'architecture ou <a href="#entry-Usthaapatyaveda" class="skt-link">Sthāpatyaveda</a>, le savoir artistique ou <a href="#entry-Uzilpazaastra" class="skt-link">Śilpaśāstra</a> et la science du gouvernement ou <a href="#entry-Uarthazaastra" class="skt-link">Arthaśāstra</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upaveza">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपवेश</span> <i>upaveśa</i> [act. <a href="#entry-upaviz" class="skt-link">upaviś</a>] <a href="#entry-upaveza" class="skt-link">m.</a> fait de s'asseoir; fait de se mettre à une tâche <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. du <a href="#entry-_r_si" class="skt-link">ṛṣi</a> Upaveśa.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upavezana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपवेशन</span> <i>upaveśana</i> [act. <a href="#entry-upaviz" class="skt-link">upaviś</a>] <a href="#entry-upavezana" class="skt-link">n.</a> action de s'asseoir; attente paisible.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upavezi">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपवेशि</span> <i>upaveśi</i> <a href="#entry-upavezi" class="skt-link">m.</a> np. du <a href="#entry-_r_si" class="skt-link">ṛṣi</a> Upaveśi.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upavezita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपवेशित</span> <i>upaveśita</i> [ca. pp. <a href="#entry-upaviz" class="skt-link">upaviś</a>] a. <a href="#entry-upavezita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-upavezita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-upavezitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>upaveśitā</i> que l'on a fait asseoir.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upavi_s_ta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपविष्ट</span> <i>upaviṣṭa</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-upaviz" class="skt-link">upaviś</a>] a. <a href="#entry-upavi_s_ta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-upavi_s_ta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-upavi_s_taa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>upaviṣṭā</i> assis.
  </div>
</div>
