<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upavi_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपविष</span> <i>upaviṣa</i> [<a href="#entry-upa" class="skt-link">upa</a>-<a href="#entry-vi_sa" class="skt-link">viṣa</a>] <a href="#entry-upavi_sa" class="skt-link">n.</a> narcotique, drogue — <a href="#entry-upavi_saa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>upaviṣā</i> natu. bot. <i>Aconitum ferox</i>, aconit féroce, renonculacée herbacée aux fleurs bleues; sa racine contient l'alcaloïde pseudaconitine, un poison violent.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upavidyaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपविद्या</span> <i>upavidyā</i> [<a href="#entry-upa" class="skt-link">upa</a>-<a href="#entry-vidyaa" class="skt-link">vidyā</a>] <a href="#entry-upavidyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> connaissance inférieure; science profane.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upaviita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपवीत</span> <i>upavīta</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-upavyaa" class="skt-link">upavyā</a>] a. <a href="#entry-upaviita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-upaviita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-upaviitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>upavītā</i> entouré <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. ceint du cordon brahmanique — <a href="#entry-upaviita" class="skt-link">n.</a> fait de ceindre ce cordon <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. cordon sacré des <a href="#entry-dvija" class="skt-link">dvija</a>; formé de 6 brins de coton pour les brâhmanes, il est porté en diagonale entre l'épaule gauche et le flanc droit; son nœud contient <a href="#entry-Ubrahmaa" class="skt-link">Brahmā</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-upanayana" class="skt-link">upanayana</a>, <a href="#entry-anujaata" class="skt-link">anujāta</a>, <a href="#entry-viitasuutra" class="skt-link">vītasūtra</a>, <a href="#entry-niviita" class="skt-link">nivīta</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upaviz">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपविश्</span> <i>upaviś</i> [<a href="#entry-upa" class="skt-link">upa</a>-<a href="#entry-viz_1" class="skt-link">viś_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">6</span>] pr. <i>(upaviśati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>upaviṣṭa</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>sam</i><i>)</i> venir près de, s'asseoir auprès de <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> se coucher (soleil); cultiver (sol); se vouer à — ps. <i>(upaviśyate)</i> être assis — ca. <i>(upaviśyati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>upaveśita</i><i>)</i> faire asseoir, installer.<br> <i>upaviśyatām</i> adr. asseyez-vous (ps. imp. «qu'il s'asseye ici»).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upavyaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपव्या</span> <i>upavyā</i> [<a href="#entry-upa" class="skt-link">upa</a>-<a href="#entry-vyaa" class="skt-link">vyā</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">4</span>] pr. md. <i>(upavyayate)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>upavīta</i><i>)</i> ceindre le cordon brahmanique.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upavyaaghra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपव्याघ्र</span> <i>upavyāghra</i> [<a href="#entry-upa" class="skt-link">upa</a>-<a href="#entry-vyaaghra" class="skt-link">vyāghra</a>] <a href="#entry-upavyaaghra" class="skt-link">m.</a> natu. zoo. léopard — <a href="#entry-upavyaaghrii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>upavyāghrī</i> natu. léopard femelle.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upavyaakhyaana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपव्याख्यान</span> <i>upavyākhyāna</i> [<a href="#entry-upa" class="skt-link">upa</a>-<a href="#entry-vyaakhyaana" class="skt-link">vyākhyāna</a>] <a href="#entry-upavyaakhyaana" class="skt-link">n.</a> explication, interprétation.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upayaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपया</span> <i>upayā</i> [<a href="#entry-upa" class="skt-link">upa</a>-<a href="#entry-yaa_1" class="skt-link">yā_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">2</span>] pr. <i>(upayāti)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>upayāta</i><i>)</i> s'approcher, arriver; en venir à tel état.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upayaata">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपयात</span> <i>upayāta</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-upayaa" class="skt-link">upayā</a>] a. <a href="#entry-upayaata" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-upayaata" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-upayaataa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>upayātā</i> qui s'est approché; venu, arrivé — <a href="#entry-upayaata" class="skt-link">n.</a> arrivée.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upayam">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपयम्</span> <i>upayam</i> [<a href="#entry-upa" class="skt-link">upa</a>-<a href="#entry-yam" class="skt-link">yam</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(upayacchati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>upayata</i><i>)</i> épouser.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upayant_r">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपयन्तृ</span> <i>upayantṛ</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-upayam" class="skt-link">upayam</a>] <a href="#entry-upayant_r" class="skt-link">m.</a> époux.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upayoga">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपयोग</span> <i>upayoga</i> [<a href="#entry-upayuj" class="skt-link">upayuj</a>] <a href="#entry-upayoga" class="skt-link">m.</a> emploi, usage; utilisation; utilité <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> consommation, jouissance; acquisition (not. de connaissances) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. [<a href="#entry-nyaaya" class="skt-link">nyāya</a>] causalité.<br> <i>upayogaṃ</i> <i>gam</i> être utilisé, être employé.<br> <i>upayogaṃ</i> <i>vraj</i> id.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upayogin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>upayogin</i> [<a href="#entry--in" class="skt-link">-in</a>] a. <a href="#entry-upayogin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-upayogin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-upayoginii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>upayoginī</i> convenable, utile; utilisable, approprié, pertinent.<br> <a href="#entry-upayoga" class="skt-link">upayoga</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upayuj">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपयुज्</span> <i>upayuj</i> [<a href="#entry-upa" class="skt-link">upa</a>-<a href="#entry-yuj_1" class="skt-link">yuj_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">7</span>] pr. md. <i>(upayuṅkte)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>upayukta</i><i>)</i> s'approprier; entreprendre; se soucier de, adhérer à, être dévoué à <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> jouir de, utiliser <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> employer quelqu'un — ps. <i>(upayujyate)</i> être employé, être utilisé; être utile à — ca. <i>(upayojayati)</i> employer, utiliser.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upayukta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपयुक्त</span> <i>upayukta</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-upayuj" class="skt-link">upayuj</a>] a. <a href="#entry-upayukta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-upayukta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-upayuktaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>upayuktā</i> employé, utilisé; consommé <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> applicable, utile; juste; digne de.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upazaanta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपशान्त</span> <i>upaśānta</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-upazam" class="skt-link">upaśam</a>] a. <a href="#entry-upazaanta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-upazaanta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-upazaantaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>upaśāntā</i> calmé, pacifié — <a href="#entry-upazaanta" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. du philosophe bd. Upaśānta, auteur de l'<a href="#entry-Uabhidharmah_rdaya_2" class="skt-link">Abhidharmahṛdaya_2</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upazaanti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपशान्ति</span> <i>upaśānti</i> [act. <a href="#entry-upazam" class="skt-link">upaśam</a>] <a href="#entry-upazaanti" class="skt-link">f.</a> cessation; rémission; tranquillité.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upazaantiprakara_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>upaśāntiprakaraṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-prakara_na" class="skt-link">prakaraṇa</a>] <a href="#entry-upazaantiprakara_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. np. de l'Upaśāntiprakaraṇa, 5⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ section du <a href="#entry-Umok_sopaaya" class="skt-link">Mokṣopāya</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-upazaanti" class="skt-link">upaśānti</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upazaaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपशाय</span> <i>upaśāya</i> [act. <a href="#entry-upazii" class="skt-link">upaśī</a>] ifc. <a href="#entry-upazaaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> tour de dormir avec ⟨iic.⟩.<br> <i>tava</i> <i>rājopaśāyaḥ</i> c'est ton tour de dormir avec le roi.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upazam">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपशम्</span> <i>upaśam</i> [<a href="#entry-upa" class="skt-link">upa</a>-<a href="#entry-zam_1" class="skt-link">śam_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">4</span>] pr. <i>(upaśāmyati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>upaśānta</i><i>)</i> se calmer, se tranquilliser; s'éteindre.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upazama">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपशम</span> <i>upaśama</i> [act. <a href="#entry-upazam" class="skt-link">upaśam</a>] <a href="#entry-upazama" class="skt-link">m.</a> fait de se calmer, assagissement; quiétude; arrêt, cessation.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upazamaprakara_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>upaśamaprakaraṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-prakara_na" class="skt-link">prakaraṇa</a>] <a href="#entry-upazamaprakara_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. np. de l'Upaśamaprakaraṇa, 4⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ section du <a href="#entry-Umok_sopaaya" class="skt-link">Mokṣopāya</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-upazama" class="skt-link">upaśama</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upazii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपशी</span> <i>upaśī</i> [<a href="#entry-upa" class="skt-link">upa</a>-<a href="#entry-zii_1" class="skt-link">śī_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">2</span>] pr. md. <i>(upaśete)</i> être étendu le long de; être couché avec.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upazik_s">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपशिक्ष्</span> <i>upaśikṣ</i> [<a href="#entry-upa" class="skt-link">upa</a>-<a href="#entry-zik_s" class="skt-link">śikṣ</a>] v. pr. <i>(upaśikṣati)</i> vouloir subjuguer; s'approprier — pr. md. <i>(upaśikṣate)</i> s'efforcer d'aider, offrir ses services <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> s'enquérir de, étudier — ca. <i>(upaśikṣayati)</i> instruire.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upazobhita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपशोभित</span> <i>upaśobhita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-upazubh" class="skt-link">upaśubh</a>] a. <a href="#entry-upazobhita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-upazobhita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-upazobhitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>upaśobhitā</i> orné, décoré (de ⟨iic.⟩).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upazru">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपश्रु</span> <i>upaśru</i> [<a href="#entry-upa" class="skt-link">upa</a>-<a href="#entry-zru" class="skt-link">śru</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">5</span>] pr. <i>(upaśṛṇoti)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>upaśruta</i><i>)</i> entendre; entendre dire, apprendre.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upazruti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपश्रुति</span> <i>upaśruti</i> [act. <a href="#entry-upazru" class="skt-link">upaśru</a>] <a href="#entry-upazruti" class="skt-link">f.</a> fait d'entendre; rumeur <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> prophétie d'un oracle.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upazubh">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपशुभ्</span> <i>upaśubh</i> [<a href="#entry-upa" class="skt-link">upa</a>-<a href="#entry-zubh_1" class="skt-link">śubh_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. md. <i>(upaśobhate)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>upaśobhita</i><i>)</i> briller, resplendir — ca. <i>(śobhayati)</i> orner, décorer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upe_1">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपे</span> <i>upe_1</i> [<a href="#entry-upa" class="skt-link">upa</a>-<a href="#entry-i" class="skt-link">i</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">2</span>] pr. <i>(upaiti)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>upeta</i><i>)</i> abs. <i>(</i><i>upetya</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>abhi</i>, <i>sam</i><i>)</i> s'approcher de, aller vers; rencontrer, approcher sexuellement <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> se mettre à, entreprendre; exécuter (rite); se vouer à — pr. md. <i>(upite)</i> atteindre; avoir en partage.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upe_2">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपे</span> <i>upe_2</i> [<a href="#entry-upa" class="skt-link">upa</a>-<a href="#entry-e" class="skt-link">e</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">2</span>] pr. <i>(upaiti)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>upeta</i><i>)</i> abs. <i>(</i><i>upetya</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>abhi</i><i>)</i> s'approcher de, venir vers; approcher sexuellement — pr. md. <i>(upite)</i> atteindre; avoir en partage.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upek_s">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपेक्ष्</span> <i>upekṣ</i> [<a href="#entry-upa" class="skt-link">upa</a>-<a href="#entry-iik_s" class="skt-link">īkṣ</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. md. <i>(upekṣate)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>upekṣita</i><i>)</i> négliger, laisser en plan <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> percevoir; supporter, souffrir; prendre égard à, superviser; accorder.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upek_sa_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपेक्षण</span> <i>upekṣaṇa</i> [act. <a href="#entry-upek_s" class="skt-link">upekṣ</a>] <a href="#entry-upek_sa_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> laisser-aller, négligence; indifférence.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upek_saa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपेक्षा</span> <i>upekṣā</i> [<a href="#entry-upek_s" class="skt-link">upekṣ</a>] <a href="#entry-upek_saa" class="skt-link">f.</a> négligence; indifférence <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> égalité d'humeur, sérénité <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. l'équanimité, une des quatre qualités morales [<a href="#entry-brahmavihaara" class="skt-link">brahmavihāra</a>] <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pali <i>upekkhā</i>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upendra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपेन्द्र</span> <i>upendra</i> [<a href="#entry-upa" class="skt-link">upa</a>-<a href="#entry-indra" class="skt-link">indra</a>] <a href="#entry-upendra" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. d'Upendra «Cadet d'Indra», épith. de <a href="#entry-Uvi_s_nu" class="skt-link">Viṣṇu</a> en tant que dernier né d'<a href="#entry-Uaditi" class="skt-link">Aditi</a>, titre décerné par <a href="#entry-Uindra" class="skt-link">Indra</a> à <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_na" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇa</a>; on le représente enfant [<a href="#entry-baalaruupin" class="skt-link">bālarūpin</a>]; aussi épith. de <a href="#entry-Uvi_s_nu" class="skt-link">Viṣṇu</a> comme le nain <a href="#entry-Uvaamana" class="skt-link">Vāmana</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upendravajraa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>upendravajrā</i> [<a href="#entry-vajra" class="skt-link">vajra</a>] <a href="#entry-upendravajraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> phon. mètre syllabique.<br> <a href="#entry-upendra" class="skt-link">upendra</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upeta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपेत</span> <i>upeta</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-upe_1" class="skt-link">upe_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-upeta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-upeta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-upetaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>upetā</i> approché; servi par; entré dans tel état — ifc. muni de, pourvu de, doué de, possédant.<br> <i>sarvasvāmiguṇopeta</i> doué de toutes les qualités princières.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upetya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपेत्य</span> <i>upetya</i> [abs. <a href="#entry-upe_1" class="skt-link">upe_1</a>] ind. ayant approché; ayant atteint — prép. concernant.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upo_dha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपोढ</span> <i>upoḍha</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-upavah" class="skt-link">upavah</a>] a. <a href="#entry-upo_dha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-upo_dha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-upo_dhaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>upoḍhā</i> proche; prochain; avancé, amené — iic. qui va — <a href="#entry-upo_dhaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>upoḍhā</i> seconde épouse.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upo_dhatapas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>upoḍhatapas</i> [<a href="#entry-tapas" class="skt-link">tapas</a>] a. <a href="#entry-upo_dhatapas" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-upo_dhatapas" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-upo_dhatapas" class="skt-link">f.</a> qui est sur le point d'accomplir des austérités.<br> <a href="#entry-upo_dha" class="skt-link">upoḍha</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upo_s">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपोष्</span> <i>upoṣ</i> [<a href="#entry-upa" class="skt-link">upa</a>-<a href="#entry-u_s" class="skt-link">uṣ</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(upoṣati)</i> brûler.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upo_sita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपोषित</span> <i>upoṣita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-upavas" class="skt-link">upavas</a>] a. <a href="#entry-upo_sita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-upo_sita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-upo_sitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>upoṣitā</i> qui jeûne, qui a jeûné — <a href="#entry-upo_sita" class="skt-link">n.</a> jeûne.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upoddhan">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपोद्धन्</span> <i>upoddhan</i> [<a href="#entry-upa" class="skt-link">upa</a>-<a href="#entry-uddhan" class="skt-link">uddhan</a>] ca. <i>(upodghātayati)</i> parler en premier, introduire, commencer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upodghaata">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उपोद्घात</span> <i>upodghāta</i> [<a href="#entry-upoddhan" class="skt-link">upoddhan</a>] <a href="#entry-upodghaata" class="skt-link">m.</a> introduction, préface; commencement; exposition, prérequis <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit. épisode narratif intercalaire <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit. exemple, illustration; rhét. outil d'exégèse.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upta_1">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उप्त</span> <i>upta_1</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-vap_1" class="skt-link">vap_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-upta_1" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-upta_1" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-uptaa_1" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>uptā_1</i> rasé.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-upta_2">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उप्त</span> <i>upta_2</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-vap_2" class="skt-link">vap_2</a>] a. <a href="#entry-upta_2" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-upta_2" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-uptaa_2" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>uptā_2</i> semé, répandu.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ura">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उर</span> <i>ura</i> iic. de <a href="#entry-uras" class="skt-link">uras</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uraga">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>uraga</i> [<a href="#entry-ga_1" class="skt-link">ga_1</a>] <a href="#entry-uraga" class="skt-link">m.</a> serpent [«qui rampe»] <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. dragon ou esprit-serpent [<a href="#entry-naaga" class="skt-link">nāga</a>]; not. np. d'Uraga «Qui va sur la poitrine (de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a>)», le roi-dragon Uragarāja.<br> <a href="#entry-ura" class="skt-link">ura</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uras">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उरस्</span> <i>uras</i> [<a href="#entry-_r" class="skt-link">ṛ</a>] <a href="#entry-uras" class="skt-link">n.</a> poitrine — v. [<span class="skt-grammar">11</span>] pr. <i>(urasyati)</i> devenir fort.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-urasi">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उरसि</span> <i>urasi</i> [loc. <a href="#entry-uras" class="skt-link">uras</a>] iic.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-urasiloma">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>urasiloma</i> [<a href="#entry-loma" class="skt-link">loma</a>] a. <a href="#entry-urasiloma" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-urasiloma" class="skt-link">n.</a> à la poitrine velue.<br> <a href="#entry-urasi" class="skt-link">urasi</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uraska">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>uraska</i> [<a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] ifc. a. <a href="#entry-uraska" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-uraska" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-uraskaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>uraskā</i> à la poitrine ⟨iic.⟩.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-urasya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>urasya</i> [<a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-urasya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-urasya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-uraskaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>uraskā</i> concernant la poitrine; pectoral; venant de la poitrine <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> nécessitant l'usage de la poitrine <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> légitime (fils [«issu de sa propre poitrine»]); syn. <a href="#entry-aurasa" class="skt-link">aurasa</a> — <a href="#entry-urasya" class="skt-link">m.</a> sein.<br> <a href="#entry-uras" class="skt-link">uras</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-urii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उरी</span> <i>urī</i> var. <i>ūrī</i> [relié à <a href="#entry-uras" class="skt-link">uras</a>] part. indique l'assentiment, l'accord ou la promesse.<br> <i>urīkṛ</i> acquiescer, agréer; consentir, promettre, accorder.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uru">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उरु</span> <i>uru</i> a. <a href="#entry-uru" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-uru" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-urvii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>urvī</i> large, grand; étendu, spacieux; excessif; excellent — <a href="#entry-uru" class="skt-link">n.</a> l'espace, l'étendue — adv. loin, au loin; largement — <a href="#entry-urvii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>urvī</i> terre; chemin.<br> <i>uruṃ</i> <i>kṛ</i> faire de la place pour; donner une opportunité à; soulager.<br> <i>uruṃ</i> <i>vṛt</i> circonscrire, comprendre.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uru_s">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उरुष्</span> <i>uruṣ</i> [vn. <i>uru</i>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">4</span>] pr. <i>(uruṣyati)</i> distancer, s'échapper <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> mettre à distance, sauver.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-urucakri">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>urucakri</i> [<a href="#entry-cakri" class="skt-link">cakri</a>] a. <a href="#entry-urucakri" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-urucakri" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-urucakri" class="skt-link">f.</a> qui fait de grandes œuvres <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> qui soulage, qui libère (de ⟨abl.⟩).<br> <i>aṃhor</i> <i>urucakri</i> qui libère de l'angoisse.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-urugaaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>urugāya</i> [<a href="#entry-gaaya" class="skt-link">gāya</a>] <a href="#entry-urugaaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. d'Urugāya, épith. de <a href="#entry-Uvi_s_nu" class="skt-link">Viṣṇu</a>-<a href="#entry-Utrivikrama" class="skt-link">Trivikrama</a> «Qui parcourt l'Espace».
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-urukrama">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>urukrama</i> [<a href="#entry-krama" class="skt-link">krama</a>] <a href="#entry-urukrama" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. d'Urukrama, épith. de <a href="#entry-Uvi_s_nu" class="skt-link">Viṣṇu</a>-<a href="#entry-Utrivikrama" class="skt-link">Trivikrama</a> «Qui enjambe les trois mondes».
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uruu_nasa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>urūṇasa</i> [<a href="#entry-nasa" class="skt-link">nasa</a>] a. <a href="#entry-uruu_nasa" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-uruu_nasa" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-uruu_nasaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>urūṇasā</i> qui a un gros nez <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. au gros muffle (se dit des chiens de <a href="#entry-Uyama" class="skt-link">Yama</a>).<br> <a href="#entry-uru" class="skt-link">uru</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uruvizaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>uruviśā</i> [<a href="#entry-vizaa" class="skt-link">viśā</a>] <a href="#entry-uruvizaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> géo. np. d'Uruviśā l'«Excellent pays», épith. de l'Orissa.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uruvyacas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>uruvyacas</i> [<a href="#entry-vyacas" class="skt-link">vyacas</a>] <a href="#entry-uruvyacas" class="skt-link">n.</a> qui s'étend largement.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uruz_rfga">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>uruśṛṅga</i> [<a href="#entry-z_rfga" class="skt-link">śṛṅga</a>] <a href="#entry-uruz_rfga" class="skt-link">n.</a> arch. réduction de pavillon dans une toiture pyramidale; syn. <a href="#entry-afgazikhara" class="skt-link">aṅgaśikhara</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-urvaaru">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उर्वारु</span> <i>urvāru</i> [relié à <a href="#entry-urvaraa" class="skt-link">urvarā</a>] <a href="#entry-urvaaru" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-urvaaru" class="skt-link">f.</a> natu. bot. <i>Cucumis utilissimus</i>, sorte de courge, dont le fruit est le concombre; syn. <a href="#entry-karka_tii" class="skt-link">karkaṭī</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> var. <i>irvāru</i> var. <i>īrvāru</i> var. <i>ervāru</i> id.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-urvaaruka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>urvāruka</i> [<a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] <a href="#entry-urvaaruka" class="skt-link">n.</a> natu. son fruit, le concombre.<br> <a href="#entry-urvaaru" class="skt-link">urvāru</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-urvaraa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उर्वरा</span> <i>urvarā</i> [<a href="#entry-uru" class="skt-link">uru</a>-<a href="#entry-vara_2" class="skt-link">vara_2</a>] <a href="#entry-urvaraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> [«la meilleure terre»] sol fertile.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-urvazii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उर्वशी</span> <i>urvaśī</i> var. <i>ūrvaśī</i> [<a href="#entry-uru" class="skt-link">uru</a>-<a href="#entry-az_1" class="skt-link">aś_1</a>] <a href="#entry-urvazii" class="skt-link">f.</a> myth. np. de la nymphe [<a href="#entry-apsaras" class="skt-link">apsaras</a>] Urvaśī ou Ūrvaśī «l'Immense», née d'une fleur posée sur la cuisse de <a href="#entry-Unaaraaya_na" class="skt-link">Nārāyaṇa</a>; [MP.] <a href="#entry-Uindra" class="skt-link">Indra</a> ayant cherché à détourner <a href="#entry-Uvi_s_nu" class="skt-link">Viṣṇu</a> de ses austérités avec des nymphes, celui-ci produisit de sa cuisse la très belle <a href="#entry-Uurvazii" class="skt-link">Urvaśī</a>; devant sa grande beauté <a href="#entry-Umitra" class="skt-link">Mitra</a> et <a href="#entry-Uvaru_na" class="skt-link">Varuṇa</a> [<a href="#entry-Umitraavaru_nau" class="skt-link">Mitrāvaruṇau</a>] répandirent leur semence qu'elle mêla dans un pot pour donner naissance à <a href="#entry-Uagasti" class="skt-link">Agasti</a>; le reste de la semence donna naissance à <a href="#entry-Uvasi_s_tha" class="skt-link">Vasiṣṭha</a>, qui avait perdu son corps par malédiction de <a href="#entry-Unimi" class="skt-link">Nimi</a>; elle personnifie l'Aube; elle est invoquée pour obtenir le succès en amour; ses amours avec <a href="#entry-Upuruuravaa" class="skt-link">Purūravā</a>, dont elle enfanta d'<a href="#entry-Uaayu" class="skt-link">Āyu</a>, sont contées dans la <a href="#entry-Uvikramorvazii" class="skt-link">Vikramorvaśī</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-usra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उस्र</span> <i>usra</i> [<a href="#entry-u_s" class="skt-link">uṣ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-usra" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-usra" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-usraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>usrā</i> brillant; relatif à l'aube — <a href="#entry-usraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>usrā</i> l'aube <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> all. Austro; ang. Easter.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ut">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उत्</span> <i>ut</i> var. <i>uc</i>, <i>uj</i>, <i>ud</i>, <i>un</i> pf. vers le haut, hors de; échappant à, tendant à <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> supériorité sur; cessation de <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> ang. out; all. aus.<br> <i>udvikramādityam</i> adv. manifestant (le nom de) <a href="#entry-Uvikramaaditya" class="skt-link">Vikramāditya</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uta_1">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उत</span> <i>uta_1</i> conj. cl. et; en vérité (part. d'emphase ou de vœu) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> aussi, ou, même; ou bien ⟨interr.⟩.<br> <i>uta</i> <i>...</i> <i>uta</i> à la fois ... et.<br> <i>uta</i> <i>vā</i> ou même.<br> <i>kimuta</i> à plus forte raison, d'autant plus que; d'autant moins que.<br> <i>apyuta</i> aussi.<br> <i>pratyuta</i> au contraire, mais plutôt.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uta_2">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उत</span> <i>uta_2</i> var. <i>ūta</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-vaa_3" class="skt-link">vā_3</a>] a. <a href="#entry-uta_2" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-uta_2" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-utaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>utā</i> tissé.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-utathya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उतथ्य</span> <i>utathya</i> <a href="#entry-utathya" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. d'Utathya, fils d'<a href="#entry-Uafgiraa" class="skt-link">Aṅgirā</a> et de <a href="#entry-Uzraddhaa" class="skt-link">Śraddhā</a>, frère aîné de <a href="#entry-Ub_rhaspati" class="skt-link">Bṛhaspati</a>; son épouse est <a href="#entry-Umamataa" class="skt-link">Mamatā</a>, <a href="#entry-Udiirghatamaa" class="skt-link">Dīrghatamā</a> est leur fils; il enseigna le devoir royal [<a href="#entry-raajadharma" class="skt-link">rājadharma</a>] à <a href="#entry-Umaandhaataa" class="skt-link">Māndhātā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-utk_r_s">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उत्कृष्</span> <i>utkṛṣ</i> [<a href="#entry-ut" class="skt-link">ut</a>-<a href="#entry-k_r_s" class="skt-link">kṛṣ</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(utkarṣati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>utkṛṣṭa</i><i>)</i> tirer, élever; extraire, arracher — ca. <i>(utkarṣayati)</i> élever, accroître, rendre éminent.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-utk_r_s_ta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उत्कृष्ट</span> <i>utkṛṣṭa</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-utk_r_s" class="skt-link">utkṛṣ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-utk_r_s_ta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-utk_r_s_ta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-utk_r_s_taa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>utkṛṣṭā</i> extrait, arraché <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> supérieur, éminent.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-utk_rr">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उत्कॄ</span> <i>utkṝ</i> [<a href="#entry-ut" class="skt-link">ut</a>-<a href="#entry-k_rr" class="skt-link">kṝ</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">6</span>] pr. <i>(utkirati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>utkīrṇa</i><i>)</i> disperser vers le haut; entasser, empiler <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> creuser; graver.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-utk_sepa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उत्क्षेप</span> <i>utkṣepa</i> [<a href="#entry-utk_sip" class="skt-link">utkṣip</a>] <a href="#entry-utk_sepa" class="skt-link">m.</a> fait de lever, de dresser, de lancer en l'air <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> chose lancée.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-utk_sepa_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>utkṣepaṇa</i> [<a href="#entry--na" class="skt-link">-na</a>] <a href="#entry-utk_sepa_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> élévation <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. [<a href="#entry-vaize_sika" class="skt-link">vaiśeṣika</a>] l'activité [<a href="#entry-karman" class="skt-link">karman</a>] d'élévation.<br> <a href="#entry-utk_sepa" class="skt-link">utkṣepa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-utk_sip">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उत्क्षिप्</span> <i>utkṣip</i> [<a href="#entry-ut" class="skt-link">ut</a>-<a href="#entry-k_sip" class="skt-link">kṣip</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">6</span>] pr. <i>(utkṣipati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>utkṣipta</i><i>)</i> jeter en l'air; dresser, élever.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-utka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उत्क</span> <i>utka</i> [<a href="#entry-ut" class="skt-link">ut</a><a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] a. <a href="#entry-utka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-utka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-utkaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>utkā</i> qui désire ardemment <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> qui espère, qui désire ⟨inf.⟩ <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> qui pense à autre chose, absent d'esprit — <a href="#entry-utka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-utka" class="skt-link">n.</a> désir <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> opportunité, occasion — v. [<span class="skt-grammar">11</span>] pr. <i>(utkayati)</i> susciter le désir; faire regretter.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-utka_n_th">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उत्कण्ठ्</span> <i>utkaṇṭh</i> [vn. <i>utkaṇṭha</i>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. md. <i>(utkaṇṭhate)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>utkaṇṭhita</i><i>)</i> dresser le cou; désirer; avoir hâte de retrouver, être amoureux de <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> regretter ⟨g. dat.⟩; s'affliger — ca. <i>(utkaṇṭhayati)</i> exciter le désir de <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> affliger.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-utka_n_tha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उत्कण्ठ</span> <i>utkaṇṭha</i> [<a href="#entry-ut" class="skt-link">ut</a>-<a href="#entry-ka_n_tha" class="skt-link">kaṇṭha</a>] a. <a href="#entry-utka_n_tha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-utka_n_tha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-utka_n_thaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>utkaṇṭhā</i> [«qui tend le cou»] volontaire, enthousiaste; avide <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> mélancolique, regrettant l'absence d'un être cher — <a href="#entry-utka_n_thaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>utkaṇṭhā</i> vif désir, avidité; anxiété <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> tristesse, regret.<br> <i>utkaṇṭham</i> adv. avidement.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-utka_n_thita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उत्कण्ठित</span> <i>utkaṇṭhita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-utka_n_th" class="skt-link">utkaṇṭh</a>] a. <a href="#entry-utka_n_thita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-utka_n_thita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-utka_n_thitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>utkaṇṭhitā</i> qui désire, qui regrette; épris de.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-utka_ta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उत्कट</span> <i>utkaṭa</i> [<a href="#entry-ut" class="skt-link">ut</a>-<a href="#entry-ka_ta" class="skt-link">kaṭa</a>] a. <a href="#entry-utka_ta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-utka_ta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-utka_taa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>utkaṭā</i> abondamment doué de; immense, gigantesque; démesuré, excessif.<br> <i>utkaṭam</i> adv. violemment, excessivement.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-utkaca">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उत्कच</span> <i>utkaca</i> [<a href="#entry-ut" class="skt-link">ut</a>-<a href="#entry-kaca" class="skt-link">kaca</a>] a. <a href="#entry-utkaca" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-utkaca" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-utkacaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>utkacā</i> chauve.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-utkala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उत्कल</span> <i>utkala</i> <a href="#entry-utkala" class="skt-link">m.</a> géo. np. de l'Utkala, nom ancien de la partie côtière de l'Orissa; c'est l'un des cinq pays de l'Inde du Nord [<a href="#entry-pa_ncagau_da" class="skt-link">pañcagauḍa</a>] <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pl. <i>utkalās</i> ses habitants.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-utkar_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उत्कर्ण</span> <i>utkarṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-ut" class="skt-link">ut</a>-<a href="#entry-kar_na" class="skt-link">karṇa</a>] a. <a href="#entry-utkar_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-utkar_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-utkar_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>utkarṇī</i> qui a les oreilles dressées <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> qui tend l'oreille, attentif.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-utkar_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उत्कर्ष</span> <i>utkarṣa</i> [<a href="#entry-utk_r_s" class="skt-link">utkṛṣ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-utkar_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-utkar_sa" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-utkar_saa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>utkarṣā</i> supérieur, éminent; très; excessif — <a href="#entry-utkar_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> élévation, extraction <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> éminence; prospérité, abondance; vantardise.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-utkataa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>utkatā</i> [<a href="#entry--taa" class="skt-link">-tā</a>] <a href="#entry-utkataa" class="skt-link">f.</a> état de désir ardent <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> natu. bot. <i>Scindapsus officinalis</i>, philodendron, liane grimpant le long des troncs d'arbre; ses fruits, parfumés, sont utilisés en médecine; syn. <a href="#entry-gajapippalii" class="skt-link">gajapippalī</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-utka" class="skt-link">utka</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-utkhaata">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उत्खात</span> <i>utkhāta</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-utkhan" class="skt-link">utkhan</a>] a. <a href="#entry-utkhaata" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-utkhaata" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-utkhaataa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>utkhātā</i> creusé, excavé; déraciné, arraché, tiré <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> tiré, brandi (glaive) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> détruit — <a href="#entry-utkhaata" class="skt-link">n.</a> trou, cavité; terrain défoncé <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> arrachage.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-utkhan">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उत्खन्</span> <i>utkhan</i> [<a href="#entry-ut" class="skt-link">ut</a>-<a href="#entry-khan" class="skt-link">khan</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(utkhanati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>utkhāta</i><i>)</i> déterrer; excaver, creuser; arracher, déraciner, tirer hors de.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-utkhid">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उत्खिद्</span> <i>utkhid</i> [<a href="#entry-ut" class="skt-link">ut</a>-<a href="#entry-khid" class="skt-link">khid</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">6</span>] pr. <i>(utkhidati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>utkhinna</i><i>)</i> extraire.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-utkiir_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उत्कीर्ण</span> <i>utkīrṇa</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-utk_rr" class="skt-link">utkṝ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-utkiir_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-utkiir_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-utkiir_naa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>utkīrṇā</i> entassé <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> creusé; gravé.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-utkira">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उत्किर</span> <i>utkira</i> [<a href="#entry-utk_rr" class="skt-link">utkṝ</a>] ifc. a. <a href="#entry-utkira" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-utkira" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-utkiraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>utkirā</i> qui entasse; qui verse.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-utkoca">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उत्कोच</span> <i>utkoca</i> [<a href="#entry-utkuc" class="skt-link">utkuc</a>] a. <a href="#entry-utkoca" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-utkoca" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-utkocaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>utkocā</i> qui serpente — <a href="#entry-utkoca" class="skt-link">m.</a> corruption; pot de vin, argent sale.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-utkocaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उत्कोचक</span> <i>utkocaka</i> [<a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] a. <a href="#entry-utkocaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-utkocaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-utkocakaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>utkocakā</i> corrompu; qui accepte les pots de vin — <a href="#entry-utkocaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> myth. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] np. du <a href="#entry-tiirtha" class="skt-link">tīrtha</a> Utkocaka «Dont l'eau serpente»; là se trouvait l'ermitage de <a href="#entry-Udevala" class="skt-link">Devala</a> et <a href="#entry-Udhaumya" class="skt-link">Dhaumya</a>; à la suggestion de <a href="#entry-Ucitraratha" class="skt-link">Citraratha</a>, les <a href="#entry-paa_n_davaas" class="skt-link">pāṇḍavās</a> s'y rendirent pour nommer ce dernier leur chapelain.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-utkram">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उत्क्रम्</span> <i>utkram</i> [<a href="#entry-ut" class="skt-link">ut</a>-<a href="#entry-kram" class="skt-link">kram</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(utkrāmati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>utkrānta</i><i>)</i> monter à pied; s'élever; faire l'ascension de <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> faire un pas de côté; s'éloigner, s'en aller; trépasser <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> omettre, négliger; transgresser.<br> <i>urddhvaṃ</i> <i>prāṇā</i> <i>hyutkrāmanti</i> [MS.] Car les esprits vitaux sont en train de s'exhaler.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-utkrama">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उत्क्रम</span> <i>utkrama</i> [<a href="#entry-utkram" class="skt-link">utkram</a>] <a href="#entry-utkrama" class="skt-link">m.</a> ascension; sortie <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> ordre inverse; écart, déviation; transgression.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-utkrama_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>utkramaṇa</i> [<a href="#entry--na" class="skt-link">-na</a>] <a href="#entry-utkrama_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> ascension; vol <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> sortie, retour arrière <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> excès <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> fin de vie, mort.<br> <a href="#entry-utkrama" class="skt-link">utkrama</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-utkrama_niiya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उत्क्रमणीय</span> <i>utkramaṇīya</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">2</span>] <a href="#entry-utkram" class="skt-link">utkram</a>] a. <a href="#entry-utkrama_niiya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-utkrama_niiya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-utkrama_niiyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>utkramaṇīyā</i> à abandonner.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-utkuc">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उत्कुच्</span> <i>utkuc</i> [<a href="#entry-ut" class="skt-link">ut</a>-<a href="#entry-kuc" class="skt-link">kuc</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">6</span>] pr. <i>(utkucati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>utkuñcita</i><i>)</i> se courber vers le haut; s'ouvrir, s'épanouir (fleur).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-utkula">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उत्कुल</span> <i>utkula</i> [<a href="#entry-ut" class="skt-link">ut</a>-<a href="#entry-kula" class="skt-link">kula</a>] a. <a href="#entry-utkula" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-utkula" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-utkulaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>utkulā</i> rejeté de sa famille, renié, déchu; dégénéré.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-utpa_t">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उत्पट्</span> <i>utpaṭ</i> [<a href="#entry-ut" class="skt-link">ut</a>-<a href="#entry-pa_t" class="skt-link">paṭ</a>] ca. <i>(utpāṭayati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>utpāṭita</i><i>)</i> arracher; plumer <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> tirer (une épée du fourreau) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> ouvrir (les yeux); éclore (fleur).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-utpaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उत्पा</span> <i>utpā</i> [<a href="#entry-ut" class="skt-link">ut</a>-<a href="#entry-paa_1" class="skt-link">pā_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(utpibati)</i> boire jusqu'au bout, boire d'un trait.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-utpaa_ta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उत्पाट</span> <i>utpāṭa</i> [<a href="#entry-utpa_t" class="skt-link">utpaṭ</a>] <a href="#entry-utpaa_ta" class="skt-link">m.</a> arrachage.<br> <i>kīlotpāṭa</i> m. lit. [<a href="#entry-Upa_ncatantra" class="skt-link">Pañcatantra</a>] (singe imprudent) qui a arraché le coin maintenant la fente du tronc sur laquelle il est assis.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-utpaa_tana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उत्पाटन</span> <i>utpāṭana</i> [act. <a href="#entry-utpa_t" class="skt-link">utpaṭ</a>] <a href="#entry-utpaa_tana" class="skt-link">n.</a> arrachage; destruction; fait de déraciner; éradication <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> fait de bannir, de détrôner.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-utpaad">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उत्पाद्</span> <i>utpād</i> [ca. <a href="#entry-utpad" class="skt-link">utpad</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">10</span>] pr. <i>(utpādayati)</i> faire se produire, provoquer, créer, causer.<br> <i>bhagavān</i> <i>viśvam</i> <i>udapādayat</i> Dieu a créé le Monde.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-utpaada">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उत्पाद</span> <i>utpāda</i> [<a href="#entry-utpaad" class="skt-link">utpād</a>] <a href="#entry-utpaada" class="skt-link">m.</a> production; création.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-utpaadaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उत्पादक</span> <i>utpādaka</i> [<a href="#entry-utpaada" class="skt-link">utpāda</a><a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] a. <a href="#entry-utpaadaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-utpaadaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-utpaadikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>utpādikā</i> qui produit, qui engendre <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> productif; efficace — <a href="#entry-utpaadaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> géniteur; producteur.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-utpaadana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उत्पादन</span> <i>utpādana</i> [act. <a href="#entry-utpaad" class="skt-link">utpād</a>] <a href="#entry-utpaadana" class="skt-link">n.</a> procréation.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-utpaadya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उत्पाद्य</span> <i>utpādya</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] <a href="#entry-utpaad" class="skt-link">utpād</a>] a. <a href="#entry-utpaadya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-utpaadya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-utpaadyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>utpādyā</i> à produire, à créer <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> produit, créé; artificiel, fictif <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit. romanesque, relevant de la fiction.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-utpad">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उत्पद्</span> <i>utpad</i> [<a href="#entry-ut" class="skt-link">ut</a>-<a href="#entry-pad_1" class="skt-link">pad_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">4</span>] pr. md. <i>(utpadyate)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>utpanna</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>vi</i>, <i>sam</i><i>)</i> démarrer, se produire, se présenter, naître; arriver, se manifester, exister — ca. <i>(utpādayati)</i> faire se produire, provoquer, créer, causer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-utpala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उत्पल</span> <i>utpala</i> [<a href="#entry-utpa_t" class="skt-link">utpaṭ</a>] <a href="#entry-utpala" class="skt-link">n.</a> natu. (fleur du) lotus; not. fleur de bot. <i>Nymphæa nelumbo</i> ou lotus bleu <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hist. np. de <a href="#entry-Ubha_t_ta" class="skt-link">Bhaṭṭa</a> Utpala, astronome du 10⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle; cf. <a href="#entry-Ubha_t_totpala" class="skt-link">Bhaṭṭotpala</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-utpalaacaarya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>utpalācārya</i> [<a href="#entry-aacaarya" class="skt-link">ācārya</a>] <a href="#entry-utpalaacaarya" class="skt-link">m.</a> cf. <a href="#entry-utpaladeva" class="skt-link">utpaladeva</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-utpaladeva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>utpaladeva</i> [<a href="#entry-deva" class="skt-link">deva</a>] <a href="#entry-utpaladeva" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. de Utpaladeva «Seigneur du Lotus Bleu», philosophe <a href="#entry-zaivasiddhaanta" class="skt-link">śaivasiddhānta</a> du <a href="#entry-Ukazmiira" class="skt-link">Kaśmīra</a> du 10⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle; élève de <a href="#entry-Usomaananda" class="skt-link">Somānanda</a>, il fonda la doctrine de la Reconnaissance [<a href="#entry-Upratyabhij_naa" class="skt-link">Pratyabhijñā</a>]; il est l'auteur de l'<a href="#entry-Uiizvarapratyabhij_naasuutra" class="skt-link">Īśvarapratyabhijñāsūtra</a>; <a href="#entry-Uabhinavagupta" class="skt-link">Abhinavagupta</a> fut son élève; on l'appelle aussi Utpalācārya.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-utpalin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>utpalin</i> [<a href="#entry--in" class="skt-link">-in</a>] a. <a href="#entry-utpalin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-utpalin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-utpalinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>utpalinī</i> abondant en lotus — <a href="#entry-utpalinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>utpalinī</i> étang couvert de lotus.<br> <a href="#entry-utpala" class="skt-link">utpala</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-utpanna">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उत्पन्न</span> <i>utpanna</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-utpad" class="skt-link">utpad</a>] a. <a href="#entry-utpanna" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-utpanna" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-utpannaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>utpannā</i> levé, surgi; produit, manifesté.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-utpat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उत्पत्</span> <i>utpat</i> [<a href="#entry-ut" class="skt-link">ut</a>-<a href="#entry-pat_1" class="skt-link">pat_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(utpatati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>utpatita</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>sam</i><i>)</i> s'envoler; se relever.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-utpatti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उत्पत्ति</span> <i>utpatti</i> [act. <a href="#entry-utpad" class="skt-link">utpad</a>] <a href="#entry-utpatti" class="skt-link">f.</a> naissance; production, création; apparition, manifestation <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. manifestation de ce qui est créé (opp. <a href="#entry-vyakti" class="skt-link">vyakti</a> manifestation de ce qui était voilé).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-utpattiprakara_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>utpattiprakaraṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-prakara_na" class="skt-link">prakaraṇa</a>] <a href="#entry-utpattiprakara_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. np. de l'Utpattiprakaraṇa, 1⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ section du <a href="#entry-Umok_sopaaya" class="skt-link">Mokṣopāya</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-utpattisthaana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>utpattisthāna</i> [<a href="#entry-sthaana" class="skt-link">sthāna</a>] <a href="#entry-utpattisthaana" class="skt-link">n.</a> phon. position d'articulation des sons.<br> <a href="#entry-utpatti" class="skt-link">utpatti</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-utpattividhi">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>utpattividhi</i> [<a href="#entry-vidhi" class="skt-link">vidhi</a>] <a href="#entry-utpattividhi" class="skt-link">m.</a> phil. [<a href="#entry-miimaa_msaa" class="skt-link">mīmāṃsā</a>] injonction rituelle principale; elle prescrit un acte non mentionné auparavant.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-utpaz">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उत्पश्</span> <i>utpaś</i> [<a href="#entry-ut" class="skt-link">ut</a>-<a href="#entry-paz" class="skt-link">paś</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">4</span>] pr. <i>(utpaśyati)</i> regarder vers le haut.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-utpazya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उत्पश्य</span> <i>utpaśya</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-utpaz" class="skt-link">utpaś</a>] a. <a href="#entry-utpazya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-utpazya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-utpazyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>utpaśyā</i> qui regarde vers le haut.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-utphal">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उत्फल्</span> <i>utphal</i> [<a href="#entry-ut" class="skt-link">ut</a>-<a href="#entry-phal" class="skt-link">phal</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(utphalati)</i> fut. <i>(</i><i>phaliṣyati</i><i>)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>phalita</i><i>)</i> ouvrir, éclater.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-utphulla">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उत्फुल्ल</span> <i>utphulla</i> [<a href="#entry-utphal" class="skt-link">utphal</a>] a. <a href="#entry-utphulla" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-utphulla" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-utphullaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>utphullā</i> grand ouvert, éclos.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-utpiba">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उत्पिब</span> <i>utpiba</i> [<a href="#entry-ut" class="skt-link">ut</a>-<a href="#entry-piba" class="skt-link">piba</a>] a. <a href="#entry-utpiba" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-utpiba" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-utpibaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>utpibā</i> qui boit jusqu'au bout.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-utplu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उत्प्लु</span> <i>utplu</i> [<a href="#entry-ut" class="skt-link">ut</a>-<a href="#entry-plu" class="skt-link">plu</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. md. <i>(utplavate)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>utpluta</i><i>)</i> sauter, bondir.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-utprek_s">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उत्प्रेक्ष्</span> <i>utprekṣ</i> [<a href="#entry-ut" class="skt-link">ut</a>-<a href="#entry-prek_s" class="skt-link">prekṣ</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. md. <i>(utprekṣate)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>utprekṣita</i><i>)</i> regarder en levant la tête, observer, considérer <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> imaginer, deviner.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-utprek_saa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उत्प्रेक्षा</span> <i>utprekṣā</i> [<a href="#entry-utprek_s" class="skt-link">utprekṣ</a>] <a href="#entry-utprek_saa" class="skt-link">f.</a> lit. figure de style métaphorique, comparaison poétique; genre poétique de fiction merveilleuse.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-utprek_sita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उत्प्रेक्षित</span> <i>utprekṣita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-utprek_s" class="skt-link">utprekṣ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-utprek_sita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-utprek_sita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-utprek_sitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>utprekṣitā</i> imaginé.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uts_rj">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उत्सृज्</span> <i>utsṛj</i> [<a href="#entry-ut" class="skt-link">ut</a>-<a href="#entry-s_rj_1" class="skt-link">sṛj_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">6</span>] pr. <i>(utsṛjati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>utsṛṣṭa</i><i>)</i> lâcher, laisser aller, laisser s'échapper; lancer <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> rejeter, déposer; produire, répandre; vomir, exhaler <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> élever la voix.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uts_rp">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उत्सृप्</span> <i>utsṛp</i> [<a href="#entry-ut" class="skt-link">ut</a>-<a href="#entry-s_rp" class="skt-link">sṛp</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(utsarpati)</i> s'élever doucement; se lever (soleil); monter (mer).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-utsa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उत्स</span> <i>utsa</i> [<a href="#entry-und" class="skt-link">und</a>] <a href="#entry-utsa" class="skt-link">m.</a> source; fontaine; puits <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> nuage de pluie.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-utsaaha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उत्साह</span> <i>utsāha</i> [act. <a href="#entry-utsah" class="skt-link">utsah</a>] <a href="#entry-utsaaha" class="skt-link">m.</a> énergie, force; détermination, résolution; enthousiasme <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. le courage, une émotion de base [<a href="#entry-sthaayibhaava" class="skt-link">sthāyibhāva</a>].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-utsaahin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उत्साहिन्</span> <i>utsāhin</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-utsah" class="skt-link">utsah</a>] <a href="#entry-utsaahin" class="skt-link">m.</a> enthousiaste.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-utsad">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उत्सद्</span> <i>utsad</i> [<a href="#entry-ut" class="skt-link">ut</a>-<a href="#entry-sad_1" class="skt-link">sad_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(utsīdati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>utsanna</i><i>)</i> s'élever <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> disparaître — ca. <i>(utsādayati)</i> enlever, supprimer, détruire.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-utsafga">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उत्सङ्ग</span> <i>utsaṅga</i> [<a href="#entry-ut" class="skt-link">ut</a>-<a href="#entry-safga" class="skt-link">saṅga</a>] <a href="#entry-utsafga" class="skt-link">m.</a> giron, hanche <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pente, talus; terrasse.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-utsah">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उत्सह्</span> <i>utsah</i> [<a href="#entry-ut" class="skt-link">ut</a>-<a href="#entry-sah_1" class="skt-link">sah_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. md. <i>(utsahate)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>utsoḍha</i><i>)</i> endurer, supporter; agir courageusement.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-utsaha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उत्सह</span> <i>utsaha</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-utsah" class="skt-link">utsah</a>] ifc. a. <a href="#entry-utsaha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-utsaha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-utsahaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>utsahā</i> qui supporte, qui endure.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-utsanna">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उत्सन्न</span> <i>utsanna</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-utsad" class="skt-link">utsad</a>] a. <a href="#entry-utsanna" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-utsanna" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-utsannaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>utsannā</i> élevé <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> aboli, supprimé.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-utsarga">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उत्सर्ग</span> <i>utsarga</i> [<a href="#entry-uts_rj" class="skt-link">utsṛj</a>] <a href="#entry-utsarga" class="skt-link">m.</a> rejet, excrément; cessation, abandon <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> production; offrande, don.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-utsargasuutra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>utsargasūtra</i> [<a href="#entry-suutra" class="skt-link">sūtra</a>] <a href="#entry-utsargasuutra" class="skt-link">n.</a> gram. règle générale, exprimant une régularité (opp. <a href="#entry-apavaadasuutra" class="skt-link">apavādasūtra</a>, <a href="#entry-vize_sasuutra" class="skt-link">viśeṣasūtra</a>); construction productive.<br> <a href="#entry-utsarga" class="skt-link">utsarga</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-utsarjana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उत्सर्जन</span> <i>utsarjana</i> [<a href="#entry-uts_rj" class="skt-link">utsṛj</a>] <a href="#entry-utsarjana" class="skt-link">n.</a> abandon, cessation; suspension de la lecture du <a href="#entry-Uveda" class="skt-link">Veda</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. fin des études védiques; cf. <a href="#entry-upaakarman" class="skt-link">upākarman</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-utsarpa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उत्सर्प</span> <i>utsarpa</i> [<a href="#entry-uts_rp" class="skt-link">utsṛp</a>] <a href="#entry-utsarpa" class="skt-link">m.</a> qui s'élève doucement.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-utsarpa_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>utsarpaṇa</i> [<a href="#entry--na" class="skt-link">-na</a>] <a href="#entry-utsarpa_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> fait de s'élever; marée montante.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-utsarpin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>utsarpin</i> [<a href="#entry--in" class="skt-link">-in</a>] a. <a href="#entry-utsarpin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-utsarpin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-utsarpi_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>utsarpiṇī</i> qui s'élève, qui enfle — <a href="#entry-utsarpi_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>utsarpiṇī</i> jn. cycle ascendant, période cosmique d'élévation graduelle; opp. <a href="#entry-avasarpi_nii" class="skt-link">avasarpiṇī</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-utsarpa" class="skt-link">utsarpa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-utsava">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उत्सव</span> <i>utsava</i> [<a href="#entry-ut" class="skt-link">ut</a>-<a href="#entry-sava_2" class="skt-link">sava_2</a>] <a href="#entry-utsava" class="skt-link">m.</a> entreprise, commencement <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> fête, jour de fête, festival; joie, bonheur <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. festival associé à un temple <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> élévation; exhubérance, passion.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-utsavamuurti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>utsavamūrti</i> [<a href="#entry-muurti" class="skt-link">mūrti</a>] <a href="#entry-utsavamuurti" class="skt-link">f.</a> soc. statue mobile de temple; cf. <a href="#entry-calantii" class="skt-link">calantī</a> <a href="#entry-pratimaa_2" class="skt-link">pratimā_2</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-utsava" class="skt-link">utsava</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-utseka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उत्सेक</span> <i>utseka</i> [<a href="#entry-utsic" class="skt-link">utsic</a>] <a href="#entry-utseka" class="skt-link">m.</a> surabondance; démesure, orgueil.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-utsekin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>utsekin</i> [<a href="#entry--in" class="skt-link">-in</a>] a. <a href="#entry-utsekin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-utsekin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-utsekinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>utsekinī</i> orgueilleux, qui tire vanité de; excessif.<br> <a href="#entry-utseka" class="skt-link">utseka</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-utsic">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उत्सिच्</span> <i>utsic</i> [<a href="#entry-ut" class="skt-link">ut</a>-<a href="#entry-sic" class="skt-link">sic</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">6</span>] pr. <i>(utsiñcati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>utsikta</i><i>)</i> déborder en versant — ps. <i>(utsicyate)</i> monter (liquide); déborder <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> s'enfler d'orgueil.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-utsidh">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उत्सिध्</span> <i>utsidh</i> [<a href="#entry-ut" class="skt-link">ut</a>-<a href="#entry-sidh_2" class="skt-link">sidh_2</a>] v. [inusité sans pf.].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-utsikta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उत्सिक्त</span> <i>utsikta</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-utsic" class="skt-link">utsic</a>] a. <a href="#entry-utsikta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-utsikta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-utsiktaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>utsiktā</i> débordant de, rendu orgueilleux par.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-utsuka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उत्सुक</span> <i>utsuka</i> [<a href="#entry-ut" class="skt-link">ut</a>-<a href="#entry-su_1" class="skt-link">su_1</a><a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] a. <a href="#entry-utsuka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-utsuka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-utsukaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>utsukā</i> inquiet, anxieux, mélancolique <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> désireux de, préoccupé de, tendu vers ⟨i. loc.⟩.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-utsuura">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उत्सूर</span> <i>utsūra</i> [<a href="#entry-ut" class="skt-link">ut</a>-<a href="#entry-suura" class="skt-link">sūra</a>] <a href="#entry-utsuura" class="skt-link">m.</a> temps du soleil couchant.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-utsvana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उत्स्वन</span> <i>utsvana</i> [<a href="#entry-ut" class="skt-link">ut</a>-<a href="#entry-svana" class="skt-link">svana</a>] <a href="#entry-utsvana" class="skt-link">m.</a> son aigu.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-utt_rr">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उत्तॄ</span> <i>uttṝ</i> [<a href="#entry-ut" class="skt-link">ut</a>-<a href="#entry-t_rr" class="skt-link">tṝ</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(uttarati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>uttīrṇa</i><i>)</i> traverser, émerger de, sortir de, surmonter; échapper à; atteindre <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> vaincre; décharger, abandonner; débarquer — ca. <i>(uttārayati)</i> faire sortir de, extirper de <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> délivrer, secourir <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> vomir.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uttaana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उत्तान</span> <i>uttāna</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-uttan" class="skt-link">uttan</a>] a. <a href="#entry-uttaana" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-uttaana" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-uttaanaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>uttānā</i> déployé, ouvert; étendu sur le dos; concave <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> [tendu] prêt à, porté à.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uttaanapaada">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>uttānapāda</i> [<a href="#entry-paada" class="skt-link">pāda</a>] <a href="#entry-uttaanapaada" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. d'Uttānapāda «Géant», épith. de <a href="#entry-Upriyavrata" class="skt-link">Priyavrata</a>, fils de <a href="#entry-Umanu" class="skt-link">Manu</a> <a href="#entry-Usvaayambhuva" class="skt-link">Svāyaṃbhuva</a>; il est père de <a href="#entry-Udhruva" class="skt-link">Dhruva</a> par <a href="#entry-Usuniiti" class="skt-link">Sunīti</a> et d'<a href="#entry-Uuttama" class="skt-link">Uttama</a> par <a href="#entry-Usuruci" class="skt-link">Suruci</a>; il personnifie l'étoile Kochab (β de la Petite Ourse).<br> <a href="#entry-uttaana" class="skt-link">uttāna</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uttaapita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उत्तापित</span> <i>uttāpita</i> [ca. pp. <a href="#entry-uttap" class="skt-link">uttap</a>] a. <a href="#entry-uttaapita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-uttaapita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-uttaapitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>uttāpitā</i> chauffé, réchauffé; brûlant <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> excité; incité.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uttaar">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उत्तार्</span> <i>uttār</i> [ca. <a href="#entry-utt_rr" class="skt-link">uttṝ</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">10</span>] pr. <i>(uttārayati)</i> abs. <i>(</i><i>uttarya</i><i>)</i> faire sortir de, extirper de <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> délivrer, secourir <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> vomir.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uttaara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उत्तार</span> <i>uttāra</i> [<a href="#entry-uttaar" class="skt-link">uttār</a>] <a href="#entry-uttaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> traversée; arrivée, accostage; délivrance, survie <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> décharge; vomissement.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uttaara_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>uttāraṇa</i> [<a href="#entry--na" class="skt-link">-na</a>] a. <a href="#entry-uttaara_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-uttaara_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-uttaara_naa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>uttāraṇā</i> qui sort de; qui traverse — <a href="#entry-uttaara_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> sortie hors de (not. l'eau); fait d'accoster, de débarquer.<br> <a href="#entry-uttaara" class="skt-link">uttāra</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uttaarya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उत्तार्य</span> <i>uttārya</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] <a href="#entry-uttaar" class="skt-link">uttār</a>] a. <a href="#entry-uttaarya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-uttaarya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-uttaaryaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>uttāryā</i> à faire accoster; à éjecter; à délivrer; à vomir — ind. ayant délivré, etc.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uttabhita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उत्तभित</span> <i>uttabhita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-uttambh" class="skt-link">uttambh</a>] a. <a href="#entry-uttabhita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-uttabhita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-uttabhitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>uttabhitā</i> supporté, soutenu.<br> <i>satyena</i> <i>uttabhitā</i> <i>bhūmiḥ</i> [RV.] La Terre est soutenue par la vérité.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uttafka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उत्तङ्क</span> <i>uttaṅka</i> <a href="#entry-uttafka" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] np. du <a href="#entry-_r_si" class="skt-link">ṛṣi</a> Uttaṅka, brâhmane compagnon d'études védiques de <a href="#entry-Ujanamejaya" class="skt-link">Janamejaya</a>; <a href="#entry-Ugautama" class="skt-link">Gautama</a> était son <a href="#entry-guru" class="skt-link">guru</a>, et le choyait; il resta à son service jusqu'à un grand âge; alors <a href="#entry-Ugautama" class="skt-link">Gautama</a> lui redonna sa jeunesse, et offrit sa fille comme épouse; afin de s'acquitter de ses études [<a href="#entry-gurudak_si_naa" class="skt-link">gurudakṣiṇā</a>], il voulut offrir à l'épouse de <a href="#entry-Ugautama" class="skt-link">Gautama</a> les boucles d'oreilles magiques de la reine, qui protégeaient de la faim, de la soif, et de tous les dangers; suivant les versions, le roi est Pauṣya ou <a href="#entry-Usaudaasa" class="skt-link">Saudāsa</a>; en chemin vers le roi, un homme l'obligea à manger les excréments d'un bovin, et il ne se purifia pas correctement; seulement après purification il put rencontrer la reine, qui lui donna ses bijoux, convoités par les dragons [<a href="#entry-naagaas" class="skt-link">nāgās</a>]; leur roi <a href="#entry-Utak_saka" class="skt-link">Takṣaka</a>, déguisé en renonçant bouddhiste, les lui vola; avec l'aide d'<a href="#entry-Uindra" class="skt-link">Indra</a> il creusa jusqu'aux enfers [<a href="#entry-Upaataala" class="skt-link">Pātāla</a>] pour le retrouver; il y trouva un cavalier [<a href="#entry-Uindra" class="skt-link">Indra</a>] qui lui dit de souffler dans l'anus de son cheval (<a href="#entry-Uagni" class="skt-link">Agni</a> déguisé) qui émit des flammes et enfuma les serpents, jusqu'à ce qu'ils rendent les joyaux; il poussa <a href="#entry-Ujanamejaya" class="skt-link">Janamejaya</a> à faire son grand sacrifice de serpents, en lui apprenant que <a href="#entry-Utak_saka" class="skt-link">Takṣaka</a> avait tué son père <a href="#entry-Upariik_sit" class="skt-link">Parīkṣit</a>; après la grande guerre, <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_na" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇa</a> retournant à <a href="#entry-Udvaarakaa" class="skt-link">Dvārakā</a> le rencontra; au récit des massacres, il blâma <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_na" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇa</a> de n'avoir pas su rétablir la paix; celui-ci lui montra sa théophanie, et il se prosterna en demandant comme bénédiction de ne jamais manquer d'eau; plus tard, assoiffé en traversant un désert, il invoqua ce don, et un homme de basse caste [<a href="#entry-caa_n_daala" class="skt-link">cāṇḍāla</a>] se présenta avec de l'eau, qu'il refusa; <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_na" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇa</a> l'humilia en lui révélant que l'homme était <a href="#entry-Uindra" class="skt-link">Indra</a> déguisé lui offrant le nectar d'immortalité contre son gré; puis il fit un miracle, en créant un nuage de pluie dans le désert.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uttafkamegha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>uttaṅkamegha</i> [<a href="#entry-megha" class="skt-link">megha</a>] <a href="#entry-uttafkamegha" class="skt-link">m.</a> nuage de pluie dans le désert.<br> <a href="#entry-uttafka" class="skt-link">uttaṅka</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uttama">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उत्तम</span> <i>uttama</i> [super. <a href="#entry-ut" class="skt-link">ut</a>] a. <a href="#entry-uttama" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-uttama" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-uttamaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>uttamā</i> le plus haut, le principal <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> suprême, extrême, supérieur, éminent; excellent, très beau, très bien <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> dernier, final <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> supérieur à ⟨g.⟩ — ifc. forme des superlatifs: le plus, le suprême <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> le chef des, le meilleur des — <a href="#entry-uttama" class="skt-link">m.</a> gram. première personne (listée en dernier en sanskrit) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. d'Uttama le Suprême, fils de <a href="#entry-Upriyavrata" class="skt-link">Priyavrata</a>-<a href="#entry-Uuttaanapaada" class="skt-link">Uttānapāda</a> et de <a href="#entry-Usuruci" class="skt-link">Suruci</a>, (demi) frère de <a href="#entry-Udhruva" class="skt-link">Dhruva</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-Uauttami" class="skt-link">Auttami</a>.<br> <i>uttamam</i> au plus haut degré.<br> <i>sarveṣāṃ</i> <i>puruṣāṇāṃ</i> <i>rāma</i> <i>uttamaḥ</i> <a href="#entry-Uraama" class="skt-link">Rāma</a> est le meilleur parmi tous les hommes.<br> <i>vidyāyā</i> <i>buddhir</i> <i>uttamā</i> [PT.] L'intelligence est supérieure à la science.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uttamaadhama">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>uttamādhama</i> [<a href="#entry-adhama" class="skt-link">adhama</a>] a. <a href="#entry-uttamaadhama" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-uttamaadhama" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-uttamaadhamaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>uttamādhamā</i> haut et bas; meilleur et pire.
