  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uttamaadhamamadhyama">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>uttamādhamamadhyama</i> [<a href="#entry-madhyama" class="skt-link">madhyama</a>] a. <a href="#entry-uttamaadhamamadhyama" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-uttamaadhamamadhyama" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-uttamaadhamamadhyamaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>uttamādhamamadhyamā</i> le meilleur et le pire et l'intermédiaire; du plus haut au plus bas.<br> <a href="#entry-uttamaadhama" class="skt-link">uttamādhama</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uttamagandha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>uttamagandha</i> [<a href="#entry-gandha" class="skt-link">gandha</a>] <a href="#entry-uttamagandha" class="skt-link">m.</a> fragrance, odeur suave.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uttamagandhaa_dhya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>uttamagandhāḍhya</i> [<a href="#entry-aa_dhya" class="skt-link">āḍhya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-uttamagandhaa_dhya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-uttamagandhaa_dhya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-uttamagandhaa_dhyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>uttamagandhāḍhyā</i> qui exhale de suaves parfums.<br> <a href="#entry-uttamagandha" class="skt-link">uttamagandha</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uttamapuru_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>uttamapuruṣa</i> [<a href="#entry-puru_sa" class="skt-link">puruṣa</a>] <a href="#entry-uttamapuru_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> gram. 1⟨sup⟩re⟨/sup⟩ pers. (listée en dernier en sanskrit).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uttamaujas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>uttamaujas</i> [<a href="#entry-ojas" class="skt-link">ojas</a>] <a href="#entry-uttamaujas" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] np. d'Uttamaujas «de grande valeur», guerrier <a href="#entry-Upa_ncaala" class="skt-link">Pañcāla</a> qui protégeait les roues du char d'<a href="#entry-Uarjuna" class="skt-link">Arjuna</a> avec son frère <a href="#entry-Uyudhaamanyu" class="skt-link">Yudhāmanyu</a>; ils périrent tous deux dans l'incendie du camp après la victoire.<br> <a href="#entry-uttama" class="skt-link">uttama</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uttambh">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उत्तम्भ्</span> <i>uttambh</i> [<a href="#entry-ut" class="skt-link">ut</a>-<a href="#entry-stambh" class="skt-link">stambh</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">9</span>] pr. <i>(uttabhnāti)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>uttabhita</i><i>)</i> soutenir, supporter — ca. <i>(uttambhayati)</i> soulever, élever, ériger <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> honorer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uttan">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उत्तन्</span> <i>uttan</i> [<a href="#entry-ut" class="skt-link">ut</a>-<a href="#entry-tan_1" class="skt-link">tan_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">8</span>] pr. <i>(uttanoti)</i> pr. md. <i>(uttanute)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>uttata</i><i>)</i> s'étendre vers le haut, s'étirer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uttap">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उत्तप्</span> <i>uttap</i> [<a href="#entry-ut" class="skt-link">ut</a>-<a href="#entry-tap" class="skt-link">tap</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(uttapati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>uttapta</i><i>)</i> chauffer, brûler; tourmenter, faire souffrir — pr. md. <i>(uttapate)</i> se réchauffer; être brûlant — ca. <i>(uttāpayati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>uttāpita</i><i>)</i> réchauffer, chauffer; exciter.<br> <i>uttapate</i> <i>raviḥ</i> le soleil brille.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uttapta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उत्तप्त</span> <i>uttapta</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-uttap" class="skt-link">uttap</a>] a. <a href="#entry-uttapta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-uttapta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-uttaptaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>uttaptā</i> brûlé; chauffé au rouge, brûlant <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> tourmenté, brûlé au fer rouge — <a href="#entry-uttapta" class="skt-link">n.</a> grande chaleur.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uttara_1">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उत्तर</span> <i>uttara_1</i> [compar. <a href="#entry-ut" class="skt-link">ut</a>] a. <a href="#entry-uttara_1" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-uttara_1" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-uttaraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>uttarā</i> plus élevé, supérieur; opp. <a href="#entry-adhara" class="skt-link">adhara</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> septentrional <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> postérieur, futur; dernier; en plus; opp. <a href="#entry-puurva" class="skt-link">pūrva</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> meilleur, plus important; vainqueur <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] np. du prince Uttara «Vainqueur», fils cadet mais préféré du roi <a href="#entry-Uviraa_ta" class="skt-link">Virāṭa</a>; il est frivole et peu hardi, bien qu'il soit paré du titre de <a href="#entry-Ubhuumi_njaya" class="skt-link">Bhūmiñjaya</a> et qu'il se vante d'être l'égal d'<a href="#entry-Uarjuna" class="skt-link">Arjuna</a>; lorsque les <a href="#entry-Ukauravaas" class="skt-link">Kauravās</a> firent la razzia du bétail de <a href="#entry-Uviraa_ta" class="skt-link">Virāṭa</a>, alors que l'armée était partie combattre les <a href="#entry-Utrigartaas" class="skt-link">Trigartās</a>, il se trouva seul au harem, et prétexta pour ne pas avoir à combattre de ne pas avoir de conducteur de char; la coiffeuse <a href="#entry-Umaalinii_1" class="skt-link">Mālinī_1</a> (<a href="#entry-Udraupadii" class="skt-link">Draupadī</a>) le persuada de laisser le travesti <a href="#entry-Ub_rhanna_daa" class="skt-link">Bṛhannaḍā</a> (<a href="#entry-Uarjuna" class="skt-link">Arjuna</a>) conduire son char; celui-ci mit en déroute l'armée des <a href="#entry-Ukauravaas" class="skt-link">Kauravās</a>; guerrier [<a href="#entry-ratha" class="skt-link">ratha</a>] des <a href="#entry-matsyaas" class="skt-link">matsyās</a>, il est au service des <a href="#entry-paa_n_davaas" class="skt-link">pāṇḍavās</a> dans la grande guerre; monté sur un éléphant, il combattit le roi <a href="#entry-Uzalya" class="skt-link">Śalya</a> mais fut percé par son javelot — ifc. suivi de; formé de <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gram. forme des comparatifs — <a href="#entry-uttara_1" class="skt-link">n.</a> le Nord <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> la gauche <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> réponse, résultat <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gram. dernier élément d'une phrase de salutation <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit. np. de la partie [<a href="#entry-kha_n_da" class="skt-link">khaṇḍa</a>] <a href="#entry-Uuttara" class="skt-link">Uttara</a> d'un <a href="#entry-puraa_na" class="skt-link">purāṇa</a> traitant de métaphysique — acc. <i>uttaram</i> adv. finalement, en dernier — f. cf. <a href="#entry-uttaraa" class="skt-link">uttarā</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> ang. outer.<br> <i>uttareṇa</i> adv. au Nord — prép. au nord de ⟨acc. g. abl. iic.⟩.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uttara_2">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उत्तर</span> <i>uttara_2</i> [<a href="#entry-utt_rr" class="skt-link">uttṝ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-uttara_2" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-uttara_2" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-uttaraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>uttarā</i> qui traverse.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uttaraa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उत्तरा</span> <i>uttarā</i> [f. <a href="#entry-uttara_1" class="skt-link">uttara_1</a>] <a href="#entry-uttaraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> la direction du Nord <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. d'Uttarā «la Nordique», fille du roi <a href="#entry-Uviraa_ta" class="skt-link">Virāṭa</a>, épouse d'<a href="#entry-Uabhimanyu" class="skt-link">Abhimanyu</a> dont elle enfanta de <a href="#entry-Upariik_sit" class="skt-link">Parīkṣit</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uttaraa_saa_dhaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>uttarāṣāḍhā</i> [<a href="#entry-a_saa_dhaa" class="skt-link">aṣāḍhā</a>] <a href="#entry-uttaraa_saa_dhaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> pl. astr. np. d'Uttarāṣāḍhās «les Invincibles ultérieures», 19⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ astérisme lunaire [<a href="#entry-nak_satra" class="skt-link">nakṣatra</a>], corr. à l'étoile σ du Sagittaire; les <a href="#entry-Uvizvedevaas" class="skt-link">Viśvedevās</a> y président; son symbole est un lit de repos.<br> <a href="#entry-uttara_1" class="skt-link">uttara_1</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uttaraakha_n_da">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>uttarākhaṇḍa</i> [<a href="#entry-akha_n_da" class="skt-link">akhaṇḍa</a>] <a href="#entry-uttaraakha_n_da" class="skt-link">m.</a> géo. np. de l'Uttarākhaṇḍa, région du nord de l'Inde, partie moyenne de l'<a href="#entry-Uhimaalaya" class="skt-link">Himālaya</a>, à l'ouest du Népal; c'est une région d'importants pélerinages.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uttaraamnaaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>uttarāmnāya</i> [<a href="#entry-aamnaaya" class="skt-link">āmnāya</a>] <a href="#entry-uttaraamnaaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. «doctrine du Nord», ordre de <a href="#entry-Uzafkara_1" class="skt-link">Śaṅkara_1</a> dont le <a href="#entry-jyotirma_tha" class="skt-link">jyotirmaṭha</a> se trouve à <a href="#entry-Ubadariinaatha" class="skt-link">Badarīnātha</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uttaraapatha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>uttarāpatha</i> [<a href="#entry-patha" class="skt-link">patha</a>] <a href="#entry-uttaraapatha" class="skt-link">n.</a> géo. la route du Nord, qui reliait le <a href="#entry-Ugandhaara" class="skt-link">Gandhāra</a> à <a href="#entry-Upaa_taliputra" class="skt-link">Pāṭaliputra</a>; le pays du Nord qu'elle traversait.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uttaraardha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>uttarārdha</i> [<a href="#entry-ardha" class="skt-link">ardha</a>] <a href="#entry-uttaraardha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-uttaraardha" class="skt-link">n.</a> partie supérieure (du corps); partie postérieure (opp. <a href="#entry-puurvaardha" class="skt-link">pūrvārdha</a>) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> côté Nord.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uttaraardhya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>uttarārdhya</i> [<a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-uttaraardhya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-uttaraardhya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-uttaraardhyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>uttarārdhyā</i> du côté Nord.<br> <a href="#entry-uttaraardha" class="skt-link">uttarārdha</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uttaraartha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>uttarārtha</i> [<a href="#entry-artha" class="skt-link">artha</a>] <a href="#entry-uttaraartha" class="skt-link">m.</a> gram. sens du second terme d'un composé — a. <a href="#entry-uttaraartha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-uttaraartha" class="skt-link">n.</a> gram. qui concerne les règles [<a href="#entry-suutra" class="skt-link">sūtra</a>] ultérieures.<br> <i>uttarārthapradhāna</i> gram. qui a pour notion prépondérante son second membre (se dit d'un composé <a href="#entry-tatpuru_sa" class="skt-link">tatpuruṣa</a>).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uttaraasafga">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>uttarāsaṅga</i> [<a href="#entry-safga" class="skt-link">saṅga</a>] <a href="#entry-uttaraasafga" class="skt-link">m.</a> bd. robe de moine; cf. <a href="#entry-triciivara" class="skt-link">tricīvara</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uttaraaya_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>uttarāyaṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-ayana" class="skt-link">ayana</a>] <a href="#entry-uttaraaya_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> astr. période (favorable) entre le solstice d'hiver et le solstice d'été (où le soleil paraît se déplacer vers le Nord, et les jours rallongent); solstice d'été; syn. <a href="#entry-udagayana" class="skt-link">udagayana</a>; opp. <a href="#entry-dak_si_naayana" class="skt-link">dakṣiṇāyana</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uttarabhaadrapadaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>uttarabhādrapadā</i> [<a href="#entry-bhaadrapadaa" class="skt-link">bhādrapadā</a>] <a href="#entry-uttarabhaadrapadaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> pl. astr. np. du <a href="#entry-nak_satra" class="skt-link">nakṣatra</a> Uttarabhādrapadās «les Pieds arrière (du lit) de bon augure»; cf. <a href="#entry-Uuttarapro_s_thapadaas" class="skt-link">Uttaraproṣṭhapadās</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uttarabhaaga">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>uttarabhāga</i> [<a href="#entry-bhaaga" class="skt-link">bhāga</a>] <a href="#entry-uttarabhaaga" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. np. de l'Uttarabhāga, deuxième partie de la <a href="#entry-Ukaadambarii" class="skt-link">Kādambarī</a>, complétée par <a href="#entry-Ubhuu_sa_na" class="skt-link">Bhūṣaṇa</a>, fils de <a href="#entry-Ubaa_na_2" class="skt-link">Bāṇa_2</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uttaradaayaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>uttaradāyaka</i> [<a href="#entry-daayaka" class="skt-link">dāyaka</a>] a. <a href="#entry-uttaradaayaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-uttaradaayaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-uttaradaayikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>uttaradāyikā</i> qui répond; qui contredit; impertinent.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uttaragiitaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>uttaragītā</i> [<a href="#entry-giitaa" class="skt-link">gītā</a>] <a href="#entry-uttaragiitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> lit. np. de l'Uttaragītā, texte où <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_na" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇa</a> enseigne la nature du <a href="#entry-brahman" class="skt-link">brahman</a> à <a href="#entry-Uarjuna" class="skt-link">Arjuna</a>; il est considéré comme supplément au <a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uttarakaa_n_da">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>uttarakāṇḍa</i> [<a href="#entry-kaa_n_da" class="skt-link">kāṇḍa</a>] <a href="#entry-uttarakaa_n_da" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. dernier chapitre; not. du <a href="#entry-Uraamaaya_na" class="skt-link">Rām.</a>, du <a href="#entry-Upadmapuraa_na" class="skt-link">Padmapurāṇa</a>, du <a href="#entry-Uzivapuraa_na" class="skt-link">Śivapurāṇa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uttarakaala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>uttarakāla</i> [<a href="#entry-kaala_1" class="skt-link">kāla_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-uttarakaala" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-uttarakaala" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-uttarakaalaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>uttarakālā</i> ultérieur, futur; postérieur à ⟨iic.⟩ — <a href="#entry-uttarakaala" class="skt-link">m.</a> temps futur <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> astr. temps compté à partir de la pleine lune.<br> <i>uttarakālam</i> plus tard, après.<br> <i>uttarakālatas</i> id.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uttarakaazi">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>uttarakāśi</i> [<a href="#entry-kaazi" class="skt-link">kāśi</a>] <a href="#entry-uttarakaazi" class="skt-link">f.</a> géo. np. d'Uttarakāśi «<a href="#entry-Ukaazi" class="skt-link">Kāśi</a> du Nord», ville sacrée sur les bords de la <a href="#entry-Ubhaagiirathii" class="skt-link">Bhāgīrathī</a>, dans l'<a href="#entry-Uuttaraakha_n_da" class="skt-link">Uttarākhaṇḍa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uttarakuru">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>uttarakuru</i> [<a href="#entry-kuru" class="skt-link">kuru</a>] <a href="#entry-uttarakuru" class="skt-link">m.</a> pl. nom d'un peuple et d'un pays nordique (Tibet, Assam?).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uttaraloman">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>uttaraloman</i> [<a href="#entry-loman" class="skt-link">loman</a>] a. <a href="#entry-uttaraloman" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-uttaraloman" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-uttaraloman" class="skt-link">f.</a> qui a le poil dressé.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uttaramantrin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>uttaramantrin</i> [<a href="#entry-mantrin" class="skt-link">mantrin</a>] <a href="#entry-uttaramantrin" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. premier ministre d'un roi.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uttaramiimaa_msaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>uttaramīmāṃsā</i> [<a href="#entry-miimaa_msaa" class="skt-link">mīmāṃsā</a>] <a href="#entry-uttaramiimaa_msaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> phil. syn. <a href="#entry-vedaanta" class="skt-link">vedānta</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uttaranaaraaya_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>uttaranārāyaṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-naaraaya_na" class="skt-link">nārāyaṇa</a>] <a href="#entry-uttaranaaraaya_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. deuxième partie de l'hymne au <a href="#entry-Upuru_sa" class="skt-link">Puruṣa</a> [<a href="#entry-puru_sasuukta" class="skt-link">puruṣasūkta</a>].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uttarapada">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>uttarapada</i> [<a href="#entry-pada" class="skt-link">pada</a>] <a href="#entry-uttarapada" class="skt-link">n.</a> gram. second terme d'un composé [<a href="#entry-samaasa" class="skt-link">samāsa</a>]; opp. <a href="#entry-puurvapada" class="skt-link">pūrvapada</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uttarapadaartha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>uttarapadārtha</i> [<a href="#entry-artha" class="skt-link">artha</a>] <a href="#entry-uttarapadaartha" class="skt-link">m.</a> gram. sens du second terme d'un composé.<br> <i>uttarapadārthapradhāna</i> cf. <a href="#entry-uttarapadapradhaana" class="skt-link">uttarapadapradhāna</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-uttarapada" class="skt-link">uttarapada</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uttarapadalopa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>uttarapadalopa</i> [<a href="#entry-lopa" class="skt-link">lopa</a>] <a href="#entry-uttarapadalopa" class="skt-link">m.</a> gram. chute du second membre d'un composé.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uttarapadalopin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>uttarapadalopin</i> [<a href="#entry-lopin" class="skt-link">lopin</a>] a. <a href="#entry-uttarapadalopin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-uttarapadalopin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-uttarapadalopinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>uttarapadalopinī</i> gram. se dit d'un composé dont le second membre est sous-entendu; par ex. <a href="#entry-zaakapaarthiva" class="skt-link">śākapārthiva</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uttarapadapradhaana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>uttarapadapradhāna</i> [<a href="#entry-pradhaana" class="skt-link">pradhāna</a>] <a href="#entry-uttarapadapradhaana" class="skt-link">n.</a> gram. primatie sémantique du second terme d'un composé.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uttarapak_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>uttarapakṣa</i> [<a href="#entry-pak_sa" class="skt-link">pakṣa</a>] <a href="#entry-uttarapak_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> côté au Nord; côté gauche <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> thèse réfutant l'antithèse; réfutation; opp. <a href="#entry-puurvapak_sa" class="skt-link">pūrvapakṣa</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> bon argument; thèse valide; conclusion.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uttarapak_sin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>uttarapakṣin</i> [<a href="#entry-pak_sin" class="skt-link">pakṣin</a>] a. <a href="#entry-uttarapak_sin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-uttarapak_sin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-uttarapak_si_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>uttarapakṣiṇī</i> tenant de l'<a href="#entry-uttarapak_sa" class="skt-link">uttarapakṣa</a>; répondant.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uttarapatha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>uttarapatha</i> [<a href="#entry-patha" class="skt-link">patha</a>] <a href="#entry-uttarapatha" class="skt-link">m.</a> géo. le nord de l'Inde <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hist. la route du Nord de l'empire d'<a href="#entry-Uazoka" class="skt-link">Aśoka</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uttarapau_n_dra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>uttarapauṇḍra</i> [<a href="#entry-pau_n_dra" class="skt-link">pauṇḍra</a>] <a href="#entry-uttarapau_n_dra" class="skt-link">m.</a> géo. Nord-Bengale.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uttarapau_n_drakha_n_da">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>uttarapauṇḍrakhaṇḍa</i> [<a href="#entry-kha_n_da" class="skt-link">khaṇḍa</a>] <a href="#entry-uttarapau_n_drakha_n_da" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. np. de l'Uttarapauṇḍrakhaṇḍa, comprenant le <a href="#entry-Upau_n_drak_setramaahaatmya" class="skt-link">Pauṇḍrakṣetramāhātmya</a> (60 vers) et le <a href="#entry-Ukaratoyaamaahaatmya" class="skt-link">Karatoyāmāhātmya</a> (25 vers), antérieur au 12⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle.<br> <a href="#entry-uttarapau_n_dra" class="skt-link">uttarapauṇḍra</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uttarapazcima">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>uttarapaścima</i> [<a href="#entry-pazcima" class="skt-link">paścima</a>] a. <a href="#entry-uttarapazcima" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-uttarapazcima" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-uttarapazcimaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>uttarapaścimā</i> au Nord-Ouest.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uttaraphalgunii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>uttaraphalgunī</i> [<a href="#entry-phalgunii" class="skt-link">phalgunī</a>] <a href="#entry-uttaraphalgunii" class="skt-link">f.</a> astr. np. d'Uttaraphalgunī «la deuxième Rougeâtre», 10⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ astérisme lunaire [<a href="#entry-nak_satra" class="skt-link">nakṣatra</a>], corr. à l'étoile β du Lion (Denebola); <a href="#entry-Uaryamaa" class="skt-link">Aryamā</a> y préside.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uttaraprazna">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>uttarapraśna</i> [<a href="#entry-prazna" class="skt-link">praśna</a>] <a href="#entry-uttaraprazna" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. figure de style poétique, où la réponse forme la question; opp. <a href="#entry-praznottara" class="skt-link">praśnottara</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uttarapro_s_thapadaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>uttaraproṣṭhapadā</i> [<a href="#entry-pro_s_thapadaa" class="skt-link">proṣṭhapadā</a>] <a href="#entry-uttarapro_s_thapadaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> pl. astr. np. d'Uttaraproṣṭhapadās «les Pieds arrière du lit», 25⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ astérisme lunaire [<a href="#entry-nak_satra" class="skt-link">nakṣatra</a>], corr. aux étoiles γ de Pégase et α d'Andromède; <a href="#entry-Uahirbudhnya" class="skt-link">Ahirbudhnya</a> y préside; son symbole est un double visage; plus tard appelé aussi <a href="#entry-Uuttarabhaadrapadaas" class="skt-link">Uttarabhādrapadās</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uttarapurastaat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>uttarapurastāt</i> [<a href="#entry-purastaat" class="skt-link">purastāt</a>] adv. au Nord-Est — prép. au nord-est de ⟨g.⟩.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uttararaamacarita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>uttararāmacarita</i> [<a href="#entry-raamacarita" class="skt-link">rāmacarita</a>] <a href="#entry-uttararaamacarita" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. np. de l'Uttararāmacarita, drame de <a href="#entry-Ubhavabhuuti" class="skt-link">Bhavabhūti</a>, qui conte l'histoire de <a href="#entry-Uraama" class="skt-link">Rāma</a> après son couronnement à son retour de <a href="#entry-Ulafkaa" class="skt-link">Laṅkā</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-j_rmbhaka" class="skt-link">jṛmbhaka</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uttaratantra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>uttaratantra</i> [<a href="#entry-tantra" class="skt-link">tantra</a>] <a href="#entry-uttaratantra" class="skt-link">n.</a> soc. section postérieure d'un rite de l'<a href="#entry-Uatharvaveda" class="skt-link">Atharvaveda</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> bd. np. de l'Uttaratantra; cf. <a href="#entry-Uratnagotravibhaaga" class="skt-link">Ratnagotravibhāga</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uttaratas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>uttaratas</i> [<a href="#entry--tas" class="skt-link">-tas</a>] adv. vers le Nord, au Nord — prép. au nord de ⟨g.⟩.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uttaravedi">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>uttaravedi</i> [<a href="#entry-vedi" class="skt-link">vedi</a>] <a href="#entry-uttaravedi" class="skt-link">f.</a> soc. autel nord du rituel véd. solennel, situé sur un tertre carré surélevé, et portant le feu sacré [<a href="#entry-naaciketa" class="skt-link">nāciketa</a>]; il peut prendre la forme d'un empilement de briques en forme de faucon aux ailes déployées [<a href="#entry-zyenacit" class="skt-link">śyenacit</a>]; cf. <a href="#entry-agnicayana" class="skt-link">agnicayana</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uttariiya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उत्तरीय</span> <i>uttarīya</i> [<a href="#entry-uttara_1" class="skt-link">uttara_1</a><a href="#entry--iiya" class="skt-link">-īya</a>] <a href="#entry-uttariiya" class="skt-link">n.</a> vêtement de dessus, veste, manteau; tissu porté autour de l'épaule, châle, voile.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uttarottara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>uttarottara</i> [red.] a. <a href="#entry-uttarottara" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-uttarottara" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-uttarottaraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>uttarottarā</i> très haut, de plus en plus haut — <a href="#entry-uttarottara" class="skt-link">n.</a> gram. réponse à une réponse <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> bavardage — acc. <i>uttarottaram</i> adv. de plus en plus.<br> <a href="#entry-uttaraa" class="skt-link">uttarā</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-utthaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उत्था</span> <i>utthā</i> [<a href="#entry-ut" class="skt-link">ut</a>-<a href="#entry-sthaa_1" class="skt-link">sthā_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(uttiṣṭhati)</i> pr. md. <i>(uttiṣṭhate)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>utthita</i><i>)</i> abs. <i>(</i><i>utthāya</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>prati</i>, <i>vi</i>, <i>sam</i><i>)</i> se dresser, se lever; se réveiller; provenir, dériver; naître, se produire; ressusciter — ca. <i>(utthāpayati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>utthāpita</i><i>)</i> lever, dresser, ériger; animer, produire.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-utthaana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उत्थान</span> <i>utthāna</i> [act. <a href="#entry-utthaa" class="skt-link">utthā</a>] <a href="#entry-utthaana" class="skt-link">n.</a> réveil, lever; lever du soleil ou de la lune; résurrection <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> activité, effort.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-utthaap">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उत्थाप्</span> <i>utthāp</i> [ca. <a href="#entry-utthaa" class="skt-link">utthā</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">10</span>] pr. <i>(utthāpayati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>utthāpita</i><i>)</i> pfp. <i>(</i><i>utthāpya_1</i><i>)</i> lever, dresser, ériger; animer, produire.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-utthaapya_1">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उत्थाप्य</span> <i>utthāpya_1</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] <a href="#entry-utthaap" class="skt-link">utthāp</a>] a. <a href="#entry-utthaapya_1" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-utthaapya_1" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-utthaapyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>utthāpyā</i> à lever, à dresser <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> à renvoyer <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> math. (résultat) à produire par substitution <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gram. [<a href="#entry-nyaaya" class="skt-link">nyāya</a>] se dit d'une dépendance [<a href="#entry-aakaafk_saa" class="skt-link">ākāṅkṣā</a>] uni-directionnelle (comme la dépendance d'un adjectif vis-à-vis d'un nom).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-utthaapya_2">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उत्थाप्य</span> <i>utthāpya_2</i> [abs. <a href="#entry-utthaap" class="skt-link">utthāp</a>] ind. ayant levé; ayant produit.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-utthaapyaakaafk_saa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>utthāpyākāṅkṣā</i> [<a href="#entry-aakaafk_saa" class="skt-link">ākāṅkṣā</a>] <a href="#entry-utthaapyaakaafk_saa" class="skt-link">f.</a> gram. dépendances sémantiques provenant de la structure du discours.<br> <a href="#entry-utthaapya_1" class="skt-link">utthāpya_1</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-utthaaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उत्थाय</span> <i>utthāya</i> [abs. <a href="#entry-utthaa" class="skt-link">utthā</a>] ind. s'étant levé.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-utthaayin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उत्थायिन्</span> <i>utthāyin</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-utthaa" class="skt-link">utthā</a>] a. <a href="#entry-utthaayin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-utthaayin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-utthaayinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>utthāyinī</i> qui se lève, qui apparaît; actif.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-utthita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उत्थित</span> <i>utthita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-utthaa" class="skt-link">utthā</a>] a. <a href="#entry-utthita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-utthita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-utthitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>utthitā</i> levé; relevé (de maladie) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> élevé, haut; éminent <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> produit, né, dérivé, arrivé; rentré (revenu) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gram. [<a href="#entry-nyaaya" class="skt-link">nyāya</a>] se dit d'une dépendance [<a href="#entry-aakaafk_saa" class="skt-link">ākāṅkṣā</a>] mutuelle.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uttiir_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उत्तीर्ण</span> <i>uttīrṇa</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-utt_rr" class="skt-link">uttṝ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-uttiir_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-uttiir_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-uttiir_naa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>uttīrṇā</i> traversé <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> qui a traversé, qui a surmonté; expérimenté.<br> <i>uttīrṇe</i> <i>ca</i> <i>pare</i> <i>pāre</i> <i>naukāyāḥ</i> <i>kiṃ</i> <i>prayojanam</i> [SRB.] À quoi sert le bateau après qu'il vous aie fait traverser?.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uttufga">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उत्तुङ्ग</span> <i>uttuṅga</i> [<a href="#entry-ut" class="skt-link">ut</a>-<a href="#entry-tufga" class="skt-link">tuṅga</a>] a. <a href="#entry-uttufga" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-uttufga" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-uttufgaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>uttuṅgā</i> haut, dressé.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uu_dha_1">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">ऊढ</span> <i>ūḍha_1</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-vah_1" class="skt-link">vah_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-uu_dha_1" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-uu_dha_1" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-uu_dhaa_1" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ūḍhā_1</i> porté, conduit, mené, transporté — <a href="#entry-uu_dhaa_1" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ūḍhā_1</i> épouse.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uu_dha_2">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">ऊढ</span> <i>ūḍha_2</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-uuh" class="skt-link">ūh</a>] a. <a href="#entry-uu_dha_2" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-uu_dha_2" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-uu_dhaa_2" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ūḍhā_2</i> conclu, inféré.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uurj_1">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">ऊर्ज्</span> <i>ūrj_1</i> [relié à <a href="#entry-v_rj" class="skt-link">vṛj</a>] ca. <i>(ūrjayati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>ūrjita</i><i>)</i> renforcer, revigorer, rafraîchir — ca. md. <i>(ūrjayate)</i> être fort, être puissant <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gr. ορψαο; lat. <i>urgeo</i>; fr. orgasme.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uurj_2">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">ऊर्ज्</span> <i>ūrj_2</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-uurj_1" class="skt-link">ūrj_1</a>] ifc. <a href="#entry-uurj_2" class="skt-link">f.</a> sève; force, vigueur; nourriture <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lat. <i>virga</i>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uva_ta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उवट</span> <i>uvaṭa</i> <a href="#entry-uva_ta" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. d'Uvaṭa, commentateur du <a href="#entry-Uveda" class="skt-link">Veda</a>, frère de <a href="#entry-Ukaiya_ta" class="skt-link">Kaiyaṭa</a>; il vivait à la cour du roi <a href="#entry-Ubhojadeva" class="skt-link">Bhojadeva</a> (11⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uz">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उश्</span> <i>uś</i> cf. <a href="#entry-vaz" class="skt-link">vaś</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uzanas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उशनस्</span> <i>uśanas</i> [<a href="#entry-uz" class="skt-link">uś</a>] <a href="#entry-uzanas" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. du sage [<a href="#entry-_r_si" class="skt-link">ṛṣi</a>] <a href="#entry-Ukaavya" class="skt-link">Kāvya</a> Uśanā «le Subjugateur», précepteur [<a href="#entry-aacaarya" class="skt-link">ācārya</a>] des démons [<a href="#entry-asura" class="skt-link">asura</a>], identifié au <a href="#entry-graha" class="skt-link">graha</a> <a href="#entry-Uzukra" class="skt-link">Śukra</a>, régent de Vénus; ennemi de son rival <a href="#entry-Ub_rhaspati" class="skt-link">Bṛhaspati</a>, il fut l'allié de <a href="#entry-Ucandra" class="skt-link">Candra</a> dans la guerre à propos d'Étoile [<a href="#entry-taarakaamayayuddha" class="skt-link">tārakāmayayuddha</a>].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uzat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उशत्</span> <i>uśat</i> [ppr. <a href="#entry-vaz" class="skt-link">vaś</a>] a. <a href="#entry-uzat" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-uzat" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-uzatii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>uśatī</i> désirable, charmant.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uzii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उशी</span> <i>uśī</i> [<a href="#entry-uz" class="skt-link">uś</a>] <a href="#entry-uzii" class="skt-link">f.</a> souhait.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uziinara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>uśīnara</i> [<a href="#entry-nara" class="skt-link">nara</a>] <a href="#entry-uziinara" class="skt-link">m.</a> géo. nom d'un peuple ancien et de leur pays au centre de l'Inde <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. d'Uśīnara, descendant de <a href="#entry-Uyayaati" class="skt-link">Yayāti</a> par <a href="#entry-Uanudruhyu" class="skt-link">Anudruhyu</a>, leur ancêtre fondateur; il épousa <a href="#entry-Umaadhavii" class="skt-link">Mādhavī</a>, qui lui donna son fils aîné <a href="#entry-Uzibi" class="skt-link">Śibi</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-Ugaalava" class="skt-link">Gālava</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pl. <i>uśīnarās</i> les Uśīnarās, habitants du pays <a href="#entry-uziinara" class="skt-link">uśīnara</a>; syn. <a href="#entry-bhojaas" class="skt-link">bhojās</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-uzii" class="skt-link">uśī</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-uzij">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-u_nch">
